(kuṟippu : maṇimalar 1941 - paṇṭitamaṇi, mutuperum pulavar tiruvāḷar mu.katirēcac ceṭṭiyār avarkaḷuṭiya aṟupatām āṇṭu niṟivu viḻāvil ceṭṭināṭṭaracar, tirumiku rājā, car.mu.aṇṇāmalic ceṭṭiyār avar talimiyil aḷitta kaṭṭurit tokuti)

8. kōcceṅkaṭ cōḻar
ulakam tōṉṟiya nāḷ toṭaṅki mēṉmiyuṟṟuvanta tol kuṭiyiṉar cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiyar eṉṉum muṭiyuṭit tamiḻvēntareṉpatu mūtaṟiñar tuṇipu. avaruḷḷē cōḻar kulameṉṉum pālāḻiyiṟ ṟōṉṟi aṟaṉum maṟaṉum āṟṟaluṅ kuṉṟātu taṉicceṅkōl naṭāttit tam pukaḻ niṟuviya maṉṉar aḷappilarāvar. ammaṉṉarkaḷil, civa naṉmakkaḷatu uḷḷatti muḻu maṇip pīṭamākak koṇṭu vīṟṟiruttaṟ kuriyōruḷ mutaṉmiyuṟṟiruppōr kōc ceṅkaṭ cōḻar eṉpār. ipperiyāratu caritam emmaṉōrellām kaṭittēṟutaṟku eyppiṉil vippākak kiṭittirukkum ñāṉa paṇṭāramākiya periyapurāṇameṉṉum tiruttoṇṭar purāṇat tirumuṟiyakattē miḷirkiṉṟatu. ataṉ curukkam piṉvarumāṟu:
puṟa voṉṟiṉ poruṭṭākat tuliyēṟiyum muṟi purinta cōḻa maṉṉarkkut toṉṟutoṭṭu urimivāyntatākiya cōṇāṭṭiṉ kaṇṇē aliyāl maṇi koḻikkum aḻakiya kāviriyāṟṟiṉ kariyil cantira tīrttattiṉ pakkattē palatarukkaḷum niṟinta kuḷir pūñcōlioṉṟuḷatu. appūṅkāviṉkaṇ veṇṇāval marattiṉ kīḻē tēva tēvarākiya civaperumāṉ civaliṅka meṉṉum tiru aruḷurut tāṅki eḻuntaruḷi yiruntaṉar. paḻam piṟappil mēlāya tavattiṉic ceytatoru veḷḷāṉi av varuḷuruvikkaṇṭu,  aṉpu mēliṭṭuk kiyāl nīriṉi mukantu vantu āṭṭiyum, pūṅkottukkaḷip paṟittuc cāttiyum nīlakaṇṭarākiya perumāṉi nittalum vaḻipāṭu ceytoḻukā niṉṟatu. ataṉāl tiru āṉikkā eṉṉum peyarum attalattiṟku uḷatāki nilavukiṉṟatu, iḵtivvāṟāka? ñāṉa muṭiyatoru cilantiyāṉatu namparatu cempoṟṟirumuṭimēl caruku mutaliyaṉa utirāvaṇṇam mēl vitāṉam eṉṉumāṟu vāy nūlāl valiyoṉṟu ceṟiyap purintatu, arumiyāka iḻitta accilampivaliyiṉik kaṇṭatu āṇṭavari vaḻipaṭac ceṉṟa avvāṉi. kaṇṭatum ītiṅkiruppatu anucitamāmeṉṟu ataṉic citittatu. cilantiyō muṉpōl mīṇṭum pantar iḻittatu. piṟṟināḷ ataṉiyum āṉiyaḻittatu. pārttatu cilanti. ciṉam mūṇṭatu. ‘emperumāṉ tirumēṉiyiṟ caruku viḻāmal yāṉ varunti yiḻitta nūliṉ pantari ivvāṉi aḻippatō!' eṉa vempik kotitteḻuntu yāṉiyiṉ tutikkiyuṭ pukuntu kaṭittatu. kaṭikkavē yāṉiyāṉatu ataṉip poṟuk kalāṟṟātu nilattilē kiyi mōti kulintu vīḻntatu. kiyiṟ pukunta cilantiyum uyir tuṟantatu. irumaṟip poruḷākum aiyaṉum am matayāṉikkum takumāṟu varamaḷittuc, cilantiyāṉatu cōḻar kulattutittu ulakupurantaḷikka aruḷ purintaṉar. akkālattē cōḻa kulattaracaṉām cupatēvaṉ eṉpavaṉ taṉperuntēviyākiya kamalavatiyuṭaṉ tillit tiruppatiyi aṭintu tirucciṟṟampalattē tiru niruttam puriyum cēvaṭiyi vaṇaṅkit tiruppaṭiyiṉ kīḻ vaḻipāṭu ceytu vantaṉaṉ. tēvi kamalavatiyum iṟivari vaḻipaṭṭu makappēṟu karuti varam vēṇṭiṉaḷ. perumāṉum tiruvuḷḷañ ceytaṉar. muṉ tiruppaṇi purinta cilanti aracu makiḻak kamalavati tiruvayiṟṟil aḻakiya .. vāy vanteytiyatu. kamalavati karuvuṟṟup pattut tiṅkaḷum nirampi makap peṟumpoḻutu aṭutta kāliyil kālattiṉ iyalpi aṟiyum mutiyā roruvar ‘iṉṉum oru nāḻiki kaḻittu piṟakkumēl ikkuḻanti mūṉṟulakamum purakka vallatākum' eṉṟu iyampiṉar. ataṉik kēṭṭavaḷavil aṉṉi kamalavatiyum ‘oru nāḻiki kaḻittu eṉ piḷḷi piṟakkumpaṭi eṉ kālkaḷi mēlē tūkkik kaṭṭuṅkaḷ' eṉṟu colli, aṅṅaṉamē aṟiñarurittu naṟpoḻutu vantuṟṟavaḷavil kāṟ piṇippu viṭappeṟṟu, ammaṇipōlum putalvaṉippeṟṟeṭuttu 'eṉ kōc ceṅkaṇṇaṉō eṉṟāṉ.' tēvi putalvaṉip peṟṟu uyir tuṟakkavē? ceṅkōl maṉṉaṉākiya cupatēvaṉ āviyaṉiya arum putalvaṉi vaḷarttu muṭi cūṭṭuvittut tāṉum tavaneṟi cārntu cilalōkameytiṉaṉ.
kōc ceṅkaṭ cōḻarum civaperumāṉatu tiruvaruḷāl piṟappiṉi yuṇarntu aracāṇṭu varukiṉṟār ākaliṉ civaṉār makiḻntuṟiyum tirukkōyil pala camittalākiya tiruttoṇṭiṉik kaṭaṉmiyāka mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. āṉikkāvilē tām muṉpu aruḷ peṟṟataṉi aṟintu ñāṉaccārvākum veṇṇāval marattiṉuṭaṉkūṭa nalañ ciṟantu viḷaṅkumpaṭi perumāṉ eḻuntaruḷiyirukkum tirukkōyil camittaṉar. maṟṟum, amiccarkaḷi yēvic cōṇāṭṭiṉ paṟpala pakutikaḷilum ammiyappar eḻuntaruḷum cemmiyāy tirukkōyilkaḷ palacamittu akkōyil tōṟum amutupaṭi mutalāṉavaṟṟukku aḷavillāta celvaṅkaḷi ēṟpaṭuttiṉar. tillittiruppatiyiaṭintu poṉṉampalattē tiruniruttam puriyum perumāṉip pēraṉpuṭaṉ aṭivaṇaṅki uruki uḷaṅ kaḷittu uṟiyum nāḷil vāymi kuṉṟātat tillivāḻ  maṟiyavarkkut tirumāḷiki pala camittaṉar. ivvāṟākap pūvulakip potuvaṟap purantaruḷiya kōcceṅkaṭ cōḻar civaperumāṉukkuriya tiruttoṇṭukaḷ palapurintu, tēvar kaḷum pōṟṟat tilliyampalavāṇariṉ tiruvaṭi nīḻali yeytiṉar.
iṉi, ivvēntar perumāṉip paṟṟiya cila ārāyccik kuṟippukkaḷ iṅkē eḻututaṟkuriyaṉa:-
ivar, cēramāṉkaṇikkāl irumpoṟi eṉṉum cēramaṉṉaṉuṭaṉ pōrpurintu vākicūṭi, avaṉatu kaḻumalam eṉṉum ūrik kippaṟṟiyatuṭaṉ, cēramāṉiyum piṭittuc ciṟippaṭuttiṉar. appoḻutu kaṇikkālirumpoṟikku uyirttōḻaṉāyirunta poykiyār eṉṉum pulavar ceṅkaṭ cōḻaratu veṟṟittiṟattic ciṟappittup patiṉeṇ kīḻkkaṇak kiṉuḷ oṉṟākiya kaḷavaḻi nāṟpatu eṉṉum nūliṉi iyaṟṟic cēramāṉic ciṟi mīṭṭaṉar. cila pirapantaṅkaḷilē iv varalāṟu koṇṭu kōcceṅkaṭ cōḻar cuṭṭappaṭukiṉṟār.
"	kaḷavaḻik kaviti poyki yuriceyya vutiyaṉ kālvaḻit taḷiyi veṭṭiyāciṭṭa vavaṉum"
eṉṟu kaliṅkattup paraṇiyum,
"	mētakka poyki kavikoṇṭu villavaṉip pātattaḷi viṭṭa pārttivaṉum"
eṉṟu vikkirama cōḻaṉulāvum,
"	aṇaṅku, paṭuttup poṟiyaṉip poykikkup paṇṭu koṭuttuk kaḷavaḻippāk koṇṭōṉ"
eṉṟu kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉulāvum, iṅṅaṉamē irāja rāja cōḻaṉulā mutaliyaṉavum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
puṟanāṉūṟṟiluḷḷa ‘kuḻaviyiṟappiṉum' eṉṉum pāṭṭiṉ kīḻk kuṟippu "cēramāṉ kaṇikkā  lirumpāṟi cōḻaṉ ceṅkaṇāṉōṭu pōrp puṟattup porutu paṟṟuk kōṭpaṭṭuk kuṭavāyiṟ kōṭṭattuc ciṟiyiṟ kiṭantu taṇṇīr tāveṉṟu peṟātu peyarttup peṟṟuk kikkoṇṭiruntu uṇṇāṉ collit tuñciyapāṭṭu" eṉṟuḷatu. tamiḻ nāvalar caritiyil ippāṭṭiṉ kīḻkkuṟippu "cēraṉ kaṇikkālirumpoṟi ceṅkaṇāṉāṟ kuṇavāyiṟ kōṭṭattut taḷippaṭṭapōtu poykiyāruk keḻuti viṭutta pāṭṭu" eṉṟirukkiṟatu. ivi oṉṟiṉoṉṟu ciṟitu muraṇpaṭiṉum, cēramāṉ kaṇik kālirumpoṟi kōc ceṅkaṇṇarāṟ ciṟippaṭṭa ceyti ellāvaṟṟiṉum kūṟappeṟṟuḷatu.
iṉi, tirumaṅkiyāḻvār periya tirumoḻi, tirunaṟiyūrp patikattil
"	kavvimā kaḷiṟunti veṇṇi yēṟṟa kaḻaṉ maṉṉar maṇimuṭimēṟ kāka mēṟat teyvavāḷ valaṅkoṇṭa cōḻaṉ cērnta tirunaṟiyūr maṇimāṭañ cērmiṉ kaḷē", 
eṉṟum,
"	pārāḷa ravarivareṉ ṟaḻunti yēṟṟa paṭimaṉṉaruṭal tuṇiyap parimāvuytta tērāḷaṉ kōccōḻaṉ cērnta kōyil tirunaṟiyūr maṇimāṭañ cērmiṉ kaḷē", 
eṉṟum
ivvaracarik kuṟippiṭṭiruttalāl, cōḻa nāṭṭiluḷḷa veṇṇi aḻuntūr eṉṉum ūrkaḷil nikaḻnta pōrilum ivar pakiyaracari muṟiyaṭittaṉa reṉpatu terikiṉṟatu. ivvāṟṟāl cēra, pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷ ivaruṭaṉ pōr purintu tōṟṟuk kīḻp paṭintārātal vēṇṭum. mēṟkuṟitta patikattil tirumaṅki maṉṉaṉ ivarit 'teṉṉāṭaṉ kuṭakoṅkaṉ cōḻaṉ cērnta tirunaṟiyūr maṇimāṭam' eṉpataṉāl mūṉṟu nāṭṭiṟkum uriyarākak kūṟuvatum, cuntara mūrttikaḷ, tiruttoṇṭat tokiyil ‘teṉṉavaṉā yulakāṇṭa ceṅkaṇārk kaṭiyēṉ eṉṟu kūṟuvatum iṅkē karutaṟpālaṉa.
tirumaṅki maṉṉaṉ ivarip pukaḻntu kūṟuvatiliruntu, ivar aracarātaṟkēṟpa vēṟu mataṅkaḷiyum purantu vantaṉareṉa aṟiyalākum eṉiṉum ivar ceyta toṇṭukaḷellām civaperumāṉukkēyākum. ivar civaperumāṉukku āṉikkā mutal ampar iṟutiyāka aṟupattu nāṉku māṭakkōyilkaḷ kaṭṭiṉareṉṟu cila periyōr kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. tirumaṅki yāḻvārē ivaratu kōyiṟ ṟiruppaṇiyik kuṟittu "irukkilaṅku tirumoḻivā yeṇṭō vīcaṟ keḻiṉmāṭam eḻupatu  ceytulaka māṇṭa tirukkulattu vaḷaccōḻaṉ" eṉṟu kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār. itaṉāl eḻupatu māṭakkōyil eṭuttaṉareṉa aṟiyalākum. māṭakkōyil eṉpatu peruṅkōyil eṉṟum vaḻaṅkappeṟum. peruṅkōyil ellām kōcceṅkaṇṇar kaṭṭiyavē eṉiṉ, 'perukkāṟu caṭikkaṇinta perumāṉ cērum peruṅkōyi leḻupatiṉōṭeṭṭum' eṉṟu tirunāvukkaracar aṭivu tiruttāṇṭakattiṟ kūṟiyiruttaliṉ, eḻupatteṭṭup peruṅ kōyil ceṅkaṭ cōḻar eṭuttaṉareṉṟu koḷḷa vēṇṭum. civa camayattiṟku iṅṅaṉamāka ivar ceytu vitta pērutavi aḷavu paṭum taṉmiyatō? innāṭṭiṉkaṇ ettaṉiyō aracāṅkaṅkaḷ tōṉṟi maṟintaṉa. ettaṉiyō paṭi yeṭuppukkaḷ nērntaṉa. ettaṉiyō piṟamataṅkaḷ ātikkañ celuttiṉa. nam meyccamayamākiya civamāṉatu ittaṉikkum īṭukoṭuttu nāmellām uyyumāṟu iṉṟukāṟum niṉṟu nilavuvatum, iṉi eṉṟum nilava iruppatum, nam muṉṉiyōr kōyilkaḷālum, pūcikaḷālum, viḻākkaḷālum ēṟpaṭutti vitta aṭippaṭiyiṉ valimiyālaṉṟō? iṅṅaṉamāka nam camayatti nilipeṟac ceyta perumikku maṟṟellōriṉum ciṟappāka urimi pūṇṭavar cōḻar perumāṉākiya kōc ceṅkaṇṇār eṉpatu tēṟṟam. ivar cilantiyāyiruntu iṟivaṉi vaḻipaṭṭu aracāyiṉa uṇmiyiyum, ivar pala tirukkōyilkaḷ eṭuppitta uṇmiyiyum tiruneṟittamiḻ vētam aruḷiya civacamaya kuravarkaḷ tampatikaṅkaḷil palaviṭattum pārāṭṭi yirukkiṉṟārkaḷeṉṟāl ivaratu perumiyi nām eṅṅaṉam aḷaviṭṭurikkalākum?
"	ceṅkaṭ peyarkoṇṭa ṭavaṉcem piyarkōṉ aṅkaṭ karuṇi peritā yavaṉē veṅkaṇ viṭiyā yemveṇā valuḷāy aṅkat tayarvā yiṉaḷā yiḻiyē."
- tiruñāṉacampantar.
"	puttiyiṉāṟ cilantiyuntaṉ vāyi ṉūlāṟ 	potuppanta ratuviḻittuc carukāṉ mēynta cittiyiṉā laracāṇṭu ciṟappuc ceyyac 	civakaṇattup pukappeytār tiṟalāṉ mikka  vittakattāl veḷḷāṉi viḷḷā vaṉpu 	viraviyavā kaṇṭataṟku vīṭu kāṭṭip pattarkaḷuk kiṉṉamutām paracūr mēya 	parañcuṭarik kaṇṭaṭiyē ṉuyntavāṟē"
- tirunāvukkaracar.
"	teruṇṭa vāyiṭi nūlkoṇṭu cilanti 	cittirap pantar cikkeṉa viyaṟṟac curuṇṭa ceñcaṭiyāratu taṉṉic 	n cāḻaṉākkiya toṭarccikaṇ ṭaṭiyēṉ puraṇṭu vīḻntuniṉ poṉmalarp pātam 	pōṟṟi pōṟṟiyeṉ ṟaṉpoṭu pulampi aruṇṭeṉmēlviṉik kañcivan taṭintēṉ 	āvaṭutuṟi yātiyem māṉē."
- nampiyārūrar.
9. kōpperuñ cōḻar, allatu  naṭpiṉ perumi.
paṇṭoru kālattil oru kōḻiyāṉatu yāṉiyoṉṟip pōril veṉṟa iṭamātaliṉ 'kōḻi' eṉap peyar peṟṟatum, toṉṟutoṭṭuc cōḻamaṉṉarkaḷ vīṟṟiruntu aracāḷap peṟṟa talinakaraṅkaḷuḷḷē mikka paḻami poruntiyatum, aṟaṅkaḷ nilipeṟṟa avikkaḷattit taṉṉakattē koṇṭiruntatum, 'ūreṉappaṭuva tuṟiyūr' eṉa āṉṟōr palarālum ciṟappittōtap peṟuvatum ākiya uṟantiyam patiyiṉkaṇṇē ēṟakkuṟiya āyiratteṇṇūṟu āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpu kaṭiccaṅka nāḷilē kōpperuñ cōḻar eṉṉum aracar oruvar iruntār. avar kalvik kaṭaliṉ elli kaṇṭavar; tīñcuvi keḻumiya centamiḻppākkaḷ pāṭuvatiṟ ciṟappu mikkavar; aṟattiṉ nuṭpaṅkaḷiyellām aṟintu tām avvāṟoḻukuvatōṭu, yāvarum aṟampurintu mēṉmiyuṟa vēṇṭumeṉṉum peruviruppiṉar; pulavar perumakkaḷiṭattut tām vittuḷḷa aṉpālum matippālum avarkaḷuṭiya aṉpiyellām koḷḷi koṇṭavar; avvāṟṟāl tōlā nallici mēlōrākiya picirāntiyār, pottiyār eṉṉum taṇṭamiḻp pulavarkaḷ tamakku āruyir naṇparkaḷāka amiyap peṟṟavar.
picirāntiyār eṉpavar pāṇṭi nāṭṭil picir eṉṉum ūrilirunta oru pulavar. avaratu peyariliruntu avari ātaṉ eṉpāṉukkut tantiyār eṉṟāvatu, oru ātaṉ ṟantiyiṉ vaḻiyil vantu appeyariṭap peṟṟavar eṉṟāvatu koḷḷutal takum. ātaṉṟanti āntiyeṉamaruvumāṟu tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. picirāntiyār tamatu vāḻnāḷiṉiṟutik kūṟṟil tamakku nariyillātataṉ kāraṇatti viṉāviya cāṉṟōrkkut tām viṭiyiṟuttalākap pāṭiyuḷḷa pāṭṭiliruntu avaratu kaḷaṅkamaṟṟa uyariya vāḻkki nili pulappaṭum. atu
"	yāṇṭu palavāka nariyila vākutal yāṅkā kiyareṉa viṉavuti rāyiṉ māṇṭaveṉ maṉiviyoṭu makkaḷu nirampiṉar yāṉkaṇ ṭaṉiyareṉ ṉiḷiyarum vēntaṉum allavi ceyyāṉ kākku mataṉṟali āṉṟavin taṭaṅkiya koḷkic cāṉṟōr palaryāṉ vāḻumūrē"
eṉpatu. tam maṉivi makkaḷ aṟivilum kuṇattilum māṭciyuṭi yarātalum, ēvalāḷar tām karutiyataṉik karutiyavāṟē ceytu muṭittalum, tam nāṭṭaracaṉ muṟivaḻuvātu kuṭikaḷip pātukāttalum, tammūriṉkaṇ cāṉṟōr palariruttalum tamakku āṇṭukaḷ pala ceṉṟum nariyillā tiruttaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉak kūṟiya avaratu uyariya maṉanili namatu pukaḻccikkaṇ eṅṅaṉam aṭaṅkuvatākum? ippeṟṟiyarāya pāṇṭi nāṭṭup picirāntiyārkkum cōḻanāṭṭuk kōpperuñ cōḻarkkum cepputaṟ kariyakaṭ poṉṟuḷatāki vaḷarntu varuvatāyiṟṟu. āṉāl avarkaḷ orupoḻutum oruvari yoruvar nēriṟ kaṇṭavarallar. oruvar oruvaruṭiya kuṇaṅkaḷik kēḷviyuṟṟa māktiri yāṉē avarkaḷ pāl aṭaṅkiyirunta uḻuvalaṉpu veḷippaṭṭu avviruvariyum otta vuṇarcciyuṭiya naṭpiṉarākki viṭṭatu. avarkaḷ oruvaroruvarippaṟṟip pēcāta nāḷum, niṉiyāta nāḻikiyum iruntiruttal cālātu. picirāntiyār oru nāḷ mālippoḻutil tamatāruyir naṇpaṉi niṉintu kātal kimikku aṉṉaccēval oṉṟi viḷittup pāṭukiṉṟa pāṭṭu annaṭpiṉiyum peṭpiṉiyum tiṭpamuṟak kāṭṭum. atu,
‘	aṉṉac cēval! aṉṉac cēval! āṭukoḷ veṉṟi yaṭupō raṇṇal nārutali yaḷikku moṇmukam pōlak kōrukūṭu matiya mukiḻnilā viḷaṅkum miyaṉ māliyām kiyaṟu piṉiyak kumariyam peruntuṟi yayiri mānti vaṭamalip peyarkuvi yāyiṉ iṭiyatu cōḻanaṉ ṉāṭṭup paṭiṉē kōḻi uyarnili māṭattuk kuṟumpaṟi yacii vāyilviṭātu kōyil pukkem peruṅkōk kiḷḷi kēṭka irumpici rānti yaṭiyuṟi yeṉiṉē māṇṭaniṉ iṉpuṟu pēṭi yaṇiyattaṉ aṉpuṟu naṉkalam nalkuvaṉ niṉakkē’
eṉpatu. aṉṉac cēvalē! aṉṉac cēvalē!! em aṇṇal taṉ nāṭṭiṉit taliyaḷi ceyyum mukampōla matiyam viḷaṅkum mālippoḻutiṉkaṇ yām ceyalaṟṟu varuntā niṟkiṉṟēṉ. nī tāṉ kumariyāṟṟiṉ peruntuṟiyil ayiriyi mēyntu vaṭaticik kaṇṇatākiya imayamalikkuc celkutiyāyiṉ, iraṇṭiṟkum iṭiyiluḷḷatākiya cōḻanāṭṭi aṭinta viṭattu, uṟiyūriṉkaṇ uyarntu tōṉṟum māṭattiṭattē niṉ peṭiyoṭu taṅki, vāyil kāvalarkku uṇarttiviṭātē taṭiyiṉṟik kōyiliṟ pukuntu, em peruṅkōvākiya kiḷḷi kēṭka 'yāṉ picirāntiyiṉ' aṭiyuṟi eṉṟu colviyāyiṉ niṉ iṉpuṟum pēṭi aṇiyat taṉ viruppamuḷḷa aṇikalatti niṉakkut taruvaṉ-eṉpatu itaṉ poḻippu.
ivvāṟākiya uttama naṭpiṉāl viḷiyum iṉpattip pārkkilum ivvulakattuc ciṟanta viṉpam yātuḷatu? taliyāyar tammuṭceyta naṭpuk karumpi nuṉiyiliruntu tiṉṟār pōlavum, pēraṟivāḷarkaḷiyaṟṟiya nūlkaḷiṉ nayam avaṟṟiṟ payilun tōṟum veḷippaṭṭiṉpam viḷittal pōlavum mēṉmēl vaḷarntiṉpam payappatāmeṉak kūṟuvar āṉṟōr. tuṟakka viṉpamum itaṟku nikarākāteṉil itaṉ perumiyi vēṟu kūṟutal eṉ? kōpperuñ cōḻarum picirāntiyārum kāṇṭaliṉṟiyum iṅṅaṉamāka īṇṭiya naṭpiṉ payaṉ tuyttu varuṅkālattil nikaḻvatoru ceyti neñciṉiyurukkun taṉmiyatu.	
ivvaracar peruntakiyākiya kōpperuñ cōḻarukku iraṇṭu makkaḷ uḷarāyiṉar. 'makaṉurikkum tanti nalatti eṉṟum, takkār takavilareṉpa tavaravar - eccattāṟ kāṇappaṭum eṉṟum āṉṟōr kūṟum potu nītikku vilakkāka orōvaḻit takavilārkku naṉmakkaḷum, takavuṭiyārukkut tīyamakkaḷum uṇṭākiṉṟaṉar. kōpperuñcōḻariṉ makkaḷum pāṟkaṭaliṟ ṟōṉṟiya ālakālam pōlum koṭiya paṇpiṉarāyiruntaṉar. aṉpiṟkē nilikkaḷamākiya tam arumittantiyuṭaṉ pōr ceyyavum eḻuntaṉareṉṟāl aṉṉavariṉ tīyapaṇpi eṉṉeṉ ṟeṭutturippatu? koṭiyavarkaḷāṉa makkaḷ pōr kuṟittu varutali kōpperuñcōḻar kaṇṭār. ciṉaṅkoṇṭār. tāmum pōr ceytaṟku akkoṭiyār mēṟ ceṉṟār. appoḻutu aṅkiruntu annikaḻcciyip pārttuk koṇṭirunta pullāṟṟūr eyiṟṟiyaṉār eṉṉum pulavarkku ōr nalleṇṇam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. aḵtu avarukku iyaṟkiyu mākum aruñcīrttip peruñcōḻarip pōrceyyātu taṭukka vēṇṭumeṉpatē avaratu eṇṇam peruṅkōvip pārttup piṉvarumāṟu kūṟuvārāyiṉar:
"amariṉkaṭ pakivari aṭutal ceyta veṉṟiyiyum, valiya muyaṟciyiyum, nilamuḻutum niḻalceyyum veṇkoṟṟak kuṭiyiṉiyum uṭiya vēntē, niṉṉuṭaṉ pōrceytaṟku vanta iruvariyum pārkkumiṭattu avarkaḷ niṉpaki vēntarkaḷ allar nīyum avarkkup pakivaṉ alli. tōṉṟalē nī ivvulakattup paranta pukaḻi yeytit tēvarulakatti yaṭinta poḻutu niṉṉāka oḻikkappaṭṭa aracāṭciyurimi avark kuriyatu; ataṉiyum niyē aṟivi. pukaḻivirumpuvōṉē, niṉṉuṭaṉ pōrceytak keḻunta cūḻcciyaṟṟa aṟiviṉiyuṭiya niṉputalvar tōṟpiṉ niṉpeyari celvatti avarukkaṉṟi yāvarkkuk koṭuppāy nī avarukkut tōṟpiṉ niṉṉiyikaḻum pakivar uvakkumpaṭiyāka ivvulakattē paḻiyiniṟuttuvāy. ātalāl, niṉatu maṟaṉ oḻivatāka; viṇṇōrkaḷ viruppuṭaṉ niṉṉi viruntāka etirkoḷḷumāṟu nalviṉi ceytal vēṇṭum. ataṟku virintu eḻuka; niṉ uḷḷam vāḻvatāka."
ivvāṟāka nallicip pulavarāl teruṭṭappeṟṟapiṉ peruṅkōviṉ uḷḷattiṟ poṅkiyeḻunta ciṉam "iṭunīṟṟāṟpiyavinta nākampōl" aṭaṅki viṭṭatu. eṉiṉum māṉam eṉpatu aracarkku uyiriṉuñ ciṟantatātaliṉ avar piṉ uyirvāḻa oruppaṭṭilar. ‘māṉamaḻintapiṉ vāḻāmi muṉṉiṉitē' 'mayirnīppiṉ vāḻak kavarimā vaṉṉār-uyirnippar māṉamvariṉ' eṉpaṉa āṉṟōriṉ mutumoḻikaḷallavā? tam makkaḷāl tamatu kulattiṟkum, tamakkum paḻiyuṇṭātaliyuṉṉi uyir tuṟakkat tuṇintār peruñcōḻar. taṟkoli ceytukoḷvatil paḻiyillātatoru vaḻiyi muṟkālattavar kikkoṇṭiruntaṉar. tatutāṉ vaṭakkiruttal eṉpatu. vaṭakkiruttal eṉṟāl yātāṉum iṉṟiyamiyāta kāraṇampaṟṟi uyirtuṟakkat tuṇintavar āṟṟiṭik kuṟipōlum tūyatoru taṉiyiṭatti yeyti, vaṭakku nōkkiyiruntu, uṇavu mutaliyaṉa tuṟantu, kaṭavuṭ cintiyuṭaṉ uyirviṭutalām. peruṅkōvum aṅṅaṉamē vaṭakkiruttali mēṟkoḷvārāyiṉar. vaṭakakarukkuṅkāl ulakattārkku aṟattiṉ mēṉmiyit tām tutntavāṟu aṟivuṟuttic colla vēṇṭumeṉṟu avarkku viruppamuḷa tāyiṟṟu. tām uyir tuṟakkumpoḻutum ulakattārukku utaviceyvatē uyarntōriṉ nōkkameṉpataṟku iḵtoru cāṉṟām. tām aṟivuṟattak karutiyataṉi nām eṉṟum pōṟṟuva toru centamiḻc ceyyuḷākap pāṭit tantaṉar nam puvikkaracākiya kavikkaracar. atu,
"	ceykuvaṅ kollō nalviṉi yeṉavē aiya maṟāar kacaṭīṇṭu kāṭci nīṅkā neñcattut tuṇivil lōrē yāṉi vēṭṭuvaṉ āṇiyum peṟumē kuṟumpūḻ vēṭṭuvaṉ vaṟuṅkiyum varumē  ataṉāl, uyarnta vēṭṭat tuyarntici ṉōrkkuc ceyviṉi maruṅki ṉeyta luṇṭeṉil toyyā vulakattu nukarcciyuṅ kūṭum toyyā vulakattatu nukarcci yilleṉiṉ māṟip piṟappi ṉiṉmiyuṅ kūṭum māṟip piṟavā rāyiṉum imayattuk kōṭuyarn taṉṉa tammici naṭṭut tītil yākkiyoṭu māytal tavattaliyē"
(puṟanāṉūṟu)
eṉpatu.
itaṉporuḷ: ‘aḻukku niṟinta teḷivillāta uḷḷattiṉi yuṭiyōr aṟattiṉic ceyvōmō ceyyātiruppōmō eṉṟu karuti aiyam nīṅkātavarākiṉṟaṉar; yāṉi vēṭṭikkuc celvōṉ yāṉiyiyum eḷitākap peṟuvaṉ; kāṭi vēṭṭikkuc celvōṉ atu peṟāmal veṟuṅkiyuṭaṉum tirumpuvaṉ; ataṉāl uyarnta viruppattiyuṭiya uyarn tōrkkut tām ceyta nalviṉippakutiyāl ataṉi nukartal uṇṭeṉiṉ; avar iruviṉiyum ceyyā umparulakattiṉkaṇ iṉpamaṉupavittalum kūṭum; avvulakattu nukarcciyilliyāyiṉ, māṟippiṟakkum piṟappu illiyākavum kūṭum. māṟippiṟatta leṉpatē illiyeṉṟu colvāruḷarāyiṉ immaliyiṉ cikaram ōṅkiṉāṟpōṉṟa tamatu pukaḻi nilipeṟutti vakiyillāta uṭampoṭu kūṭiniṉṟu iṟattal mikavum perumiyuṭiyatu; ataṉāl, evvāṟṟāṉum nalviṉi ceytal aḻakitu eṉpatu.
iṅṅaṉam aṟivuṟuttaruḷi, aṅkē kuḻumiyirunta cāṉṟōr kaḷip pārttu 'eṉ naṇpaṉ picirānti ippoḻutu varuvaṉ eṉṟu peruñcōḻar kūṟiṉar. cāṉṟōrkaḷō avar vārā eṉṟaṉar. atu kēṭṭatum peruṅkōvāṉavar' eṉ uyiri pātukākkum naṇpaṉ pāṇṭi nāṭṭuḷḷum cēymiyiluḷḷa tākiya picir eṉṉum ūriṭattāṉ eṉṟu colluvar; eṉiṉum nām celvam uṭiya kālattu avaṉ vārātiruppiṉum yām tuṉpuṟuṅkālattu vārātiruppāṉallaṉ' eṉṟaṉar. cāṉṟōr kaṭkō aiyantīrntapāṭilli, kōpperuñcōḻar piṉṉarum kūṟukiṉṟār. "niṟinta aṟiviṉiyuṭiyīr, ‘avaṉ niṉṉik kēḷviyuṟṟiruppatallatu ciṟitupoḻutum kaṇṭatilli; pala āṇṭukaḷ tavaṟātu paḻakippōnta urimiyuṭiyarāyiṉum nī kūṟiyavāṟu vaḻuvutaviṉṟiyoḻukutal aritēyākam' eṉṟu karuti aiyuṟātoḻivīrāka; avaṉ eṉṟum eṉṉip puṟakkaṇit tavaṉallaṉ, iṉiya kuṇaṅkaḷi yuṭiyavaṉ; piṇitta naṭpiṉi yuṭiyavaṉ; pukaḻiyaḻikkum poymmiyi virumpāṉ; taṉpeyarip piṟarkkuc collumpoḻutu eṉ peyar ‘kōpperuñcōḻaṉ' eṉṟu eṉ peyariyē taṉ peyarākac collum aṉpiṉ urimiyiyum uṭiyaṉ; ataṉmēlum ippaṭi yāṉ tuyaramuṟuṅkālattu āṇṭu nillāṉ, ippoḻutē varuvaṉ; avaṉukku iṭamoḻittu vippīrāka" eṉṟaṉar.
iṅṅaṉaṅkūṟik kōpperuñ cōḻar vaṭakkirukkavē, picirāntiyārum avar kūṟiyāṅku appoḻutu avaṇ vantu cērntaṉar. picirāntiyār pōṉṟē peruṅkōvukku āruyir naṇparum, uṟiyūriliruntavarum ākiya pottiyār eṉṉum pulavar ataṉik kaṇṭār. aḷaviṭamuṭiyāta viyappiṉiyum, aṭakkavoṇṇāta tuyarattiṉiyum uṭiyarāyiṉar. appoḻutu avar kūṟiyatu, 
"	niṉikkuṅ kāli maruṭki yuṭittē yeṉipperuñ ciṟappiṉō ṭīṅkitu tuṇital ataṉiṉu maruṭki yuṭittē piṟaṉāṭṭut  tōṟṟañcāṉṟa cāṉṟōṉ pōṟṟi ici marapāka naṭpukkantāka iṉiyatōr kāli yīṅku varutal varuva ṉeṉṟa vōṉatu perumiyum  atupaḻutiṉṟi vantava ṉaṟivum viyattoṟum viyattoṟum viyappiṟan taṉṟē ataṉāl, taṉkō liyaṅkāt tēyat tuṟiyuñ cāṉṟō ṉeñcuṟap peṟṟa toṉṟici aṉṉōṉi yiḻanta vivvulakam eṉṉāvatu kol aḷiyatu tāṉē."	(puṟanāṉūṟu)
eṉpatu.
itaṉ poruḷ: ivvaracaṉ ettuṇiyum periya ciṟappuk kaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiyum avaṟṟiyellām kiviṭṭu ivviṭattē ippaṭi varat tuṇital niṉikkumiṭattu viyappiṉiyuṭiyatu; vēṟṟu vēntaṉatu nāṭṭiṉkaṇ viḷakkamaminta cāṉṟōṉ pukaḻ mēmpāṭāka naṭpē paṟṟuk kōṭāka iṉṉatōr iṉṉāk kālattu vaḻuviṉṟi ivviṭattu varutal ataṉiṉum viyappi yuṭiyatu; ivvāṟu varuvaṉeṉṟu tuṇintu colliya vēntaṉatu perumiyum, avaṉ colliya coṟ paḻutiṉṟāka vantavaṉatu aṟivum viyakkun tōṟum viyakkuntōṟum viyappu elli kaṭantuḷatu; ātalāl, taṉ ceṅkōl cellāta tēyattuṟiyum cāṉṟōṉatu neñcit taṉṉiṭattē urittākap peṟṟa paḻiya pukaḻiyuṭiya attaṉmiyuḷḷa periyōṉi yiḻanta ittēyam eṉṉatuṉpa muṟuṅ kollō! itutāṉ iraṅkattakkatu eṉpatu.
iṅṅaṉam pottiyār viyantum kiyaṟṟum kūṟumāṟu ceṉṟa picirāntiyārum vaṭakkiruntaṉar. maṟṟum vaṭakkiruntār palarāvar. eṉṉē aṉpiṉ takimi! aṉpeṉpatu pala uṭampiluḷḷa uyirkaḷi oṉṟākap piṇikkun taṉmiyāṟṟāṉ ataṟku nār eṉṟum peyar uṇṭāyiṟṟātal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam peruṅkōvuṭaṉ oruṅkē vaṭakkiruntār pala rākavum, avarkku uyir naṇparākiya pottiyār māttiram uṭaṉ vaṭakkiruntārallar. ataṟkuk kāraṇam avar maṉivi karuppamuṟṟiruntami kāraṇamākak kōpperuñcōḻar avarit taṭuttu 'niṉakku makaṉ piṟantapiṉ varuka, eṉṟu kūṟiya moḻiyi maṟukkalāṟṟāmiyēyākam. eṉavē avar uṭaṉuyir tuṟavāmikkum naṇpaṉ colli maṟukkalāṟṟāta avaratu naṭpurimiyē kāraṇamāyiṟṟeṉpatu teḷivu. picirānti yārum, peruṅkōvum vaṭakkiruntami kaṇṭu muṟiyē kaṇṇakaṉār, karu'd2vūrp peruñcatukkattup pūtanātaṉār eṉṉum pulavarkaḷ iraṅkip pāṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ. kaṇṇakaṉār pāṭiyatu,
"	poṉṉun tukiru mattu maṉṉiya māmali payanta kāmaru maṇiyum iṭipaṭac cēyavāyiṉan toṭi puṇarntu aruvili naṉkala mamikkuṅ kāli oruvaḻit tōṉṟiyāṅ keṉṟuñ cāṉṟōr cāṉṟōr pāla rāpa cālār cālār pālarā kupavē"
eṉṉum aḻakiya pāṭṭākum. poṉṉum, pavaḷamum, muttum, maṇiyum, nilam, kaṭal, mali eṉpavaṟṟil oṉṟukkoṇṟu neṭiya cēymiyiliruppiṉum, ariya viliyiṉiyuṭiya nalla aṇikalaṉkaḷic ceyyum poḻutu avi ōriṭattut tōṉṟiṉāṟpōla eppoḻutum cāṉṟōr cāṉṟōr pakkattiṉarāvar eṉpatu  itaṉ poruḷām.
pottiyār eṉṉum pulavar tam naṇpaṉ colli maṟātu ceṉṟu uriyūrik kaṇṭapoḻutum piṉporukāl aracaṉatu naṭukallik kaṇṭapoḻutum iraṅkippāṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷ neñciṉi yurukkuntaṉmiyaṉa. 'ceṅkōliṉaṉ, tiṇṇiya aṉpiṉaṉ, makaḷirkku melliyaṉ, vīrarkku vīraṉ, uyarn tōrkkup pukaliṭam; avaṉi attaṉmiyaṉeṉṟu karutātu kūṟṟam uyir koṇṭupōyiṟṟu; ātalāl vāymiyiyuṭiya pulavarkaḷē, tam tuṉpuṟṟa cuṟṟattiyum cērttuk koṇṭu akkūṟṟatti vivōm vārīr' eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār. ivvāṟu tam āruyir naṇpaṉip pirintu orunāḷum poṟuttiruk kalāṟṟāta pottiyārum, tamakku makaṉ piṟanta piṉṉarum vāḷā amintirupparō? makaṉum piṟantāṉ. avaṉuñ ceṉṟār. ceṉṟu naṭu kallik kaṇṭu avar ‘niḻalik kāṭṭilum niṉṉip piriyātuṟiyum niṉ viruppamuḷḷa maṉivi vayiṟṟu makaṉ piṟantapiṉ vā' eṉaccolli eṉṉi iṅku niṉṟum pōkkiya aṉpillātavaṉē, niṉṉōṭu eṉṉiṭiyuḷḷa naṭpiṉi nī eṇṇā tiruppāyalli; pukaḻi virumpuvōṉē, eṉakku  kuṟittuḷḷa iṭam yātu? colvīrāka eṉṟaṉar. kōpperuñ cōḻarō kallākiyum avarkku iṭaṅkoṭuttaṉar. avarum ‘iṉṉuyir virumpuṅ kiḻamit toṉṉaṭpuṭiyōr tammuḻic celiṉ, avar naṭukal ākiyak kaṇṇum iṭaṅkoṭut taḷippar' eṉṟu ataṉi viyantu kūṟi, vaṭakkiruppārāyiṉar. innikaḻcci nām irukkum innakariyaṭuttu nikaḻntateṉpati niṉikkumpoḻutu uṇṭākum uṇarcci peritākiṉṟatu. ivvaralāṟṟiṉkaṇ viḷakkavēṇṭiya vēṟucila nūṇ poruḷkaḷ aṟṟamiṉmiyāṉ viḷakkātu viṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṟivuṭiyōr avaṟṟiyum ōrntuṇarvārāka.
10. kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ
kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ eṉṉum peyaruṭiya cōḻamaṉṉar iruvar iruntaṉar. oruvaṉ cōḻaṉ kuḷamuṟṟattut tuñciya kiḷḷi vaḷavaṉ. maṟṟoruvaṉ cōḻaṉ kurāppaḷḷit tuñciya kiḷḷi vaḷavaṉ. muṉṉavaṉ varalāṟē iṅku ārāyappaṭuvatu. ivaṉ cōḻaṉ karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉukku mintaṉeṉṟum, maṇimēkaliyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭa utayakumaraṉ ṟantiyākiya māvaṇkiḷḷi eṉpāṉ ivaṉēyeṉṟum, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉat tiliruntu ceṅkōlōcciya immaṉṉaṉ, maṇimēkalik kāppiyaṅ kūṟumāṟu appaṭṭiṉattik kaṭal koṇṭapiṉ uṟiyūrit talinakarākak koṇṭu aracāṇṭaṉaṉeṉṟum karutappaṭu kiṉṟatu. cōḻaṉ nalaṅkiḷḷi, vaḷattāṉ eṉṉum iruvarum ivaṉukkut tampiyarāvar.
ikkiḷḷivaḷavaṉ oppaṟṟa peruvīraṉākavum peru vaḷḷalākavum perum pulavaṉākavum viḷaṅkiṉāṉ. ālattūr kiḻār, veḷḷikkuṭi nākaṉār, māṟōkkattu nappacaliyār, ā'd2vūr mūlaṅkiḻār, kōvūrkiḻār, iṭikkāṭaṉār, āṭutuṟi mācāttaṉār, aiyūrmuṭavaṉār, kalliṟiyaṉār, erukkāṭṭūrt tāyaṅkaṇṇaṉār eṉṉum pulavarperu makkaḷāṟ pukaḻntu pāṭappeṟṟa peruñciṟappuṭiyaṉ ivaṉ. ivarkaḷ ivaṉip pāṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷellām eṭṭut tokiyuḷ oṉṟākiya puṟanāṉūṟṟiluḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiliruntu ivaṉuṭiya vīram, vaṇmi mutaliyaṉavēyaṉṟip, pulavarkaḷ pāl ivaṉ evvaḷavu aṉpum matippum uṭiyavaṉāy avarkaḷ kūṟum aṟivurikaḷi ēṟṟukkoḷḷum iyalpiṉaṉāyiruntāṉ eṉpatum viḷaṅkum. avaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟi iṅkē kāṭṭutum:
ālattūr kiḻār eṉṉum pulavar immaṉṉaṉ pāl paricil peṟṟuccellumpoḻutu, pulavarkaḷōṭaḷavaḷāviyirukkum iṉpattiyē virumpumiyalpuṭiya ivvaḷavaṉ 'emmi niṉittum varuvīrō?, eṉa ārāmiyuṭaṉ kūṟiṉāṉ. atu kēṭṭa pulavar tamatu naṉṟiyaṟivit terivippārāka,
"āṉ koli mutaliyaṉa purintatīviṉi yāḷarkkum appātakattiṉip pōkkum vaḻiyum uḷaveṉavum, nilam kīḻmēlāṅkālamāyiṉum oruvaṉ ceyta naṉṟiyic citit tōrkku uytiyilli yeṉavumaṟanūl kūṟā niṉṟatu; vēntē, kāppillāta uḷḷattuṭaṉē iṉiyamoḻikaḷ palavuṅkūṟi maṉṟattiṭattē ... uṇaviṉi aruntacceytu, aṅṅaṉam aruntiya pāṇarkku nīṅkāta celvam ellāvaṟṟiyum aḷittōṉum ematu vēntaṉumākiya vaḷavaṉ vāḻvāṉāka' eṉṟu niṉ perumi poruntiya tāḷip pāṭēṉāyiṉ pala katirkaḷiyuṭiya ātittaṉ tōṉṟutalaṟiyāṉ; yāṉō tañcam; peruma; ivvulakiṉkaṇ cāṉṟōr ceyta nalviṉi yuṇṭāyiṉ imayamaliyiṉkaṇ mēkaṅkaḷellāntiraṇṭu kārkālattuc corinta maḻittuḷiyiṉum pala kālam vāḻvāyāka" eṉṉum karuttaminta ceyyuḷip pāṭiṉar.
kōvūr kiḻār eṉṉum pulavar ivaṉuṭiya añcāmiyum vīramum pulappaṭa "kālaṉum tāṉ uyirkoḷḷalākum. kālam varuntuṇiyum pārttiruppaṉ; aṅṅaṉam kālam pārātu, vēṟpaṭiyiṉiyuṭiya pakiyaracarkaḷ keṭumpaṭi vēṇṭiya viṭattuk koṉṟu vellum pōrilvalla  vēntē? eṭṭut ticiyum erikoḷḷi erintu viḻavum, marattiṉ ularnta kompukaḷ tāmē paṟṟik koḷḷavum, ñāyiṟu palaviṭattuc ceṟintu tōṉṟavum, añcat takavaṉavākiya puṭkaḷ kuralicikkavum naṉavilē uṟpātaṅkaḷ kaṇṭum, pal nilattil viḻavum, ta'aclimayiril eṇṇey vārkkavum, paṉṟiyēṟṟi ēṟavum, āṭiyik kaḷiyavum, paṭikkalam tāṉ irunta kaṭṭiluṭaṉ kaviḻavum iṅṅaṉam kaṉavilē poṟuttaṟkariyavaṟṟik kaṇṭum añcātu pōr ceyyum valiyiyuṭiyōy" eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
iṭikkāṭar eṉṉuñ cāṉṟōr ivaṉuṭiya koṭiyum vīramum viḷaṅkittōṉṟa "maliyiṉiṉṟiḻintu kaṭali nōkki yōṭukiṉṟa pala yāṟukaḷip pōlap pulavar yāvarum niṉṉi nōkkiṉar; nī tāṉ avarkaṭkup paricil koṭuttaṟporuṭṭu maruntillāta kaṇicciyeṉṉum paṭiyic cuḻaṟṟik kūṟṟuvaṉ vekuṇṭāṟpōlum valiyuṭaṉē māṟṟu vēntariṉ nilattik koḷḷak karutiṉāy" eṉṟaṉar.
āvūr mūlaṅkiḻār eṉṉum pulavar,
"	nī, uṭaṉṟu nōkkum vāyeri tavaḻa nī, nayantu nōkkum vāypoṉ pūppac ceññā yiṟṟa nilavu vēṇṭiṉam veṇṭiṅkaḷuḷ veyilvēṇṭiṉam vēṇṭiyatu viḷikkum āṟṟali"
eṉṟu ivaṉip pāṭiṉar. maṟṟum palar palavāṟu ivaṉip pāṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
ivvēntaṉ iṅṅaṉam pulavar palarāṟ pukaḻntu pāṭap peṟṟatēyaṉṟi, orōvaḻit tāṉ iḻukki naṭanta viṭattu avarkaḷāl iṭitturikkap peṟṟu muḷḷāṉ. ulakilē kaṟṟañ ciṟitumillātavar ettuṇiyar? viruppu veṟuppuk kaḷōṭu vaṭi nilattiṉi āṇṭu varukiṉṟa oru maṉṉaṉ aṇuvaḷavuṅ kuṟṟamiṉṟi yiruppateṉpatu eḷitākumō? tirukkōvalūrilirunta ciṟṟaracaṉummūvēntarum pōriṉkaṇ tattamakkut tuṇiyāka vēṇṭi irantaḻikkumpaṭiyāṉa peru viramum variyāta vaḷḷaṉmiyum uṭiyavaṉum kapilar mutaliya pulavar perumakkaḷāl mikappukaḻntu pāṭappeṟṟa perumi vāyntavaṉum ākiya maliyamāṉ tiru muṭikkāriyiṉpāl immaṉṉaṉmikka pakimi pūṇṭiruntāṉ. ataṟkuk kāraṇam taṉakkup pakivaṉāy uṟiyūrilirunta cōḻaṉākiya neṭuṅkiḷḷi eṉpāṉukkut tirumuṭikkāriyāṉavaṉ pōriṉkaṇ tuṇi purintatē yātal vēṇṭum. inneṭuṅkiḷḷiyik kiḷḷi vaḷavaṉ taṉṟampiyākiya nalaṅkiḷḷiyāl kāriyāṟu eṉṉumiṭattiṟ tolvittāṉeṉpatu
"	ārpuṉi teriya viḷaṅkōṉ ṟaṉṉāl kāriyāṟṟuk koṇṭa kāval veṇkuṭi valikeḻu taṭakki māvaṇ kiḷḷi"
eṉa maturik kūlavāṇikaṉ cāttaṉārāl maṇimēkaliyiṟ kūṟappaṭutalālum, neṭuṅkiḷḷi kāriyāṟṟut tuñciya eṉṉum aṭiyuṭaṉ vaḻaṅkappaṭutalālum aṟiyak kiṭakkiṉṟatu. inneṭuṅ kiḷḷikkut tuṇipurintamiyāl taṉakkup pakivaṉāyiṉa kāriyiṉ makkaḷ oru kāl taṉṉiṭattakappaṭṭapoḻutu kiḷḷi vaḷavaṉ niṉitaṟkumariya koṭuñceyaloṉṟic ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉāṉ. acceyalāvatu maliyamāṉ makkaḷi yāṉik kālāl aṭaṟuvippatē. kōvūrkiḻār eṉṉum pulavarkku icceyti terintatu. aruḷ niṟinta neñca muṭiya pulavar nikaḻavirukkum ik koṭuñceyaliyaṟintum vāḷāviruppatō? kiḷḷivaḷavaṉi virintaṇuki avaṉip pārttu "aracē? nī tāṉ oru puṟāvuṟṟa tuṉpattiyum maṟṟum piṟavuyirkaḷumuṟṟa tuṉpaṅkaḷ pala vaṟṟiyum pōkkiya cempiyaṉ marapiṉuḷḷāy; ivartāmaṟivāl uḻutuṇṇum kaṟṟōruṭiya vaṟumikkut tāmañcit tammuṭiya poruḷip pakuttuṇṇum kuḷirnta niḻaliyuṭiyāriṉ marapiṉuḷḷār; aṉṟiyum ivarkaḷ tammik kollaveṉa niṟkiṉṟa yāṉiyik kaṇṭu tām iḷamiyāl muṉpu veruviya aḻukiyiyum maṟantiruk kiṉṟa ciṟu piḷḷikaḷ; immaṉṟatti maruṇṭu nōkki muṉpu aṟiyātatōr varuttattiyum uṭiyar; itu kēṭṭāyāyiṉ nī virumpiyatic ceyvāyāka" eṉṉum karuttuḷḷa pāṭṭoṉṟi vaṟiṉar. iṅṅaṉam pulavar perumāṉ maṉaṅkarin turukap pāṭiya pāṭṭik kēṭṭapiṉ kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ appiḷḷi kaḷik kollātu viṭuttiruppāṉeṉpatil aiyamilli.
ivvēntaṉ oru kālattil cēra maṉṉaṉ oruvaṉōṭu pōr kuṟittuc ceṉṟu karuvūri muṟṟuki yiṭṭāṉ. cēraṉō cōḻaṉuṭaṉ etirttupporum tuṇiviṉaṉāy matilvāyili aṭittukkoṇṭu kōyiliṉkaṇ iṉitiruppāṉāyiṉaṉ. muṟṟuki nīṭṭittatāl nakariluḷḷa makkaṭku ettuṇit tuṉpamuṇṭāmeṉpatu kūṟavēṇṭuvatiṉṟu. itaṉi aṟinta ālattūr kiḻār eṉṉum pulavar kiḷḷivaḷavaṉi aṇuki, "cilampiṉiyum ciṟiya toṭiyiṉiyumuṭiya makaḷir kaḻaṅku viḷiyāṭum aṇimiyiyuṭiya āṉporuntattiṉ (amarāvatiāṟṟiṉ) maṇal citaṟumpaṭi kāval maraṅkaḷi veṭṭum ōci taṉatu kōyiṟ kaṇṇē ceṉṟu olippavum, avviṭattu māṉamiṉṟi iṉitākayirunta vēntaṉuṭaṉ ivviṭattu niṉ muracamolippap porutāyeṉpatu kēṭṭār nāṇun taṉmiyiyuṭittu; avaṉik kolvāyāyiṉum, kollātu viṭuvāyāyiṉum avaṟṟāl niṉakka varum uyarcci yām colla vēṇṭā; nīyē eṇṇiyaṟivi" eṉṟiṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiccantu ceytu mīṭṭaṉar.
veḷḷikkuṭinākaṉār eṉṉum pulavar ceviyaṟivuṟūu vāka ivvaracaṉip pāṭiya pāṭṭu mikac ciṟantaporuḷ kaḷiyuṭiyatu. kaṭali elliyākavuṭiya ulakattiṉ kaṇ kuḷirnta tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟkuriya muracolikkum paṭiyiṉiyuṭiya mūvēntaruḷḷum araceṉṟu collutaṟkuc ciṟantatu niṉṉuṭiya aracē; ñāyiṟu nāṉku tikkiṉumtōṉṟiṉum, veḷḷi teṉṟicikkaṭ celliṉum, aḻakiya kāviri palakālkaḷāl ōṭi ūṭṭutalāl tokuti koṇṭa vēṟpaṭiyiṉ ṟōṟṟam pōl karumpiṉ veḷip pūkkaḷ aciyum nāṭeṉṟu collap paṭuvatu niṉṉuṭiya nāṭē; annāṭu poruntiya celvatti yuṭiya perumi poruntiya aracē, niṉakkariyavākiya cilakāriyañ colluvēṉ; yāṉ kūṟuñ cilavārttikaḷik kēṭpāyāka; aṟakkaṭavuḷ vīṟṟiruntu āṭci purintāloppac ceṅkōlāl ārāytaliyum, muṟiyiṉik kēṭkavēṇṭuṅ kālattil eḷiya cevviyiyumuṭiya aracar vēṇṭum poḻutil maḻiyiṉip peṟṟavarāvar, vāṉi muṭṭiya paranta veṇ koṟṟakkuṭi veyili maṟittaṟkuk koṇṭatō veṉiṉ, aṉṟu; varuntiya kuṭikaḷi niḻal ceytal kāraṇattāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭatu; vaḷava, varukiṉṟa māṟṟār paṭiyip poṟuttu niṉṟu, atu puṟaṅ koṭuttal kaṇṭu āravārittu niṉ paṭi tarukiṉṟa veṟṟiyum uḻukiṉṟa kalappi nilattilūṉṟiya cāliṭattu viḷinta nelliṉatu payaṉē; maḻi peyyātoḻiyiṉum, viḷivu kuṟiyiṉum, iyalpallātaṉa ceyaṟkiyil tōṉṟiṉumivvulakamāṉatu kāvalarip paḻitturikkum; ataṉi naṉku aṟintāyāyiṉ, nīyum kuṟaḷi kūṟuvāratu uṟutiyillā moḻiyi yēṟṟukkoḷḷātu ēriṉip pātukākkum uḻavar kuṭiyip pātukāttu, akkāvalāl ēṉikkuṭikaḷiyum pātukāppāyāyiṉ niṉ pakivar niṉ aṭiyip pōṟṟuvar eṉṉuṅ karuttukkaḷamintatu appāṭṭu. itaṉip pāṭiya nākaṉār tampāl iṟukkalāṟṟātirunta paḻiya nilavariyākiya kaṭali viṭuvittuk koṇṭaṉar. iṅkē kāṭṭiya iraṇṭu mūṉṟu cāṉṟukaḷiliruntu, ivvaracaṉ pulavarāyiṉār kūṟum aṟivurikaḷik kēṭṭu vaḻukkaḷintu kōl tirutti āṇṭuvantaṉa ṉeṉpatu viḷaṅkāniṟkum.
iṉi ivvēntaṉatu ceṅkōṉmiyip pulappaṭuttum cāṉṟu oṉṟu maṇimēkalik kāppiyattiluḷḷatu. ivaṉ taṉ mintaṉākiya utayakumaraṉ tavattiṟam pūṇṭirunta maṇimēkaliyit kātalittuk kāñcaṉaṉ eṉṉum viñciyaṉāl veṭṭuṇṭiruntaṉaṉ eṉak kēḷviyuṟṟapoḻutu, taṉ amiccaṉākiya cōḻintavēṉātiyi nōkki, "yāṉ ceyyun taṇṭaṉiyit tāṉ ceytataṉāl viñciyaṉ takavuṭiyāṉallaṉ; tavattiṉar nōṉpum maṭantiyar kaṟpum aracaṉ kāval illi yāyiṉ illiyām; taṉ makaṉ mītu tērkkālic celutti muṟi ceyta  vēntar perumāṉatu vaḻiyil oru tīviṉiyāḷaṉ piṟantāṉeṉpati maṟṟi aracarkaḷ kēṭku muṉṉam avaṉip puṟaṅkāṭaṭivittu akkaṇiki makaḷiyum ciṟippaṭuttuka" eṉṟaṉaṉ; eṉṉum poruḷuṭittāka
"	yāṉ ceyaṟpāla tiḷaṅkōṉ ṟaṉṉit tāṉ ceytataṉāṟ ṟakavilaṉ viñciyaṉ mātavar nōṉpu maṭavār kaṟpum  kāvalaṉ kāva liṉṟeṉi ṉiṉṟāl makaṉi muṟiceyta maṉṉavaṉ vaḻiyōr tuyarviṉi yāḷaṉ ṟōṉṟiṉa ṉeṉpatu vēntar tañcevi yuṟuvataṉ muṉṉam īṅkivaṉ ṟaṉṉiyu mīmat tēṟṟik kaṇiki makaḷiyuṅ kāval ceykeṉṟaṉaṉ aṇikiḷar neṭumuṭi yaracāḷ vēnteṉa"
eṉṟu taṇṭamiḻācāṉākiya cāttaṉārāl aruḷic ceyyap paṭṭuḷatu. itil, taṉ makaṉiṟantataṟkuc ciṟitum varuntutaliṉṟi, makaṉi muṟi ceyta maṉṉavaṉ vaḻiyil ōr tīviṉiyāḷaṉ tōṉṟiṉāṉeṉa ēṉiyaracar kēḷviyuṟutaṟku muṉṉē avaṉi camattilēṟṟuka veṉa aracaṉ paṇittāṉeṉak kaḻaṟiyiruppatu avvaracaṉ nītiyilum, taṉ kulap perumiyip pātukāttalilum evvaḷavu peru nōkkuṭiya ṉeṉpataṉip pulappaṭutti neñciṉiyurukac ceykiṉṟatu.
peruṅ koṭiyāḷiyākiya ciṟukuṭikiḻāṉ paṇṇaṉ eṉpavaṉ ivvaracaṉukkuc ciṟanta naṇpaṉāvaṉ. paṇṇaṉ mītu ivaṉ pāṭiya puṟappāṭṭu ivaṉatu ceyyuḷiyaṟṟum cennāp pulamiyiṉiyum, taṉ naṇpariṭattu ivaṉukkirunta keḻutakiyaṉpiṉiyum, viḻumiya matippiṉiyum naṉku pulappaṭuttā niṟkum. atu,
"	yāṉ vāḻu nāḷum paṇṇaṉ vāḻiya pāṇar kāṇkivaṉ kaṭumpiṉa tiṭumpi yāṇarp paḻumaram puḷḷimirn taṉṉa ūṇoli yaravan tāṉuṅ kēṭkum poyyā veḻili peyviṭa nōkki muṭṭi koṇṭu vaṟpulañ cēruñ ciṟunuṇ ṇeṟumpiṉ cilloḻuk kēyppac cōṟuṭik kiyar vīṟṟuvīṟ ṟiyaṅkum iruṅkiḷic ciṟāark kāṇṭuṅ kaṇṭu maṟṟu maṟṟum viṉavutun teṟṟeṉap pacippiṇi maruttuva ṉillam aṇittō cēyttō vaṟumi ṉemakkē"
eṉpatu. ippāṭṭilē, paṇṇaṉ illattil pāṇ caṟṟu ūṇ uṇṇutalāl eḻukiṉṟa aravattiṟkup paḻutta marattil paṟavikaḷ kūṭiyolikkum oliyiyum, avaṉ illattiṉiṉṟum cōṟṟiṉiyuṭiya kiyiṉarāyp pāṇpiḷḷikaḷ cellum oḻuṅkiṟku maḻi peyyuṅ kālatti nōkki muṭṭi koṇṭu mēṭṭu nilatti yaṭiyum ciṟṟeṟumpukaḷiṉ oḻuṅkiyum uvami kūṟiyiruppatum, paṇṇaṉip pacippiṇi maruttuvaṉ eṉṟatum, yāṉ vāḻunāḷiyumpeṟṟuttāṉ vāḻka eṉa avaṉi vāḻttiyatum mikka viyappiṉiyum uvappiṉiyum viḷippaṉavām.
iṅṅaṉam vīrattiṉum, nītiyiṉum, vaṇmiyiṉum mēm paṭṭup pulamittiṟaṉum vāyntu, pulavar kūṟum aṟivuṟū ukkaḷik kēṭṭu muṟipurintu vanta ivvaracar peruntaki cēraṉāṭṭil cērar maṉṉaṉākiya cēraṉuṭaṉ purinta pōrilē uyirtuṟantāṉāvaṉ eṉpatu ivaṉ peyaruṭaṉ iṇikkap paṭṭu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟa "kuḷamuṟṟattuttuñciya" eṉṉum aṭiyāl viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. immaṉṉaṉ iṟantamikkāṟṟātu pulavarkaḷ pāṭiya kiyaṟunilic ceyyuṭkaḷ mikka urukka muṭiyaṉa: "tiṇṇiya tēriṉiyuṭiya vaḷavaṉik koṇṭa kūṟṟamāṉatu taṉ maṉattuḷḷē kaṟuvu koṇṭatāyiṉum, veḷippaṭaniṉṟu vekuṇṭatāyiṉum, uṟṟu niṉṟu kiyōṭu mey tīṇṭi varuntiṟṟāyiṉum aḵtu uyyumāṟilli; ātalāl pāṭuvārip pōlak kiyāṟ ṟoḻutu vāḻtti irantu uyirkoṇṭatākal vēṇṭum" eṉṟu māṟōkattu nappacaliyār pāṭiṉar. 'aṉpillāta kūṟṟamē nī mikavum pētiyāyirukkiṉṟāy; cūḻccit tiṟa ṉillāmiyāl mēl viḷintu payaṉpaṭum vitiyik kuttiyuṇṭāy; nāṉ colliya vārtti meyyātali iṉṉamum kāṇpāy; paṭivīrariyum yāṉiyiyum kutiriyiyum etirniṉṟu koṉṟu nāṭōṟum niṉpaci tīrumpaṭi uṇpitta vaciyillāta āṟṟaliyuṭiya vaḷavaṉi nī koṇṭāyāyiṉ iṉi niṉpaciyit tīrppōr yār colluvāyāka" eṉṟu āṭutuṟi mācāttaṉār pāṭiṉar. "kalamvaṉiyum vēṭkōvē, kalamvaṉiyum vēṭkōvē, pulavar pukaḻnta poyyāta nalla pukaḻiyuṭiya cempiyar marapiṉuḷḷāṉākiya vaḷavaṉ tēvarulakatti yaṭintāṉākalāṉ, attaṉmi yōṉikkavikkum iṭamakaṉṟa tāḻiyi vaṉitali nī virumpiṉāyāyiṉ irunilam tikiriyāka mērumali maṇṇāka vaṉiya iyalumō niṉakku; iyalāmiyiṉ eṉṉa varutta muṟuvāy; nī iraṅkat takkāy;" eṉṟu cuṟṟattār iraṅkik kūṟuvatupōl aiyūr muṭavaṉār pāṭiṉar.
11. ōr tamiḻvēntiṉ iṟuti aṟavuri
ēṟakkuṟiya āyiratteṇṇūṟu āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ vaḷavar kulat tōṉṟaloruvaṉ uṟiyūriṉkaṇ iruntu āṭci naṭāttiṉaṉ. avaṉ, tāṉ pulami cāṉṟiruntatōṭu, nallicip pulavarkaḷiṉ arumi perumikaḷip pārāṭṭi avarkaḷō ṭaḷavaḷāvi iṉpan tuykkumpēṟum uṭiyaṉāyiṉāṉ. "nuṇṇuṇar viṉāroṭu kūṭi nukarvuṭimi viṇṇulakē yokkum viḻiviṟṟu" eṉpatu ivaṉatu vāḻkkiyi nōkkiyē eḻuntatu pōlum. pulavarkaḷi ivaṉ kaṇpōlum naṇpiṉarākak koṇṭoḻukiṉaṉ eṉil, avarkaḷ avaṉ ṟiṟattil eṅṅaṉa miruntaṉar eṉpatu kūṟutal vēṇṭumō? aḷavillāta pulavarkaḷ nāṭoṟum ivaṉ avikkaḷatti aṇi ceytu pōntaṉar. avarkaṭku uyireṉpatu ivaṉaṉṟi vēṟilliyāyiṟṟu. aṟivuc celvatti aritiṉiṟ pōṟṟum ivvēntar perumāṉuṭiya iṉiya kuṇam palavum yāvar ceviyiṉum ceṉṟu cērvaṉavāyiṉa. ivaṉ kōl cellāta vēṟṟu nāṭṭuṟiyum ōr pulavar perumāṉuṭiya uḷḷattiyum ivaṉ kuṇaṅkaḷ piṇittu viṭṭaṉa. avaruṭiya kuṇaṅkaḷum ivaṉi aṅṅaṉamē piṇittaṉa. "iruvarum māṟippukkitaya meytiṉār" eṉṟāṅku oruvaruḷḷattil oruvar uṟintaṉar. "mukattiṟ kaṇkoṇṭu pārkkiṉṟa mūṭarkāḷ! akattiṟ kaṇkoṇṭu pārppatē yāṉantam" eṉpatu oru periyār vēṟu karuttiṟ colliya tāyiṉum ivarkaḷ tiṟattilum poruntuvatākiṉṟatu. ivarkaḷ puṟattē oruvariyoruvar aṭuttup paḻakiyavarallar; oru muṟiyēṉum cantittavarumallar. āṉāl akattē kaṇṭu, uriyāṭi, makiḻntu pōntaṉar. ā! avarkaḷ naṭpiṉ tūymiyum perumiyum cepputaṟ keḷiyavō? vaḷavar perumāṉ vaṭakkirukkat tiruvuḷaṅ koṇṭaṉaṉ. vaṭakkiruttalāvatu aṟattiṟkum pukaḻukkum māṟākātu tam uyirnīttal kaṭaṉeṉa ārāynatu tuṇintōr tūyatōriṭattil vaṭakku nōkkiyiruntu uṭaliyoṭukki umpareytutalēyākum. vaṭakkirukkuṅkāl nam puvivēntaṉ niṉintaṉaṉ; vēṟṟu nāṭṭirunta kavivēntaṉum aṇintaṉaṉ. iruvarum puṟavuṭal nīttup pukaḻuṭal eytiṉar. ivvarum pēṟṟiṉi uṭaṉuṟintu iṉpantuytta ēṉip pulavarum eytā toḻivarō? pulavar palar teṟkiruntār vaṭakkiruntārāyiṉar. iṅṅaṉam taṉ pirivu poṟukkātu parivuṟṟuyir viṭum pulavar palariyuṭiya vēntaṉ ettuṇip perumiyuṭiyaṉātal vēṇṭum? kōpperuñ cōḻaṉ eṉṉum peyar kōkkaḷuḷḷē perumi mikka akkuricilukkup peritum ēṟpuṭiyatoṉṟām.
peruṅkō uyirviṭukku muṉ ulakiṟku viṭutta ceyti yoṉṟuḷatu. aḵtu attōṉṟaliṉ nalvāḻviyum cel katiyiyum aḷantu kāṭṭum aḷavukōl pōlvatoṉṟām. ataṉi aṟintu cintittup payaṉ kōṭal nammaṉōr kaṭaṉākum. apperuntakiyiṉ maruntaṉiya poruṇ moḻiyāvatu:-
makkaḷ yāvarum nalviṉi puriyuṅ kaṭappāṭuṭiyarāvar. aṟattiṉkaṇ viruppa millātār makkaḷiṉattiṟ cērntavarē yākār. avvirup piruntum apakkaṭinta aṟiviṉiyuṭiya maṉattiṭṭa millātavarkaḷ nalviṉi ceykavēmō ceyyēmō eṉa eppoḻutum aiyattiṉiṉṟu nīṅkātavarā yirukkiṉṟaṉar. avvaruviṉi ceyyum āṟṟal tamakkuḷatō ilatō eṉṉum aiyamē ataṟkuk kāraṇamākum. oruvaṉ mēṟkoṇṭa viṉi muṭivatum muṭiyāmiyum avviṉiyiṉ ciṟumi perumi yāṉaṉṟu. avaṉatu tiṇmiyāṉum tiṇmi yiṉmi yāṉumē yākum.
yāṉi vēṭṭikkuc celvōṉ yāṉiyit tappātu peṟṟu varutalum, kāṭi vēṭṭikkuc celvōṉ vaṟuṅkiyuṭaṉ tirumputalum uṇṭu. makkaḷ uḷḷuvavellām uṇarntaṉavē yāka vēṇṭum. uyarnta viruppattiyuṭiya uyarntōrkaḷ tām purinta nalviṉikkīṭāka viṉi ceytuḻala vēṇṭāta umparulakattiṉkaṇ iṉpam anupaviyā niṟpavar; aḵtiṉṟeṉil maṟu piṟappiṉ kaṇ virumpiya nalaṅkaḷip peṟuvar; allatu māṟip piṟavāmiyākiya pēṟṟiṉip peṟavar. māṟip piṟattaleṉpatē illi yeṉpāruḷarāyiṉ, imaya maliyiṉ cikaram ōṅkiṉāṟ pōlum tam pukaḻi nili niṟutti vaciyillāta uṭampuṭaṉ kūṭi niṉṟiṟattal mikavum talimi yuṭittu; ātaliṉ, evvāṟṟāṉum nalviṉi ceytal aḻakitu. ikkaruttu nam uḷḷattē eṉṟum niṉṟu nilavumāṟu ap perumāṉāl ōr aḻakiya centamiḻc ceyyuḷ vaṭivil vaḻaṅkap peṟṟuḷatu. atu:-
"	ceykavaṅ kollō nalviṉi yeṉavē 	aiya maṟāar kacaṭīṇṭu kāṭci nīṅkā neñcattut tuṇivil lōrē;  	yāṉi vēṭṭuvaṉ yāṉiyum peṟumē;  kuṟumpūḻ vēṭṭuvaṉ vaṟuṅkiyum varumē; 	ataṉāl, uyarnta vēṭṭat tuyarntici ṉōrkkuc ceyviṉi maruṅki ṉeyta luṇṭeṉil 	toy yāvulakattu nukarcci yuṅkūṭum;  tōy yāvulakattu nukarcciyil leṉiṉ 	māṟip piṟappiṉmiyuṅ kūṭum; māṟip piṟavā rāyiṉu mimayattuk 	kōṭuyarn taṉṉa tammici naṭṭut tītil yākkiyoṭu māytal tavattaliyē" eṉpatu.

12. aiyaravarkaḷiṉ poṟumi
tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ paḻami perumikaḷi aṟitaṟkuk karuvi yākum toṉṉūlkaḷ palavaṟṟiyum naṉṉar ārāyntu veḷiyiṭṭu ayaṉmoḻiyāḷar muṉṉē tamiḻ makkaḷ talinimirntirukkumāṟu purintavar makāmakōpāttiyāya, ṭākṭar vē.cāminātiyaravarkaḷāvar eṉpatu ulakam aṟinta toṉṟu. apperiyārkku eṇpatāvatu āṇṭu niṟitalik koṇṭāṭum nikaḻcci yāvariyum iṉpuṟuttal orutali.
tamiḻoṉṟē vallār ārāycciyiṉ peṟṟiyaṟiyā reṉṟum veḷiṟṟuriyāḷar nāṇit talikaviḻkkumāṟu avaruṭiya patippukaḷ viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. aiyaravarkaḷ patippiṉ viḻuppaṅkaḷiyum, avaṟṟāl avarkaḷ eytalākum nalliciyiṉ elliyiṉiyum urippāṉpukiṉ "virippiṉ akalum tokuppiṉ eñcum" eṉṟum caṅkattuc cāṉṟōr vāymoḻi niṉivukku varukiṉṟatu. avarkkuk kalvi kaṟpitta paṉṉaruñ ciṟappiṉ nallāciriyarākiya māka vittuvāṉ mīṉāṭcicuntaram piḷḷiyavarkaḷ varalāṟu pōṉṟē avaratu varalāṟum tamiḻppulavar varalāṟukaḷil taliciṟantatoṉṟāka nilavutaṟ kuriyatu. avar oṉṉārum viḻiyum ittakiya ciṟappiṉip peṟutaṟkuk kāraṇamāka iruntatu viṉimuyaṟciyāṉ varum iṉṉāmiyi yellām iṉpameṉak karutum avaratu maṉattiṭpamē.
paṉṉāṭ payiṟciyāl avarpāṟ kaṇṭaṟinta ariya kuṇa moṉṟi īṇṭuk kuṟippiṭutal poruntum. atutāṉ poṟumi eṉpatu. poṟik kayiṟṟiṟ pukaḻi'p piṇitta periyārkaḷil aiyaravarkaḷ mutaṟpattiyil niṟṟaṟkuriyavarāvar. kāraṇa muṉ variyum avarpāṟ kiṉamuṇṭāka yāṇṭuk kaṇṭatilli. 
"	uṇṇātu nōṟpār periyār piṟar collum  iṉṉāccol nōṟpāriṟ piṉ"
eṉṟum tamiḻmaṟi poṟumiyiṉ perumiyi iṉitu viḷakku kiṉṟatu.
nam tamiḻp periyār iṉṟum pallāṇṭu vāḻntu tamiḻaṉṉikkāvaṉa paṇikaḷ purintu iṉpuṟac centamiḻk kuravamākiya tilliyampalavāṇar cempoṟ cēvaṭikaḷi ēttutum.
kuṟippu : kalimakaḷ 14.03.35

13. rāv cāhip ve.pa. cuppiramaṇiya  mutaliyāravarkaḷ  
eṇpatām āṇṭu niṟivu viḻā niṉivu malar - 1940 (1.5.1937 - viḻā nāḷ) kūṭṭamottirunta vīrar aṇṇāmali nakar, na.mu.vēṅkaṭacāmi nāṭṭār.
tiruvāḷar rāv cākip cuppiramaṇiya mutaliyāravarkaḷ eṇpatām āṇṭu niṟivu viḻāp pattiram varappeṟṟu mikka makiḻcciyaṭikiṉṟēṉ. tiru mutaliyāravarkaḷuṭaṉ neruṅkip paḻakumpēṟu eṉakku vāyttiyatu. eṉiṉum muppatāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉtoṭṭu avarkaḷiṉ kalvi aṟivu āṟṟal mutaliyavikaḷi yaṟintirukkiṟēṉ. avarkaḷiyaṟṟiya cila ceyyuḷ nūṟpakuti kaḷiyum urippakutikaḷiyum paṭittum makiḻntum irukkiṟēṉ. ceyyuḷiyaṟṟal, uriyeḻututal, moḻipeyarttal, coṟ poḻivāṟṟal eṉṉum pulamik kūṟukaḷ palavum mutaliyāravarkaḷiṭam naṉkamintuḷḷaṉa. avarkaḷ uriyip paṭikkuṅkāl avarkaḷuṭiya nuṇṇaṟivum naṭuniliyum maṉaamitiyum aṉupava niṟivum pulappaṭṭu iṉpam viḷip paṉavākavuḷḷaṉa. tamiḻiṉiṭattu āḻnta paṟṟum, ataṟkēṟṟa pulamiyum, colvaḷamum uṭiyavarkaḷāy iruppatuṭaṉ mutaliyāravarkaḷ eṉṟum ūkkamum uṟcākamum niṟintavar kaḷāyiruppatu avarkaḷ vāḻkki muṟpōkki virumpun tamiḻ makkaḷukku eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka nilavumpaṭi ceyvaṉavām.
apperiyāruṭiya eṇpatām āṇṭu niṟivik koṇṭāṭi makiḻvatum, avarkaḷip pārāṭṭi vāḻttuvatum tamiḻ makkaṭkuc ciṟanta kaṭappāṭēyākum. akkoṇṭāṭṭap pēravi makāmakōpātyāya tākṣiṉāttiya kalāniti ṭākṭar vē. cāminātayyaravarkaḷ talimiyil naṭappatē cālavum poruttam. aiyaravarkaḷ tamiḻmoḻiyip pallāṟṟāṉum vaḷamuṟac ceytu ataṟku ōr tantipōl viḷaṅkuvatuṭaṉ āṇṭu aṉupavaṅkaḷilum mēṉmiyuṟṟut tamiḻ makkaḷ ellōruṭiya uḷḷattilum vīṟṟiruppavarkaḷ. tamiḻ muṉivarkkum tamiḻ aṟivuṟutta centamiḻk kuravarākiya cevvēḷiṉ tiruppēri iru periyōrum peṟṟiruttal uvaki viḷivippatākum. avviru periyāriṉ kūṭṭattik kaṇṭukaḷikkum tamiḻp pulavarkaḷukku "kūṭṭa mottirunta vīrar" eṉṉum kampar pāṭṭu niṉiviṟku varāmaṟ pōkātu.

14. ṭākṭar u.vē. cāmināta aiyaravarkaḷ
(catāpicēka varalāṟu - 1936)
tamiḻ nāṭṭiluḷḷa caṅkaṅkaḷālum, vēṟu pulavar palarālum ceyyalākāta ariya paṇikaḷi aiyaravarkaḷ caliyāta ūkkamum uḻippuṅ koṇṭu taṉiyē amitiyākac ceytu muṭittirukkum tiṟattiṉi yāvartām viyantu pōṟṟātiruttal kūṭum. īṭeṭuppillāta miḻmoḻiyiṉ pīṭu veḷippaṭutaṟkuk kāraṇamāyuḷḷa periyāriṉ (81-āvatu) piṟapput tirunāḷit tamiḻakattiluḷḷa paltiṟatta perumakkaḷum oruṅku kūṭik koṇṭāṭip pōṟṟum ivviṉiya ceykiyāṉatu tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ etirkāla mēṉmikkuc cupapūcakamākiṉṟatu.
tamiḻaṉṉiyiṉ tavapperum putalvarum taṅkaḷ tavat tantiyārumākiya pulavar perumāṉ pirammasrī aiyaravarkaḷiṉ catāpicēka mukūrttamāṉatu tamiḻmakkaḷaṉivarkkum avaravar illattil nikaḻum maṅkalaviḻāp pōṉṟu makiḻcci viḷippa toṉṟākum. aiyaravarkaḷ tām aṭintuḷḷa perumikkum pukaḻukkum kāraṇamāka vāṇāḷ muḻutum caliyāta uḻippum ūkkamum koṇṭu ceytu pōntirukkiṟa tamiḻp paṇi kaḷiyum, avaṟṟāl tamiḻakam aṭintuḷḷa elliyillāta naṉmikaḷiyum tamiḻmakkaḷ ovvoruvarum innāḷil niṉivu kūrntu pōṟṟuṅ kaṭappāṭuṭiyarāvar.

15. aṉpum kātalum
naṉmakkaḷāl eytaṟpālavāya uṟutipporuḷ nāṉkaṉuḷ otta aṉpuṭiya talivaṉum taliviyum kūṭi nukarum iṉpam oṉṟākum. ivviṉpiṟkuc ciṟanta kāraṇamākiya aṉpu kātal eṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭum. kātalāṉatu makkaṭku uḷḷattiṉ curukkam mella nekiḻntu malarvippatu; eppoḻutun tēṉpōl iṉippatu; evvaki mācukaḷiyum tuṭippatu; ceyviṉit tiṟaṅkaḷil ūkkatti yeḻuppuvatu; immiyiṉpamēyaṉṟi maṟumi yiṉpamum payappatu; maṟamali yulakamākiya vaṉkoṭum pāliyil uḻaṉṟu tiriyum māntarkaḷ parukiyum tiḷittum vemputaloḻiyum iṉpa'd2vūṟṟāvatu ikkātalē yāmeṉka. ‘ākki aḷittu aḻikkum kantaḻiyiṉ pēruruvē eṉṟum āṉṟōr kūṟupa. paṇṭit taṇṭamiḻ vāṇar palarum ik kātaliṉpamākiya akapporuḷ pāritturittup pōntatum, pēriṉpak kaṭaliṟ ṟiḷitta māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ mutalāyiṉār akapporuṭ kōvi mutaliyaṉa pāṭiyaruḷiṉatum, teyvap pulamit tiruvaḷḷuvaṉār tamatu tamiḻ maṟiyil irunūṟṟimpatu kuṟaḷ veṇpākkaḷāl itaṉi viritturittamittatum mutalāyiṉa nuṉittuṇarvārkku itaṉ ciṟappu naṉku pulaṉākum.
kuṟippu : centamiḻc celvi 1935 cilampu-13, paral- 9  pava- paṅkuṉi

16. vākit tiṇi
makkaḷ aṭiya vēṇṭum uṟutip poruḷkaḷi akap poruḷ eṉavum, puṟapporuḷ eṉavum pākupaṭutturippatu tamiḻ nūṉmuṟi. ivi aṟam, poruḷ, iṉpam, vīṭu eṉa viritturikkavumpaṭum. akam eṉpatil iṉpamum, puṟam eṉpatil aṟam poruḷkaḷum aṭaṅkum. vīṭu eṉpatu akam, puṟam iraṇṭiṉum aṭakkik kūṟappaṭum. akapporuḷ, puṟap poruḷ paṟṟiya oḻukalāṟu muṟiyē akattiṇi, puṟattiṇi eṉappaṭum. kuṟiñci, pāli, mulli, marutam, neytal, kikkiḷi, peruntiṇi eṉa akattiṇi ēḻu vakippaṭum. veṭci, vañci, uḻiñi, tumpi, vāki, kāñci, pāṭāṇ eṉap puṟattiṇi ēḻu vakippaṭum. kuṟiñci, pāli mutaliya akattiṇi illaṟattiṟkuriya talivaṉ taliviyariṉ kūṭṭam, pirivu mutaliyavaṟṟiyum, veṭci, vañci mutaliyapuṟat tiṇikaḷ aṟam poruḷ karutiya aracar mutalāyiṉāriṉ nirikōḷ, pakimēṟ celavu mutaliyavaṟṟiyum kuṟikkum.
makkaḷ puṟattilē poruḷīṭṭutalum, aṟam puritalumākiya ceykiyellām akattil iṉpan tuykkuñ ceyalōṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpum oṟṟumiyum uṭiyatuvē. ataṉāṟṟāṉ, āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār, 
"veṭci tāṉē kuṟiñciyatu puṟaṉē"
eṉṟivvāṟākap puṟattiṇi ēḻiṉiyum ovvōrakat tiṇi yōṭum cārttiyurippārāyiṉar. āyiṉ, avaṟṟiṟkuḷḷa iyipukaḷ ippoḻutuḷḷa urikaḷiṉāl muṟṟum teḷivāka aṟiyakkūṭavilli.
puṟattiṇikaḷil muṉṉuḷḷa veṭci, vañci, uḻiñi, tumpi eṉṟa nāṉkum pōrc ceytikaḷākaliṉ maṟattiṟaṉ uṭiyaṉa. aṭuttuḷḷa vāki maṟam, aṟam eṉṉum irutiṟaṉu muṭiyatu.
"	tāvil koḷkit tattaṅ kūṟṟip pākupaṭa mikutip paṭuttaleṉpa"
eṉpatu vākit tiṇiyiṉ ilakkaṇam. itaṉ poruḷ kēṭillāta kōṭpāṭṭiṉiyuṭiya tattamakkuḷḷa iyalpi vēṟupaṭa mikutippaṭuttal eṉpatām. eṉavē, kulam, nili, toḻil mutaliyavaṟṟāl vēṟupāṭuṭiya makkaḷ yāvarum tattamakkuriya pakutikaḷit tam aṟivu āṇmi mutaliyavaṟṟāl mēmpaṭuttutal vākiyām eṉka. iyalpallātavaṟṟi mēṟkoṇṭu avaṟṟiṉ mēmpaṭutal vākiyākāteṉpatu "tāvil koḷki" eṉpataṉāṟ peṟappaṭum. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, itaṟku, 'valiyum varuttamumiṉṟi iyalpākiya oḻukkattāl' eṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi, 'tāvil koḷki yeṉavē iraṇiyaṉip pōla valiyāṉum varuttattāṉum kūṟuvittuk kōṭal vāki yaṉṟāyiṟṟu" eṉṟurippar.
itaṉip pālittiṇiyiṉ puṟam eṉpar. ōtal, kāval poruḷīṭṭal mutaliyaṉa kuṟittut talivaṉ taliviyip pirintu cellutalākiya pāli, aṟam, poruḷ, iṉpaṅkaṭku ētuvāyiṉāṟpōla vākiyum ātalāṉum, piṟavaṟṟāṉum avviraṇṭum oṟṟumiyuṭiyaṉavām. iṉi, 'tattam kūṟu' eṉpati virittu vākittiṇip pākupaṭutturikkum tolkāppiyac cūttiram,
"	aṟuvakip paṭṭa pārppaṉap pakkamum aivaki marapiṉ aracar pakkamum irumūṉṟu marapiṉ ēṉōr pakkamum maṟuvil ceyti mūvakik kālamum neṟiyiṉ āṟṟiya aṟivaṉ ṟēyamum nāliru vaḻakkiṟ ṟāpatap pakkamum pālaṟi marapiṟ porunar kaṇṇum  aṉinili vakiyō ṭāṅkeḻu vakiyil  tokinili peṟṟa teṉmaṉār pulavar
eṉpatu.
āciriyar, vakiyaṟuttiyākkap peṟṟa oḻukkamuṭiya pārppaṉar mutaliya nāṟkuṭiyiṉari mutaṟkaṇ niṟuvi, ōr kuṭikkuriyarallāta aṟivar, tāpatar eṉpōriyum, col, pāṭṭu, kūttu mutaliyavaṟṟāṟ porutu veṉṟi koḷḷum porunariyum piṉṉarkkūṟi, aṉṉār oḻukkam nilikkaḷamāka vāki piṟakkum eṉpatu tōṉṟa vittuḷḷami kāṇka.
"kūtir vēṉil" eṉṉum aṭutta cūttirattil vākit tiṇiyiṉ tuṟikaḷ maṟattiṉ pakutiyāka oṉpatum, aṟattiṉ pakutiyāka oṉpatum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ittuṟikaḷ muṟkūṟap paṭṭa pārppaṉar mutaliyōr, kūṟappaṭāta tiṇi makkaḷ, pāṇar mutaliya ēṉiyap paḻaṅkuṭimakkaḷ ākiya yāvarkkum potuvurimi yuṭiyaṉavē. veṉṟi karutip pācaṟikkaṭ ceṉṟiruttalum, veṉṟukaḷam vēṭṭalum, veṟṟik kaḷippāl tērmuṉ kuraviyāṭutalum  mutalāyiṉa maṟattiṉ pāṟpaṭuvaṉa. pōr veṉṟiyi vāki eṉṟalum, ataṟku vāki māli cūṭutaluṇṭeṉṟalum piṟṟi nāḷilakkiyaṅkaḷiṟ parakkak kāṇappaṭum. veṉṟa vīrarēyaṉṟi avaratu paṭikkalamum vākimilitali kūṟappaṭum.
"	nīṭiya arakkar cēṉi nīṟupaṭṭaḻiya vāki  cūṭiya ciliyirāmaṉ"
eṉṟār kampar.
"veṟṟi vākiyē cumakkum vēli vaṇaṅkuvatemakku vēli" eṉa murukak kaṭavuḷiṉ vēṟpaṭikku vaṇakkaṅ kūṟiṉār ōr kaviñar.
iṉi, aṟattiṉ pakutiyākak kūṟiya tuṟikaḷ ovvoṉṟum vāḻkkiyiṉ kuṟikkōḷākum. viḻuppamuṭiyaṉavākaliṉ, ovvoruvarum karuttil iruttaṟ pālaṉa. mēlic cūttirat tōtiya 'pārppaṉap pakkam' mutaliyavaṟṟi mutaṟkaṇ ārāyntu, piṉ ivaṟṟi viḷakki muṭippām.
pārppaṉap pakkamāvatu pārppaṉariyal aracar pakkamāvatu aracariyal. ēṉōr pakkamāvatu vaṇikariyalum, vēḷāḷariyalum aṟuvaki, aivaki. irumūṉṟu eṉpaṉa avarkaṭku vitittuḷḷa toḻilkaḷ. āciriyar vaḻakkum ceyyuḷum ārāyntu nūliyaṟṟiṉārākalāṉum, cilvaki yeḻuttiṉ ceyyuṭṭākiya cūttira yāppil iyaṟṟappaṭum nūliṉ (ilakkaṇattiṉ)kaṇ yāvaṟṟiyum añcātu kiḷanturittal poruntāmiyāṉum, aṟuvaki mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷi ilakkiyam nōkki aṟintu koḷḷumāṟu viṭuttār. pārppaṉar anatṇareṉavum paṭuvar. avarkkuriya toḻilkaḷ: ōtal, ōtuvittal, vēṭṭal, vēṭpittal, ītal, ēṟṟal eṉṉum āṟumām; 
"	ōtal vēṭṭal avipiṟarc ceytal ītal ēṟṟaleṉ ṟāṟupurin toḻukum aṟam puri yantaṇar"		
(patiṟṟup pattu-24) 
eṉa ilakkiyattuḷ avipeṟappaṭutal kāṇka. avaṉ avaṟṟuḷ ōrōvoṉṟic ceytamiyum ceyyuṭkaḷā laṟiyappaṭum. aṅṅaṉamē aracar, vaṇikar,vēḷāḷar eṉpārkkuriyaṉavum aṟiyappaṭum. aracarkkuriya toḻilkaḷ ōtal, vēṭṭal, ītal, kāttal, taṇṭañ ceytal eṉṉum aintumām. vaṇikarkkuriyaṉa ōtal, vēṭṭal, ītal, uḻavu, pacuk kāttal, vaṇikam eṉṉum āṟumām;
"	uḻutu payaṉkoṇ ṭoliniri yōmpip paḻutilāp paṇṭam pakarntu - muḻutuṇara ōti yaḻalvaḻipaṭ ṭōmpāta vīkiyāṉ  āti vaṇikark karacu"			(pu. ve. māli)
eṉpatu kāṇka. vēḷāḷarkkuriyaṉa uḻavu, ēṉittoḻil, viruntōmpal, pakaṭu puṟantaral, vēṭṭal, ōtal eṉṉum āṟumām eṉpar iḷampūraṇar. viruntōmpal eṉpatu īkiyil aṭaṅkum. ivvāṟṟāl otuvittal, vēṭpittal, ēṟṟal eṉṉum mūṉṟum pārppaṉarkkum, kāttal, taṇṭañ ceytal eṉṉum iraṇṭum aracarkkum, niriyōmpal, vāṇikam eṉṉum iraṇṭum vaṇikarkkum, pakaṭu puṟantaral, uḻavu, uḻavoḻinta toḻil eṉṉum mūṉṟum vēḷāḷarkkum ciṟappurimi yuṭiyaṉa eṉpatum, ōtal, vēṭṭal, ītal eṉṉum mūṉṟum nāṟpālālārkkum potuvāvaṉa eṉpatum peṟṟām. uḻavu vaṇikarkku muḷatēṉum vēḷāḷarkku, uḻavum, vaṇikarkku vaṇikamum ciṟantaṉa veṉpatu marapilāṉaṟiyappaṭum. ivi yāvum marapiyaliṟ kūṟaṟpālavēṉum, ivi paṟṟi vāki nikaḻtaliṉ īṇṭuk kūṟi, ivaṟṟuḷaṭaṅkāta cilavaṟṟi marapiyaliṟ kūṟiṉā reṉka. īṇṭu "irumūṉṟu marapiṉ ēṉōr" eṉa vaṇikariyum vēḷāḷariyum oruṅkiyittuk kūṟiyatu avviruvarkkum vēṟṟumi ciṟitākaliṉ eṉka. eṉavē āriya varuṇaṅkaḷuḷ nāṉkām varuṇattiṟkum tamiḻ vēḷāṇ kuṭikkum yātum iyipiṉṟeṉpatu peṟṟām.
iṉi, potut toḻilkaḷāṉa mūṉṟaṉuḷ vēṭṭal yāvarkkum oru peṟṟiyataṉṟit tiripuṭiyatākum. kālatēyam paṟṟi māṟun taṉmiyatumām. ēṉiyiraṇṭumē yāvarum eññāṉṟum kaṭaṉākak koḷḷaṟpālavi. makkaḷiyaṟṟum viṉikaḷuḷ ivviraṇṭum pōṟ ciṟantaṉa vēṟilli. pavaṇanti muṉivar tamatu naṉṉūṟkaṇ "ōtal ītal ātip palviṉi" eṉa ivviraṇṭiyum kiḷantōti, ēṉiyavaṟṟi ‘āti' eṉpataṉuḷ aṭakkiṉami ivaṟṟiṉ ciṟappi uḷaṅkoṇṭēyātal vēṇṭum. ivaṟṟuḷḷum īkiyāṉatu pukaḻukkuc ciṟanta kāraṇa meṉpatu. vaḷḷuvaṉār īkiyiṉpiṉ pukaḻi vittu,  ‘ītal icipaṭa vāḻtal', īvār mēl niṟkum pukaḻ' eṉak kiḷan tōtutalāṉum, tolkāppiyaṉār vākiyiṉ tuṟikūṟu miṭattu ‘iṭiyil vaṇpukaḻk koṭimi' eṉṟurit talāṉum iṉitaṟiyappaṭum. maṟṟum, vaṇmipeṉpatu ciṟanta vīrarāyiṉārk kuriyatātalip puṟappāṭṭukkaḷ mutaliya vaṟṟālaṟiyalākum. 
"	āṉāvīki yaṭupō raṇṇal"
‘	pēkaṉ, koṭimaṭam paṭuta lallatu paṭimaṭam paṭāṉ piṟar paṭimayak kuṟiṉē’
eṉa varutal kāṇka.
iṉi, aṟivaṉ eṉpataṟku nacciṉārkkiṉiyar muḻutuṇarvuṭiyōṉ eṉakkūṟi, aṟivarkkuk kāṭṭāka akattiyaṉār mutaliyōrik kuṟittamiyē poruttamākum. iḷampūraṇar kaṇivaṉ (cōtiṭaṉ) eṉak kūṟiyatu poruntuva taṉṟu. veṇpāmāli yuṭiyārum aṟuvaṉ vāki eṉpataṟku mukkālamum uṇarum periyāriyalpu eṉṟē kūṟi, kaṇivaṉik kuṟittuk kaṇivaṉ mulli eṉa vēṟu tuṟikūṟip pōntār. nāliru vaḻakkiṟṟāpatap pakkam eṉpatu tavañ ceyvārkkuriya eṇvakiyiyalpu;
"	nīrpalakāl mūḻki nilattaciit tōluṭīic carcaṭi tāḻac cuṭarōmpi - ūraṭiyār kāṉakatta koṇṭu kaṭavuḷ viruntōmpal vāṉakat tuykkum vaḻi"	(pu.ve.māli)
eṉpatu avveṭṭaṉiyum uṇarttum. āciriyar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar ciṟitu vēṟupaṭa urittuḷḷataṉi avvuri nōkki aṟika. pālaṟi marapiṟ porunar eṉpār coṟpōr, icippōr mutaliyavaṟṟil veṉṟi yeytuvōrāvar.
iṉi, aṟattiṉ pakutiyākak kūṟiya tuṟi oṉpataṉiyum nōkkutum:
1. "pakaṭṭiṉāṉu māviṉāṉum, tukaṭṭapu ciṟappiṟ cāṉṟōr pakkam" 		- iḷampūraṇar.
āviṉāṉum eṉap pirittu, itu, pakaṭu puṟantarum vēḷāḷariyum, niriyōmpum vaṇikariyum kaṟikkumeṉak koṇṭār. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar māviṉāṉum eṉap pirittu, uḻavañcāmiyum, pakiyañcāmiyumākiya veṟṟiyik kuṟikkumeṉṟaṉar.
2. "kaṭṭil nītta pāl" - itaṟku aracaṉ aracavurimiyik kiviṭṭa pakuti eṉṟurittu, aracu tuṟanta veṉṟikkup parataṉiyum pārttaṉiyum kāṭṭiṉar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. iḷampūraṇar 'kaṭimaṉi nītta pāl' eṉap pāṭaṅkoṇṭu, piṟarmaṉi nayavāmiyeṉap poruḷ koṇṭār. itu 'naṭṭamiyoḻukkam' eṉa mēl varuvataṉuḷ aṭaṅkutaliṉ 'kaṭṭil nītta' eṉpatu poruttamākat tōṉṟukiṟatu.
3. "eṭṭu vaki nutaliya aviyam" - iḵtu avikkāṉ irukkum cāṉṟōriyalpu kūṟukiṉṟatu. puṟapporuḷ veṇpā māli aviya mulli, cālpu mulli eṉa iru tuṟi koṇṭu, muṉṉataṟku "navi nīṅka naṭuvu kūṟum, avi māntar iyalpu" eṉṟum, piṉṉataṟku, "cāṉṟōrtam cālpuku" eṉṟum kūṟukiṉṟatu. ivviraṇṭum 'eṭṭu vaki nutaliya aviyam' eṉpatil aṭaṅkum. eṇ vakik kuṇattiṉiyum uruvakañ ceytu cāṉṟōriyalpu kūṟum piṉvarum aḻakiya āciriya māṇavar ceyyuḷ aṟintu iṉpuṟaṟ pālatu:
"	kuṭippiṟap puṭuttup paṉuval cūṭi viḻuppē roḻukkam pūṇṭu kāmuṟa vāymivāy maṭuttu māntit tūymiyiṟ  kātaliṉ pattuṭ ṭaṅkit tītaṟu naṭuvunili neṭunakar viki vikalum aḻukkā ṟiṉmi avāa viṉmiyeṉ ṟiruperu nitiyamum orutā mīṭṭum  tōlā nāviṉ mēlōr pēravi uṭaṉamar irukki yorunāṭ peṟumeṉiṟ peṟukatil amma yāmē varaṉmuṟit tōṉṟuvaḻit tōṉṟuvaḻip palavup potintu niṉṟuḻi niṉṟuḻi ñāṅkar nillātu niliyaḻi yākki vāyppa im malartali yulakattuk koṭṭum piṟappē"
4. "kaṭṭami oḻukkattuk kaṇṇumi" - uṇmi nulkaḷāṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭa oḻukkattōṭu poruntiya kāṭci eṉṟapaṭi vaḷḷuvaṉār illaṟa viyaliṟ kūṟiya aṭakkamuṭimi, oḻukka muṭimi, piṟaṉmaṉi nayavāmi, aḻukkāṟāmi, veḵkāmi, tīviṉiyaccam mutaliyaṉa itaṉkaṇ aṭaṅkum.
5. "iṭiyil vaṇpukaḻk koṭimi" - iṭiyīṭillāta vaṇpukaḻip payakkum koṭittaṉmi eṉṟapaṭi ‘ulaka muḻutum piṟar pukaḻ vārāmit taṉpukaḻ parattaliṉ iṭiyil eṉṟār' eṉavum, ‘ikkoṭip pukaḻuṭiyāṉ mūppup piṇi cākkāṭṭukku añcāmiyiṉ atu vākiyām' eṉavum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar urittavi viḷaṅkoḷiṟpālaṉa.
6.	"piḻittōrt tāṅkuṅ kāval" - tammip piḻit tōrip poṟukkum pātukāppu eṉṟavāṟu. piḻi cey tōrkku immiyum maṟumiyum ētam varāmaṟ kāttaliṉa iḵtu ēṉiyōriṉ veṟṟiyāyiṟṟu eṉpar. koṭiyum, poṟiyum illaṟamāyiṉum ciṟappu nōkki vēṟu kūṟiṉār.
7.	"poruḷoṭu puṇarnta pakkam" meymmiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya iyalpu; āvatu meyyuṇarvu. itaṟku aracarkkuriya āṟaṅkaṅ kaḷiyum putalvarp pēṟṟiyum poruḷākak koḷvatu poruttamuṭittaṉṟu.
8. "aruḷoṭu puṇarnta akaṟci" aruḷuṭimiyōṭu poruntiya tuṟavaṟam eṉṟapaṭi. 'aruḷoṭu puṇartalāvatu ōruyirkku iṭar vantuḻit taṉṉuyirik koṭuttuk kāttalum, ataṉ varuttam taṉatāka varuntutalum, poyyāmi, kaḷḷāmi mutaliyaṉavumām eṉṟār nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
9. "kāmam nītta pāl" - epporuḷkaḷiṉum paṟṟaṟṟa pakuti.
iṟutikkaṭ kūṟiya mūṉṟum tirukkuṟaḷiluḷḷa mey yuṇarvu, tuṟavu, avāvaṟuttal eṉṉum atikāraṅkaḷiṉ poruṇmiyuṭiyaṉa. iṅṅaṉam tolkāppiyar karuttum vaḷḷuvar karuttum orumiyuṟum iṭaṅkaḷ pala.
itukāṟum, vākiyāvatu māntar tattam iyalpiṉi mikutippaṭuttalām eṉpatum, atu maṟam aṟam eṉa iru tiṟappaṭum eṉpatum, avaṟṟiṉ pakutikaḷ ivi yeṉpatum oruvāṟu urikkap peṟṟaṉa. aṟattiṉ pakutiyāyavaṟṟuḷ koṭi veṉṟi oṉṟeṉak kaṇṭōm. tamiḻ nāṭṭilē iññāṉṟu iṭiyil vaṇpukaḻk koṭimiyāl vāki cūṭiyavar aṇṇāmalip palkalik kaḻakatti niṟuviyō rākiya rācā car. aṇṇāmalic ceṭṭiyāravarkaḷē yāvar. avar kuṭumpattiṉarum avarum iyaṟṟiya piṟavaṟaṅkaḷum uvappillaṉavē. eṉiṉum kalviyiṉ poruṭṭu avar aḷitta ikkoṭiyāvaṟṟiṉum viñciya toṉṟāy vākik kuriyatāyiṟṟu. vāṇika vākikku utāraṇamākak kāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷatōr ceyyuḷ īṇṭikku mikavum iyipuṭiyatu aḵtu,
"	īṭṭiya tellām itaṉ poruṭṭeṉpatē  kāṭṭiya kivaṇmi kāṭṭiṉār - vēṭṭōṟum kāmarutārc ceṉṉi kaṭalcūḻ pukār vaṇikar tāmariyum cuṅkum pōl tantu"
eṉpatu. tāmari - patumaniti. caṅku - caṅkaniti. aṇṇāmali vaḷḷaliṉ aṟupatām āṇṭu niṟiveytutal kaṇṭu aṉivarum kaḷikavarvar; koṇṭāṭuvar; vāḻtteṭuppar. yāmum avaratu neṭiya nalvāḻvu karuti, tirucciṟṟampala muṭiyār tirukkiyā leḻutiya tiruppāṭṭiṉic cintittu vāḻttukiṉṟōm.
"	kāraṇi kaṟpakam kaṟṟavar naṟṟuṇi pāṉarokkal cīraṇi cintāmaṇi aṇitillic civaṉaṭikkut tāraṇi koṉṟiyaṉ takkōrtam caṅkaniti viticēr ūraṇi vaṟṟavark kūraṉmaṟṟi yāvarkkum ūtiyamē"

17.vikaṟi niivu
(nilimaṇṭila āciriyappā)
	māṇava naṇparkaḷkāḷ! māṇava naṇparkāḷ!! pēṇiyāṉ kūṟuva peṭpuṭaṉ kēṇmō, mārkaḻi patiṉeṭ ṭiravukaḻi vikaṟic cīrkeḻu poḻutiṟ ṟuyilorī it tikaḻum
	teḷḷiya vuṇarvuṭaṉ cintit tiṭuvēṟ	5	kuḷḷan taṉṉi lutitta niṉivukaḷ paṟpala vāmavi pāṅkuṟak kiḷakkuval;	 coṟpaṭu patiṉin tāṇṭumuṉ ṟoṭaṅkik kallūri yiṟṟamiḻk kaṇakkāya ṉākip
	pallōru makiḻap paṇpuṭaṉ virivuri	10 nikaḻttivaru kiṉṟēṉ nīrmiyil muṉ piṉ mikapperu vēṟṟumi viḷiyumā ṟeṉṉē! muṉṉelā māṇavark kāciriya ṉaṉṟi aṉṉavark kaminta tōḻaṉu māvēṉ;
	nampiyā rūrar tampirāṉ ṟōḻar	15 eṉpatu terivār impariṟ ṟantiyar makkaḷ, āciriyar māṇavar, kaṇavar takka maṉiviyar, aracartam kuṭikaḷ tāmuṟu tōḻami takutiyaṉ ṟeṉṉār;
	ēmuṟa muṉṉi māṇava riṟpalar	20 kaṇṇaṉ pullāṅ kuḻalici kēṭṭa vaṇṇa nallā ṉiṉameṉa makiḻntē eṟcūḻ taruvar; yāṉumaṉ ṉārum  aṟpuṭaṉ kūṭi yaḷavaḷā vuṟuvēm;
	yāṉava rirukki yeytuva; laṉṉār	25 āṉavaki yeṉṉi laṭintu kāṇkuṟuvar; cērntuṇṭi koḷvēm, cērntu viḷiyāṭuvēm; nērntiṭu miṉpa tuṉpaṅka ḷellām oruvark koruva rucāvuvam, avaruḷ
	maruvu nuṇṇaṟi vāḷarum yāṉum	30 vāṉa nūl eṇṇūl maṉṉumey yuṇarvunūl ēṉavum pēci yirunilan taṉṉil ‘nuṇṇa ṟiviṉāroṭu kūṭi nukarvuṭimi viṇṇulakē yokkum viḻiviṟ ṟāl’ eṉum
	tollōr kaṭṭuric cuvi terintiṭuvam;	35 pallā ṟiṅṅaṉ payaṉ ṟuyt tiṭuṅkāṟ cilar tama tūrkkuc ciṉṉāṭ piriyiṉum alaman tuḷḷa maṉaliṭi meḻukāyk kaṇṇī rukuttaluṅ kaṭavam; maṟ ṟavaruḷ
	kaṟkun tuṟiviṭut takaṉṟataṟ piṉṉum	40 eṟkuṟu naṇparā yiyintuḷār palarāl; maṟṟin nāḷiṉ vakimiyō veṉṉil, māṇava reṉpāl matippu naṉiyuṭiyar, kāṇavu maṭakkaṅ kāṭṭuvar, maṇiyil
	uṭaṉirup pataṉuk kañci yoṭuṅkuvar,	45 toṭarpu vēṟilar; coṟṟiṭil yāṉum paḷḷiyi lavarkkup palkalik kaḻakam teḷḷi vititta nūl cepputa laṉṟip piṟitu toṭarpilāp peṟṟiya ṉāvēṉ;
	keḻutaki yaṉpum kēṇmiyum ucāvum	50 paḻutilā iṉpum piriviṉiṟ parivum iṉṉayāṅ koḷittaṉa? ivvaki nilimi eṉṉavā ṟeytiṉa? iḷamitīr tiṟattiṉ peṟṟimi yālō? peruviṉic ceṟiviṉ
	uṟṟiṭa lāṉō? oṇporuḷ kalviyiṟ	55 perumicāl cillōr payiṟciyum piṟarāl varuciṟu pukaḻcciyum vāyttami yālō? oṉṟun tēṟēṉ; uvikaḷā leṉṉiṉ, āṇṭiṉ mutirvum aḻivilā muyaṟciyum
	yāṇṭum parakkum īṭeṭup pillā	60 viḻuttaku pukaḻum mēviyōr cilar tām paḻuttaaṉ puṭiya rākunaṟ pāṉmi kāṇṭaliṉ, aviyuṅ kāraṇa mākā; evvāṟāyiṉu maṉpiṉi yiḻantē
	ovvāt tuyariṉuk kōrilak kāṉēṉ;	65 nalloḻuk karukiṉaṉ, nalantikaḻ tūymi ollumā ṟillēṉ, nōṉpuka ḷuñaṟṟēṉ, meyyuṇar vuṟukilaṉ, mētaku pūcaṉi ceyyumā ṟaṟiyēṉ, āyiṉum cīrtta
	aṉpē yamiyumeṉ ṟiruntēṉ, atuvum	70 vaṉpē yāki muṭitaliṉ, vaḷḷuvar ‘aṉpiṉ vaḻiya tuyirnili yaḵtilārk keṉputōl pōrtta vuṭam ‘peṉum paṭiyāṉ uyiriliyāṉēṉ ō!ō! koṭitē!!
	ayarvuṟu meṉṉuḷam; āṟṟakil lēṉē!	75 aṇmiyiṟ payilu maṟivucā liḷiñar tamvayi ṉaṉpu tāṉpeṟā tēṉuk kevvuyir māṭṭumaṉ peṅṅaṉam kūṭum? uyirkaḷpā laṉpilā vuṟutiyil lēṟku
	mayarvaṟu maṟiyum māṟṟamu maṉaṉum	80 tīṇṭutaṟ kariya cemporu ḷiṉpāl vēṇ maṉpu viḷiyumā ṟeḷitō? āyiṉu mapporu ḷaṉpuru vākaliṉ tāyiṉuñ ciṟanta taṉipporu ḷatuvē
	vāymicā laṉpi vaḻaṅkumeṉ ṟamivēṉ;	85 iṉṉavē yeṉṉuḷan tuṉṉiya niṉivukaḷ; maṉṉiya cīrpeṟu māṇava naṇparkāḷ! nivirtā maṉpiṉ nilikkaḷa mākit tāvil paḻamporuḷ tāṇmalar cūṭi
	aṟivun tiruvu māṟṟalu meyti	90  neṟimiyiṉ vāḻiyar nīṇilat tuyarntē.
18. uḻutoḻil
ulaki āḷum aracaṉ mutal kāṭṭilē tiriyum vēṭṭuvaṉ variyil yāvarkkum iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa uṇavum, uṭiyum  ām. caṅkappulavarākiya nakkīrar,
“	teṇkaṭal vaḷākam potumi yiṉṟi  veṇkuṭi niḻaṟṟiya vorumi yōrkkum  naṭunāḷ yāmattum pakalun tuñcāṉ  kaṭumāp pārkkuṅ kallā voruvaṟkum  uṇpatu nāḻi uṭuppavi yiraṇṭē”
eṉṟu puṟap pāṭṭilē pukaṉṟu vittaṉar. oḷavi yārum tamatu nīti nūlil,
“uṇpatu nāḻi yuṭuppatu nāṉku muḻam”
eṉṟu iyampiṉār.
uṇavukkum uṭikkum iṉṟiyamiyāta mūlap poruḷ kaḷ uḻutoḻilāṟ kiṭippaṉa. makkaḷ eññāṉṟu uḻutu payir ceyyat toṭaṅkiṉarō aññāṉṟē nākarikam kāl koḷḷap peṟuvatāyiṟṟu. tamiḻ nāṭṭilē ittoḻil eṉṟu toṭaṅkiyateṉa iyamputal aritu. mikap paḻiya nūlākiya tolkāppiyattil uḻuttoḻil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. maṟṟum annūlilē tiṇikaḷ vakuttu, avaṟṟiṉkaṇ uṇṭām karupporuḷkaḷ iviyeṉat terivikkumiṭattu uṇavu, maram, pū eṉpaṉavum. makkaḷ ceytoḻilum karupporuḷākak kaṇikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. malinel, tiṉi, mūṅkilarici eṉpaṉa kuṟiñciyilum, varaku, cāmi, mutiri eṉpaṉa mulliyilum , cennel, veṇṇel eṉpaṉa marutattilum viḷiyum uṇavup poruḷkaḷām. karumpum marutattiṟ kuriyatu. vēṅki, cantaṉam, akil, tēkku mutaliyaṉa kuṟiñci nilattilum, uḻiñi, pāli, ōmi, iruppi mutaliyaṉa pāli nilattilum, koṉṟi, kāyā, kuruntam mutaliyaṉa mulli nilattilum, kāñci, vañci, marutam mutaliyaṉa maruta nilattilum, kaṇṭal, puṉṉi, ñāḻal mutaliyaṉa neytal nilattilum vaḷarum maraṅkaḷām. iṅṅaṉam ovvoru nilattiṟkē uriyavākāmal, iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nilaṅkaḷil vaḷarntu payaṉṟarattakka maram mutaliyaṉavum uḷḷaṉa. vāḻi marutattilum, kuṟiñciyiṉum payirātaṟkuriyatu. teṅku marutam neytal iraṇṭiṉum payaṉṟarum. māmaram marutam, mulli, kuṟiñci eṉṉum mūṉṟu nilattiṉum taḷirkkum paḻiya caṅkappulavarkaḷ ivviyalpiṉi naṉkaṟintu tiṇi vaḷaṅ kaḷik kūṟumiṭattu avvattiṇikkuriya poruḷkaḷiyē kūṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē, kuṟiñci, pāli,mulli, marutam, neytal eṉṉum nilaṅkaḷil iṉṉiṉṉa payirkaḷum, maraṅkaḷum, malar tarum ceṭi koṭi mutaliyaṉavum payirākum eṉpataṉip paṇṭit tamiḻar aṟintiruntaṉar eṉpatum, aṟintu ammuṟiyē vēḷāṇmi ceytu vantaṉar eṉpatum pulaṉākiṉṟaṉa. iṟṟi nāḷ araciyalār vivacāyak kallūrikaḷāl iṉṉamum potu makkaḷ aṟiyaviyalāta ittakiya vēḷāṇmi nuṇukkaṅkaḷi ettaṉiyō āyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ tamiḻmakkaḷ aṟin tiruntaṉar eṉpatu viyappum makiḻcciyum viḷikkuñ ceytiyām
nāṭṭiṉ ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟavanta teyvap pulamit tiruvaḷḷuvaṉār toṭakkattilēyē ‘taḷḷāviḷiyuḷum’ eṉa vēḷāṇmiyik kuṟippiṭṭup piṉṉarum ataṉi vaṟpuṟuttuvārāyiṉar. uṇavup poruḷukku vēṟṟu nāṭṭi etirnōkkum nāṭu eṉṟēṉum pakivar muṟṟukiyālō, piṟa taṭiyālō ataṉip peṟa iyalāviṭiṉ aḻin toḻivatu tiṇṇam. ātaliṉ akkuṟipāṭillāta nāṭṭiṉiyē ‘nāṭu’ eṉac ciṟappittuc colvar āṉṟōr. nāṭṭil viḷivu mikutaṟku nilattiṉ naṉmiyum, uḻavar muyaṟciyum kāraṇa mākum. ātaliṉ uḻutoḻiliyum, uḻavariyum tamiḻmakkaḷ peritum pārāṭṭip pōṟṟi vantaṉar.
“	cuḻaṉṟum ērppiṉṉatu ulakam ataṉāl uḻantum uḻavē tali”
“	uḻuvā rulakattārkku āṇi aḵtāṟṟātu eḻuvāri yellām poṟuttu’
eṉṟivvāṟāka vaḷḷuvaṉār teḷḷiyuritta pākkaḷ aṉittiyum paṉmuṟi cintittu uṇmi yuṇartal vēṇṭum.
kalviyiṟ periyarāya kampar itaṉic ciṟappittaṟ keṉṟē ‘ēreḻupatu’ eṉa oru nūliyaṟṟiṉar. oḷaviyār ciṟuvar uḷḷattil itaṉip patiya vikkuṅ karuttuṭaṉ,
“	paruvattē payir cey”
“	neṟpayir viḷi”
“	pūmi tiruttiyuṇ”			eṉavum.
“	cīrittēṭiṉ ērittēṭu”
“	toḻutūṇ cuviyiṉ uḻutūṇ iṉitu”
“	mēḻic celvam kōḻi paṭātu”	eṉavum
muṟiyē ātticūṭi, koṉṟivēntaṉ eṉṉum nūṟkaḷiṟ kūṟi yuḷḷaṉar.
muṟkālattirunta tamiḻ vēntarkaḷum vēḷāṇmiyiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmiyi uṇarntu kāṭu koṉṟu nāṭākkiṉar; ēri, kuḷaṅkaḷit tōṇṭuvittaṉar; yāṟṟiṉiṉṟu kiḷi kaḷum, kaṇṇāṟukaḷum veṭṭuvittaṉar; aṇikaḷum, mataku kaḷum, kaṭṭuvittaṉar; iṅṅaṉam uḻavukkuriya vacatikaḷip perukkiyataṉṟi, viḷiporuḷkaḷil āṟil oru kūṟē iṟiyākap peṟṟum, ataṉiyum taḷḷa vēṇṭiya vaḻit taḷḷiyum palavāṟu uḻavar kuṭiyip purantu vantaṉar. ak kālattirunta pulavarkaḷum aracarkaḷi neruṅki vēḷāṇ kuṭiyip pātukākkum kaṭappāṭṭiṉi avarkku eṭuttuk kāṭṭit teruṭṭiṉar.
pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉip pāṭiya oru pulavar ‘tam nāṭṭilē nīr nilikaḷip perukkiya aracarē uṭampum uyirum paṭittōrāvar; avarē pakivari vellavum, maṟumi yiṉpatti nukaravum, pukaḻi niṟuttavum vallavarāvar’ eṉṟiyampiṉār. cōḻaṉ kiḷḷivaḷavaṉip pāṭiya oruvar,
“	varupaṭi tāṅkip peyarpuṟat tārttup  porupaṭi tarūuṅ koṟṟamum uḻupaṭi ūṉṟucāl maruṅkiṉ īṉṟataṉ payaṉē”	eṉṟum.
“	pakaṭu puṟantarunar pāram ompik kuṭipuṟan tarukuvi yāyiṉ niṉ aṭipuṟan tarukuvar aṭaṅkā tōrē”	eṉṟum
aṟivuṟuttaṉar.
pāṇṭiyaṉ aṟivuṭi nampiyi nōkkip picirānti yār eṉṉum pulavar ‘aṟivuṭiya aracaṉ iṟi koḷḷum neṟiyi aṟintu koḷḷiṉ, avaṉatu nāṭu kōṭi poruḷi īṭṭik koṭuttut tāṉum mikavum taḻikkum; avaṉ aṟivāl melliyaṉāki, nāṭōṟum variciyaṟiyāta āra vārattiyuṭiya cuṟṟattōṭu kūṭi, aṉpukeṭak koḷḷum poruḷi virumpiṉ avaṉum uṇṇap peṟāṉ ulakamum keṭum’ eṉṟu urittaruḷiṉar. 
ivvāṟu pulavarkaḷ kūṟum aṟivuriyic cevi maṭuttum, nallamiccarōṭu kūṭiyum tamiḻ vēntarkaḷ uḻavarkku utaviceytu uḻutoḻili vaḷarttu vantamiyāl vēḷāṇmi vaḷam peṟṟatu. ‘vēliyāyiram viḷiyuṭṭāka’ eṉṟa paṭi oru vēli nilam āyirakkalam viḷivuṭiyatāyiṟṟu. makkaḷ paciyiṉṟi vāḻntu pala nalaṅkaḷukkum uriyarāyiṉar. uḻavarkaḷ nilattiṉ kiḻavarāyiruntaṉar; ataṉāl, kiḻār eṉṟa peyarum avarkku uḷatāyiṟṟu.
iṅṅaṉam muṉṉāḷil
“uḻutuṇṭu vāḻvārē vāḻvār”
eṉṟu pukaḻumpaṭi perumitamuṭaṉ urimi vāḻkki vāḻntu vanta vēḷāṇ māntar ippoḻutu eḻucciyiṉṟi uraṅ kuṉṟi niṟkiṉṟaṉar; uḻutoḻil veṟukkattakka niliyi aṭin tuḷḷatu. itaṉ kāraṇam ārāytaṟkuriyatu.
paṇṭi nāḷilē innāṭṭil vēḷāṇmi ceḻit tiruntatu pōṉṟē palavakik kittoḻilkaḷum, vāṇika mum mēṉmiyuṟṟiruntaṉa. kit toḻilkaḷuḷ neytaṟ ṟoḻil eytiyirunta mēṉmiyāvarkkum iṟum pūtu viḷippatoṉṟām. paḻiya tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷi ārāyiṉ ivvuṇmi pulaṉām. paḻiya nāḷil innāṭṭukku vantu ceṉṟa yavaṉāciriyarkaḷākiya tālami, piḷiṉi, perippuḷūs mutalāṉavarkaḷ eḻutiyuḷḷa kuṟippukkaḷālum itaṉi naṉku aṟiyalām. innāṭṭiṉ viḷi poruḷkaḷum ceyporuḷ kaḷum vēṟṟu nāṭukaḷil mikutiyāṉa vilikku viṟkappaṭṭami yāl innāṭṭiṉ celvam mēṉmēṟ perukuvatāyiṟṟu. vāḻkkikku vēṇṭum iṉṟiyamiyāta poruḷkaṭkup piṟa nāṭṭiṉar kiyi etirpārkkāmalē iṅkuḷḷavarkaḷ vāḻntu vantaṉar. uḻutoḻil ceyvār tam viḷi poruḷ kaḷik koṇṭē vēṇṭum paṇṭaṅkaḷi malivākap peṟṟu vāḻkki naṭāttiṉar; viruntōmpal mutaliya aṟaṅkaḷi mēṟkoṇṭu ēṉiyōrkkum utavi purintu vantaṉar. ataṉāl uḻutoḻilukkē taṉiyāka perumi iruntuvantatu.
innāḷilō innāṭṭuk kittoḻil vāṇika mellām nacittuviṭṭamiyāl, uṭi mutaliya iṉṟiyamiyāta poruḷ kaṭkum piṟanāṭṭut toḻilāḷariyum, vāṇikariyum etir nōkka vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. vēṟṟu nāṭṭavarkaḷiṉ kūṭṭuṟa vāl vāḻkkiyiṉ iyalpukaḷum māṟic celaviṉi mikuvittu varukiṟatu. makaḷir tam kiyāṟ ceytuvanta aṭukkaḷit toḻilkaḷiyum iyantiraṅkaḷ kavarntu koṇṭaṉa. ūṟukaṟikaḷum, tiṉpaṇṭaṅkaḷum kūṭa vēṟṟu nāṭṭiliruntu varuvikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
iṅṅaṉam makkaḷiṉ muyaṟci curuṅkavum, celavu perukavum pala vaḻikaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. inniliyil vēḷāṇmi cey vōrkku varippaḷuvum nāḷukku nāḷ mikuvatāyiṟṟu. avarkaḷ vari celutti eñciya poruḷ koṇṭu vāḻkki naṭatta iyalātavarāki viṭṭaṉar. ataṉāl uḻutoḻilukkum pōtiyavaḷavu celavu ceyya āṟṟal aṟṟaṉar. iyalpilē uraṅkuṉṟi varukiṉṟa nilaṅkaḷ takkavāṟu paṇpaṭuttap paṭāmiyālum viḷivu kuṟivaṉavāyiṉa. aṉṉōr eṉ ceyvar? mēlum mēlum kaṭaṉ vāṅkuvārāyiṉar; nilaṅkaḷi viṟpārāyiṉar. ippoḻutuḷḷa nilaṅkaḷellām vāṅku vār iṉmiyiṉ viṟkappaṭātaviyē tavira vēṟilli. inniliyil viṭṭiruppiṉ ciṉṉāḷil uḻutoḻil eṉpatu oḻintuviṭum. tēca nalattiṟ karuttuṭiya perumakkaḷ ippoḻutē itaṟku ēṟṟa ceyalmuṟikaḷik kiyāṇṭu, ittoḻilic citiyaviṭātu pātukāttal vēṇṭum.
innōykku mutalāvatākac ceyyavēṇṭiya marut tuvam nilaṅkaḷiṉ paṇpukaḷi uḷḷavāṟu cōtittuṇarntu, niliyāṉa kuṟinta variyi vitittalākum. kuṟinta vaṭṭikkuk kaṭaṉ koṭuttal, vēḷāṇmi nuṭpaṅkaḷi yāvarum naṉku aṟiyum paṭi ceytal, mutaliyaṉa. aṭuttuc ceytaṟkuriyaṉa. ivi ōraḷavu ceyyappaṭṭuvariṉum, iṉṉum mikutiyākavum virivākavum ceyyappaṭa vēṇṭum. maṟṟu, kalvi kaṟṟup paṭṭam peṟṟavarkaḷum uḻutoḻil ceyya muṟpaṭṭu, toḻil ceyvatik kuṟivāka matikkum eṇṇatti māṟṟutal vēṇṭum. ittakiya ceyalkaḷil aracāṅkattiṉarum nāṭṭu naṉmakkaḷum karuttu vittāl nāṭu nalam peṟum. 

19. paḻantamiḻ makkaḷiṉ camayanili
tamiḻ makkaḷ pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpē uyir uṭalkaḷi vevvēṟāka uṇarntiruntaṉar eṉpatu tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷukku uyir eṉṟum, mey eṉṟum peyariṭṭirut talāṉē peṟappaṭum. evvaḷavu paḻaṅkālattiṟ ceṉṟu nōkkiṉum tamiḻarkaḷ ulakam, uyir, kaṭavuḷ eṉṉum mūṉṟu poruḷkaḷiyum uṇarntiruntamiyōṭu, ulakam aimpūtaṅ kaḷālāyateṉṟum, uyirkaḷ viṉikkaṭṭuṭiyaṉa eṉṟum, kaṭavuḷ viṉikkaṭṭillātavar eṉṟum aṟintiruntaṉar eṉpatu pulaṉākiṉṟatu. mikka paḻami vāyntatākiya tol kāppiyattil,
“	kālam ulakam uyirē uṭampē pālvari teyvam viṉiyē pūtam”
eṉavum,
“	nilattī nīrvaḷi vicumpō ṭintum kalanta mayakkam ulakam”
eṉavum,
“	viṉiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉ muṉivaṉ”
eṉavum kūṟap paṭṭiruttal kāṇka. maṟṟum atilē kaṭavuḷ eṉṉum peyarum, kaṭavuḷi vāḻttutalum kūṟiyiruppa tiṉiṉṟu paramporuḷāṉatu ellāvaṟṟiyum kaṭantu niṟkum iyalpiṉateṉpatum ataṉi niṉintu vāḻtti vaḻipaṭutalē uyirkaḷ kaṭṭiṉiṉṟum nīṅki iṉpuṟutaṟkuriya neṟi eṉpatum avarkaḷ aṟittiruntami pōtarum. iyalpākavē kaṭṭiṉ miyum, muṟṟuṇarvum, varampiliṉpamum uṭiya am meyp poruḷiyē palvēṟu kālaṅkaḷil paṟpala tēyattiṉumuḷḷa māntarkaḷ tāmtām aṟintavāṟu vevvēṟu peyarāṉum, vevvēṟu neṟiyāṉum vaḻipaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉar.
tamiḻ nāṭṭilē kuṟiñcinila makkaṭku murukak kaṭavuḷum, mullinila makkaṭkut tirumālum, marutanila makkaṭku intiraṉum, neytalnila makkaṭku varuṇaṉum teyvaṅkaḷ eṉat tamiḻ nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. tiṇippirivum, tiṇi makkaṭ pirivum iyaṟkiyāṉaviyē eṉiṉum, tiṇikaṭkut teyva aṭivu ēṟakkuṟiya nālāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpu ēṟpaṭṭirukka vēṇṭumeṉat tōṉṟukiṟatu. kaṇṇappirāṉ āyarpāṭiyil vaḷarntu āyarkaḷuṭaṉ cērntu āviṉam mēyttal mutaliya viḷiyāṭalkaḷik ceytu pōnta piṉṉarē mulli makkaḷiṭattu māyōṉ vaḻipāṭu ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṟṟu eṉṉalām. āriya vētaṅkaḷil mikuttuk kūṟap paṭum intiraṉ vaḻipāṭum varuṇaṉ vaḻipāṭum akkālat tiṟṟāṉ tamiḻarkaḷālē kikkoḷḷap paṭṭirukka vēṇṭum. murukaṉ vaḻipāṭu aṅṅaṉam iṭiyil uṇṭāyateṉa niṉikkak kāraṇamilli. iṉi, paṇṭit tamiḻa ilakkiyaṅ kaḷil ciṟappākak kūṟappaṭum kaṭavuḷar nālvar. nakkīrar pāṭiya 56-m puṟappāṭṭilē iṭapakkoṭiyum kaṇiccip paṭiyum maṇimiṭaṟum uṭiya civaperumāṉum, vālvaḷi mēṉiyum kalappip paṭiyum paṉikkoṭiyum uṭiya palarāmaṉum, nīlamaṇi yokkum mēṉiyum kaluḻakkoṭiyum uṭiya kaṇṇaṉum, mayilākiya koṭiyum mayilūrtiyum uṭiya murukavēḷum muṟiyē kūṟappeṟṟuḷar. atil,
“	ñālaṅ kākkum kāla muṉpiṉ tōlā nallici nālvar”
eṉa ivarkaḷ ulakattip purappōrākak kūṟiyiruppatum cintikkaṟpālatu. kapilar pāṭiya iṉṉānāṟpatilum,
“	mukkaṭ pakavaṉ aṭitoḻā tārkkiṉṉā poṟpaṉi veḷḷiyi uḷḷā toḻukiṉṉā cakkarat tāṉi maṟappiṉṉā āṅkiṉṉā cattiyāṉ ṟāṭoḻā tārkku”
eṉa innālvarum immuṟiyē kūṟappeṟṟaṉar. cilappatikāram intira viḻavūreṭutta kātiyil,
“	piṟavā yākkip periyōṉ kōyilum aṟumukac cevvēḷ aṇitikaḻ kōyilum vālvaḷi mēṉi vāliyōṉ kōyilum nīlamēṉi neṭiyōṉ kōyilum”
eṉavum, ūrkāṇ kātiyil,
“	nutalviḻi nāṭṭat tiṟiyōṉ kōyilum uvaṇac cēval uyarttōṉ niyamamum mēḻivalaṉ uyartta veḷḷi nakaramum kōḻic cēvaṟ koṭiyōṉ kōṭṭamum”
eṉavum kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattilum, maturiyilum innāṉku tēvarkaḷiṉ kōyilkaḷ iruntaṉavāka muṟiyē kūṟap peṟṟuḷa. ivaṟṟil nālvariṉ muṟi muṟkūṟiya vāṟaṉṟic ciṟitu piṟaḻntuḷatu. eṉiṉum, yāṇṭum civaperumāṉē mutalil vittuk kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉar aṉṟiyum. avar piṟavā yākkip periyōṉ eṉavum pakavaṉ eṉavum, iṟiyōṉ eṉavum, talimi tōṉṟak kūṟappaṭutalum cintikkaṟpālatu. vēṟiṭaṅkaḷil iṉṉum veḷippaṭiyākavum civapirāṉukku mutaṉmi kūṟappeṟṟuḷatu. 
“	nīrum nilaṉum tīyum vaḷiyum māka vicumpō ṭintuṭa ṉiyaṟṟiya  maḻuvāḷ neṭiyōṉ tali vaṉāka mācaṟa viḷaṅkiya yākkiyar”
eṉa māṅkuṭi marutaṉār ‘maturik kāñci yilum,
“	nutalviḻi nāṭṭan tiṟiyōṉ mutalāp pativāḻ catukkattut teyvamī ṟāka”
eṉak kūlavāṇikaṉ cāttaṉār maṇimēkaliyilum kūṟi yiruttal nōkkuka. ivvāṟṟāl, toṉṟu toṭṭut tamiḻ makkaḷ muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷākak koṇṭu vaḻipaṭṭu vanta teyvam civaperumāṉē eṉpatu veḷḷiṭi malipōl teḷḷitiṉ viḷaṅkum. piṟ kālattuc camaṇavāciriyar mutalāyiṉārum tamiḻ vaḻakkiṉ ivvuṇmi yuṇarntē tam nūlkaḷil pala kaṭavuḷariyum kūṟumiṭattuc civaperumāṉi mutaṟ kaṇ vittuk kūṟip pōntaṉar. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, aṭiyārkku nallār mutaliya uriyāciriyaṉmārum .inneṟi piḻiyāmalē uri varivārāyiṉar. iṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷ tirumāliṭattum cevvēḷiṭattum niṟinta aṉpuṭiyarākavē viḷaṅkiṉar. iṉṉōriyum ulakaṅkākkum kaṭavuḷarākap pōṟṟip pukaḻntaṉarēyaṉṟi oru ciṟitum kuṟivupaṭak kūṟiṉārallar. maṟṟum toki nūl kaḷiṉ kaṭavuḷ vāḻttilum, paripāṭaliṉum civapirāṉōṭu vēṟṟumiyillāta mutaṟ ṟeyvaṅkaḷākavē ivarkaḷ paravappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. iṉṉum, civa vaḻipāṭṭiṉum cevvēḷ vaḻipāṭu viñciyiruntateṉak kūṟutalum cālum. pattup pāṭṭil oru pāṭṭum (tirumurukāṟṟup paṭiyum) paripāṭaliṉ muppattoru pāṭṭum murukappirāṉukku uriyavāyiruttalē ivvuṇmiyi viḷakkum; murukavēḷ eḻuntaruḷi yirukkum tiruppatikaḷ pala caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷiṟ payiṉṟiruttalum ataṟkuc cāṉṟākum. tirupparaṅkuṉṟam, tiruccīralivāy, tiruvāviṉaṉkuṭi, tiruvērakam mutaliya tiruppatikaḷit tirumurukāṟṟup paṭi kūṟukiṟatu. paripāṭalum, piṟa toki kaḷum avaṟṟip pukaḻnturikkiṉṟaṉa; cilappatikāramum,
“	cīrkeḻu centilum *ceṅkōṭum veṇkuṉṟum ērakamum nīṅkā iṟivaṉ”
eṉṟēttukiṉṟatu. 
iṉi, vaṭa nāṭṭiṟ ceṅkōlōcciya peruvēnta ṉākiya acōkaṉ taṉ makkaḷākiya caṅkamittiri, makēntu eṉpavarkaḷuṭaṉ puttamata pōtakar palarit tēyantōṟum viṭuttu ammatattip paravac ceytamiyāl, ki.mu. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē puttamatam tamiḻ nāṭṭilum iṭam peṟṟu vaḷarat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. camaṇa matamum akkālan toṭaṅkiyē paravaluṟṟatu eṉṉalām. cilappatikāram mutaliyaṉa koṇṭu nōkkumiṭattu, ki.pi. mutal, iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷilē, tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ talinakaraṅkaḷākiya pukār, uṟanti, maturi, vañci, kāñci eṉṉum iṭaṅkaḷil camaṇa putta mataṅkaḷ mikka valimiyuṟṟiruntami pulaṉākiṉṟatu. arukaṉ kōṭṭamum, puttacittiyamum nakarppuṟaṅkaḷil kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa; camaṇa muṉivarkaḷum, putta muṉivar kaḷum uṟiyum paḷḷikaḷ āṅkāṅkiruntaṉa; cāraṇarkaḷ iṭiyaṟātu matapōtaṉi purintu vantārkaḷ; āṭavarkaḷum peṇṭirkaḷum mikutiyāka ammataṅkaḷiṟ pukuvārāyiṉar. akkālattut tamiḻ vēntarkaḷ naṭuvuniliyuṭaṉ it taṉikkum iṭantantu avarkaḷiyum purantu vantami peritum pārāṭṭaṟkuriyatu. civaṉ, tirumāl mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷi eḷḷutaliṉṟiyē avarkaḷ tam mataṅkaḷip pōtittu vantamiyāṟṟāṉ avi virintu paravaluṟṟaṉa eṉak karutal poruntum.
iṉi, akkālattiluḷḷa pulavarkaḷāyiṉum piṟarāyiṉum avarkaḷ tammiṉ vēṟupaṭṭa camayattārkaḷiṉ uṇarcciyi matittu avaṟṟicciṟitum ikaḻātu pārāṭṭi vantaṉar eṉpatum, ōr kuṭumpattiluḷḷa palar tattam viruppattiṟ kiyintavāṟu vevvēṟu mataṅkaḷik kikkoṇṭum tammuḷ vēṟṟumi ciṟitumiṉṟi oḻuki vantaṉar eṉpatum paḻiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum aṟiyalākiṉṟa uṇmikaḷām. ec camayattiyum ikaḻātu pōṟṟum peruntaṉmiyuṭimikkut teyvap pulamit tiruvaḷḷuvaṉāriyum, iḷaṅkōvaṭi kaḷiyum ciṟanta eṭuttuk kāṭṭākak koḷḷal takum.
vaḷḷuvaṉār,
“	nulōr tokuttavaṟṟu ḷellām tali”
“	pallāṟṟāl tēriṉu maḵtē tuṇi”
eṉṟiṅṅaṉam ellā nūliṉum nallaṉa eṭuttu ellārkkum potuppaṭak kūṟic celvar; em matattiyum ikaḻār.
iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, tam tamiyaṉākiya ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ civaperumāṉ tiruvaruḷāṟ piṟantavaṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ imayañ cellap puṟappaṭṭa poḻutu ulaku potiyuruvat tuyarn tōṉākiya civaperumāṉ cēvaṭiyi muṭimēṟ koṇṭu yāriyum iṟiñcāc ceṉṉiyāl iṟiñci valaṅkoṇṭu pōntu yāṉiyiṉ piṭaril ēṟiyaruḷiya piṉpu āṭakamāṭattu aṟituyilamarnta tirumāliṉ cēṭaṅ koṇṭu vantu cilar niṉṟu tutittakāliyil tāṉ civaperumāṉ tiruvaṭiyi maṇimuṭi mēl vittiruttaliṉ ataṉi vāṅkit tōḷiṉ mītu tarittaṉaṉ eṉṟum kūṟu mukattāl ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ civaperumāṉiyē muḻu mutalākak koṇṭu vaḻipaṭuvōṉ eṉpatu peṟa vittu, tam koḷkiyum atuvātali ‘piṟavāyāk kip periyōṉ’ eṉpatu mutaliyavaṟṟāṟ kuṟippiṭṭaṉar. aṉṉarāyiṉum, avar, kavuntiyaṭikaḷ arukatēvaṉi vāḻttutal kūṟumiṭattu,
“	orumūṉ ṟavittōṉ ōtiya ñāṉat tirumoḻik kallateṉ ceviyakam tiṟavā kāmaṉi veṉṟōṉ āyiratteṭṭu nāmam allatu navilā teṉṉā aivari veṉṟōṉ aṭiyiṇi yallatu kivarik kāṇiṉum kāṇā eṉkaṇ aruḷaṟam pūṇṭōṉ tirumeyk kallateṉ poruḷil yākki pūmiyiṟ poruntātu”
eṉṟiṅṅaṉamum;
vēṭṭuva variyil avarkaḷ koṟṟaviyip paravu miṭattu,
“	amari kumari kavuri camari cūli nīli mālavaṟ kiḷaṅkiḷi aiyi ceyyavaḷ veyyavāṭ ṭaṭakkip pāykalip pāvi pintoṭip pāvi āykalip pāvi aruṅkalappāvi”
“	āṉittōl pōrttup puliyi ṉuriyuṭuttuk kāṉat terumik karuntalimēl niṉṟāyāl vāṉōr vaṇaṅka maṟimēṉ maṟiyāki ñāṉak koḻuntāy naṭukkiṉṟi yēniṟpāy”
eṉṟu ivvāṟākavum;
āycciyar kuraviyil avarkaḷ tirumālip paravu miṭattu,
“	periyavaṉi māyavaṉip pērulaka mellām virikamala vuntiyuṭi viṇṇavaṉik kaṇṇum tiruvaṭiyum kiyum kaṉivāyum ceyya kariyavaṉik kāṇāta kaṇṇeṉṉa kaṇṇe kaṇṇimittuk kāṇpārtam kaṇṇeṉṉa kaṇṇē”
eṉṟu ittaṉmiyākavum:
kuṉṟak kuraviyil kuṟavarkaḷ murukavēḷip pāṭu miṭattu, 
“	cīrkeḻu centilum ceṅkōṭum veṇkuṉṟum ērakamum nīṅkā iṟivaṉki vēlaṉṟē pārirum puvatti ṉuḷpukkup paṇṭorunāḷ cūrmā taṭinta cuṭariliya veḷvēlē”
eṉṟu iṉṉaṇamum; avaravar uṇarcciyuṭaṉ kalantu ellāt teyvaṅkaḷiyum vāyāra maṉamāra vaḻuttuvārāyiṉar.
iṉi, civapirāṉ tirukkōyil mutalāka muṉ kūṟip pōnta kōyilkaḷaṉṟi, mācāttaṉ, ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ eṉṉum teyvaṅkaṭkum, tirukkili, cevvēḷiṉ kivēl mutaliya vaṟṟukkum taṉittaṉiyāka iyaṟṟappeṟṟa kōyilkaḷ annāḷilē nilaviṉaveṉka.

20. iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum iyaṟkippuṉivum
ulakamum ulakattil uḷḷaṉavumākiya poruḷkaḷi yellām iyaṟkip poruḷ, ceyaṟkip poruḷ eṉa iruvakiyākap pākupāṭu ceytal kūṭum. nam camaya nūlkaḷiṉ vaḻi niṉṟu nōkkiṉ, aimpūtaṅkaḷum iyaṟkip poru ḷeṉavum, avaṟṟiṉ kalappālākiya ulakamum ulakattup poruḷkaḷum ceyaṟkip poruḷeṉavum tōṉṟum.
“	nilantī nīrvaḷi vicumpō ṭintum kalanta mayakkam ulaka mātaliṉ”
eṉa āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉārum kūṟiṉar. iṉṉum nuṇṇitiṉ nōkkiṉ māyiyiṉ kāriyamākum aimpūtam uḷḷiṭṭa aṉittumē ceyaṟkip poruḷeṉal tōṉṟum. navīṉa aṟiviyaṟkalikaḷeṉappaṭum caṭatattuva nūlkaḷ kūṟum iyaṟkip poruḷ ceyaṟkip poruḷ eṉpaṉa vēṟu vakiyiṉa. ivviru vakiyiṉum vēṟupaṭṭaṉa kavivāṇar koḷḷum iyaṟkip poruḷ ceyaṟkip poruḷ eṉpaṉa,
kaviñarulakilē, makkaḷāl iyaṟṟappaṭātu kaṭavuḷāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭaṉavō, iyaṟkiyil amintiruppaṉavō ākiya mali, kaṭal, nāṭu, kāṭu, eṉpaṉavum, avaṟṟiṭi iyalpil uṇṭāvaṉavum ākiyavi iyaṟkip poruḷ eṉavum makkaḷāl iyaṟṟappaṭum aṟucuviyuṇṭi, eḻunili māṭam, paṭṭāṭi, maṇikkalaṉ, mutaliya yāvum ceyaṟkip poruḷ eṉavum koḷḷappaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatākiya iyaṟkip poruḷiyē Nature eṉṟu  āṅkilattiṟ kūṟuvar. makkaḷ tam aṟivāṟṟalkaḷāl evvaḷavu ariya poruḷkaḷi ākkik koḷḷiṉum aviyellām iyaṟkip poruḷkaḷpōl uṭal vaḷamum maṉa makiḻvum taraṟpālaṉavalla. ivvuṇmiyi aṟintē nam muṉṉōrkaḷ tam vāḻkkiyip peritum iyaṟkiyi oṭṭi naṭatti vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. paṇṭik kālattilirunta tamiḻp pulavarkaḷ iyaṟkiyiṉ perumiyi naṉkaṟintavarkaḷ eṉpataṟkut tamiḻiṉ tiṇi vakuppukaḷē cāṉṟākum.
maliyum ataṉic cārnta iṭamum kuṟiñciyeṉavum, puṟavu allatu kāṭu mulliyeṉavum, nīr vaḷamuṭiya paḻaṉam mutaliyaṉa marutam eṉavum, kaṭal cārnta nilam neytal eṉavum tamiḻilakkaṇam kūṟum. vaḷañciṟitumillāta vaṟaṇṭa curam pāli yeṉappaṭum. avvan nilattil uṟiyum makkaḷ, talimakkaḷ, avarkaḷ vaḻipaṭum teyvam, avarkaṭkuriya uṇavu, toḻil, yāḻ, aṅkuḷḷa vilaṅku, paṟavi,maram,pū mutaliyaṉavellām karupporuḷ eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum iṉṉa nilattil iṉṉavi uḷavākum eṉṉum iyaṟki yuṇmi yip paḻantamiḻp pulavarkaḷ aṟintāṟ pōla vēṟu yārum aṟintilareṉṟē kūṟalām. caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷākiya eṭṭut toki pattup pāṭṭu eṉpavaṟṟi iyaṟkiyiṉ ōviyaṅkaḷ eṉṉalām. avaṟṟiluḷḷa ovvoru pāṭṭum iyaṟkip poruḷkaḷi nam akak kaṇmuṉ tōṟṟuvittu iṉpam viḷikkiṉṟaṉa. utāraṇamāka oṉṟu kāṭṭutum:
‘kuṟiñci nilattiluḷḷa oru taliviyum tōḻiyum, tiṉippuṉam kāttaṟkuc ceṉṟu, uyarnta marattiṉmītu  iṭappaṭṭirukkum paraṇiṉ kaṇ ēṟiyiruntu, taṭṭi. mutaliya karuvikaḷāl kiḷikaḷi yōṭṭukiṉṟaṉar. ñāyiṟu kāyum naṇpakaṟ poḻutil paṟavikaḷ tattam cēkkikaḷi aṭi kiṉṟaṉa. appoḻutu avviruvarum aṅku niṉṟakaṉṟu, maliyucciyiṉiṉṟum tūya veḷḷāṭi pōṟ parantu iḻiyum aruviyil nīrāṭukiṉṟaṉar. paḷiṅkik karittu vittāṟ pōṉṟa kaṉikaḷilē iṉiya pala pāṭṭukkaḷip pāṭik koṇṭu kuṭintu kuṭintu viḷiyāṭukiṉṟaṉar; piṉ kariyēṟi, poṉṉilē nīlamaṇiyip patittu vittāṟ pōl mutukilē tāḻnta piṉṉalākiya kūntalip piḻintu īram pularttiviṭṭu, civanta kaṇkaḷuṭaṉ pala pakkamum tirintu malar koykiṉṟaṉar. ceṅkāntaḷ, cuṉikkuvaḷi, kuṟiñci, veṭci, eruvi, kuravam, ceruviṉi, karuviṉi, kōṅkam, pōṅkam, cerunti, karanti, tilli, mulli, pātiri, caṇpakam, mutaliya nūṟuvakiyāṉa pūkkaḷip paṟittu vantu akaṉṟa pāṟiyilē kuvittu, avaṟṟāl taḻi eṉṉum uṭiyiṉic ceytu iṭiyil uṭuttiyum, palvēṟu uruviṉiyuṭiya māli kaḷip puṉintu cūṭiyum,. neruppeṉa viḷaṅkun taḷirkaḷi yuṭiya acōka marattiṉ kuḷirnta niḻalil iṉitu vīṟṟiruk kiṉṟaṉar.’ ivviyaṟkip puṉivu kapilar pāṭiya kuṟiñcip pāṭṭil uḷḷatu. kōtāvariyāṟṟukkuc cāṉṟōr kaviyiṉi uvami kūṟiya kampar iraṇṭaṟkum potuvāka, “aintiṇi neṟiyaḷāvi” eṉṟurittār. eṉavē kavitikaṭku vaṉapput taruvaṉa aintiṇip pāṟpaṭum iyaṟkip poruḷkaḷām eṉpatu pōtarum.
iṉi, peruṅ kāppiyaṅkaṭku ivviyaṟkip puṉivukaḷ iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa veṉpatu, “malikaṭal nāṭu vaḷanakar paruvam, irucuṭart tōṟṟa meṉṟiṉiyaṉa puṉintu” eṉa ataṟkilakkaṇaṅ kūṟuvataṉāṉ aṟiyappaṭum. tamiḻiluḷḷa peruṅkāppiyaṅkaḷil kālattāl muṟpaṭṭatum, tamiḻ moḻiyiṉ iyalpiyum tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ perumiyiyum aṟitaṟkuk karuviyāyiruppatum muttamiḻk kāppiyamākiya cilappatikāram ākum. ataṉi iyaṟṟiyavar ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ eṉṉum cēra vēntaṟkut tampiyārum tuṟavu pūṇṭirunta varumākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ āvar. muttamiḻt tuṟiyiṉ muṟi pōkiya uttamak kaviyākum iḷaṅkōviṉ kāppiyat til ettaṉiyō pala vittaka cittiraṅkaḷ viyappum uvappum viḷikka vallaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ iyaṟkip poruḷiṉ puṉivāka vuḷḷa cilavaṟṟi īṇṭu nōkkutum.
cilappatikārattiṉ muppatu kātikaḷil ‘nāṭu kāṇ kāti’ eṉpatoṉṟu. kōvalaṉum kaṇṇakiyum kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattiliruntu puṟappaṭṭuk kāviriyiṉ vaṭakari vaḻiyākak kuṭatici nōkki vantu tiruvaraṅkattiṟku etirē oṭam ēṟit teṉkariyeyti uriyūri aṭintaṉar eṉṉum in nikaḻcciyit terivikkum pakutikku nāṭu kāṇ kāti eṉṟu peyariṭṭuḷḷa nuṭpam aṟintu makiḻtaṟkuriyatu.
“	nāṭeṉpa nāṭā vaḷattaṉa nāṭalla nāṭa vaḷantaru nāṭu”
