eṉpatu teyvappulavariṉ meyyuriyaṉṟō? vaḷam mikka nilappakutiyi āḷutaliṉālēyē vaḷavar eṉap peyar peṟṟa cōḻaratu nāṭṭiṉ kaṇ avvaḷattiṟkuk kāraṇamākiya kāviriyiṉ maruṅkuḷḷa nilam ettuṇi vaḷamikkatākal vēṇṭum? avvaḷamellām avarkaṭkuk kāṭcip poruḷāki iṉpam viḷittaṉa eṉṉuṅ karuttut tōṉṟavē aṭikaḷ ‘nāṭukaṭanta kāti’ eṉṉātu ‘nāṭukāṇ kāti’ eṉap peyariṭṭār eṉka. kōvalaṉ kaṇṇaki iruvarum camaṇ camayattavamutiyōḷākiya kavunti irukkumiṭam aṭintaṉar. kavuntiyaṭikaḷum avar kaḷuṭaṉ maturikkuvara oruppaṭṭu kāvirimaruṅkuḷḷa en neṟiyil evvāṟu tām cella vēṇṭumeṉpati avar kaṭkuk kūṟukiṉṟavar, “veyiliṉ vemmiyip poṟukka lāṟṟāta meṉmiyiyuṭiya kaṇṇakiyip pūkkaḷ niṟinta cōlikaḷiṉ vaḻiyē aḻittuc celvēmāyiṉ kiḻaṅkukaḷ tōṇṭiyeṭutta kuḻikaḷic caṇpakattiṉ pūntukaḷum, paḻam pūkkaḷum viḻuntu mūṭi poykkuḻippaṭuttalāl avaṟṟi aṟintu vilakic cellātavarkaṭku avi mikka tuṉpattiṉit tarā niṟkum. avvāṟu vilakic cellum poḻutu mēlē toṅkum palāppaḻam muṭṭiṉum muṭṭum. mañcaḷum iñciyum oṉṟōṭoṉṟu piṉṉikkiṭakkum pāttikaḷiluḷḷa palāviṉ vittukkaḷ paralpōlat tuṉpuṟuttaluṅkūṭum. taṇṭali vaḻiyiṟcellātu vayal vaḻiyē pōvōm eṉṟāl, āṅkaṇ poykiyiluḷḷa kayal mīṉkaḷi ōṭṭi nīrnāy kavviya vāḷikaḷ malaṅkukaḷ piṟaḻum ceṟuvilē kuṟukkākap pāyiṉ ivaḷ kalaṅkaluṅkūṭum. vayalum cōliyumallatu vēṟākiya vaḻiyumilli; ākaliṉ avvaḻikaḷil kaṇṇakikku iṉṉal uṇṭākātu pātukāttu varuka” eṉṟu kūṟi avarum uṭaṉ ceṉṟaṉar. mūvarum celvuḻi., kāviriyiṉ nīr vāyttalik kiṭṭa kataviṉ mīteḻuntu kutikkum oliyaṉṟi, āmpi, kiḻār, ēttam mutaliya nīr iṟikkum poṟikaḷ olittal illāta kaḻaṉikaḷilē cennelum karumpum cūḻnta paḻaṉaṅkaḷiluḷḷa tāmarip pūkkaḷākiya kāṭṭil,
“	kampuṭ kōḻiyum kaṉikural nāriyum, ceṅkāl aṉṉamum piṅkāṟ kokkum,  kāṉak kōḻiyum, nīr niṟakkākkiyum, uḷḷum ūralum puḷḷum putāvum”
eṉṉum paṟavikaḷ vēntariruvar pōr ceyyumiṭattil aṇivakuttu niṟkum paṭikaḷ pōl niriniriyāyiruntu eḻuppum palvēṟu vakiyāṉa olikaḷum eḻukiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟum, cēṟṟiṉiṟ puraṇṭu kaḻuvappaṭāta uṭampiṉiyuṭiya erumi kaḷ tiṉavuṭiya mutukiṉit tēyttalāl purinetiḻntu neṟ kūṭṭiluḷḷa palavaki neṟkaḷum arukē kaḻaṉikaḷiṟ kavaripōṟ kāytta cen neṟkatirkaḷiṉ mītē coriyā niṟka ataṉik kaṇṭa viṉiñarum, kaḷamarum,oruṅku kūṭi ārkkum oli orupuṟamum, kaṭiciyar tāmari āmpal mutaliya kaḷi kaḷip paṟit teṟintu nāṟṟiṉi naṭṭut tōḷum mārpum cēṟāṭiya kōlattuṭaṉ vīṟupeṟat tōṉṟi matuvuṇṭu pāṭum pāṭṭukkaḷiṉ oli oru puṟamum, aṟukiyum kuvaḷi malariyum katiruṭaṉ kalantu toṭutta māliyi, mēḻiyilē cūṭṭi nilattiṉip piḷappavar pōl poṉṉēr pūṭṭi niṉṟōr pāṭum ēr maṅkalappāṭṭiṉ oci oru puṟamum, neṟ katiri arintu pōriṭṭōr ataṉik kaṭāviṭum poḻutu pāṭum mukavip pāṭṭiṉ oci oru puṟamum eḻāniṉṟaṉa, kāviriyiṉ aṭi kariyiṟ cellum kōvalaṉum, kaṇṇa kiyum ippalavaki yolikaḷiyum muṟiyiṟkēṭṭu maṉaṅ kaḷittalāl vaḻi naṭattaliṉuṇṭām varuttatti neñciṟ koḷḷātu celvārāyiṉar. cōṇāṭṭiṉ iyaṟki vaḷattiṉi iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ ivvāṟu niralpaṭak kūṟi yirukkun tiṟaṉ kaṟpārkkuk kaḻipēṟuvaki payappatākum.
iṉi, ivarkaḷ viyi yāṟṟik kaṭantu maturikkuc cellutal kūṟum poḻutu iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ viyiyi ōr naṅkiyāka uruvakappaṭutti ‘pulavar nāviṟ poruntiya pūṅkoṭi’ eṉavum, ‘viyiyeṉṟa poyyāk kulakkoṭi’ eṉavum arumi pārāṭṭukiṉṟavar, ataṟkēṟpa, ataṉic cārntuḷḷa iyaṟkip poruḷkaḷi an naṅkiyiṉ aṅkaṅkaḷākap puṉinturikkiṉṟaṉar. viyiyiṉ kari maruṅkulāka, ataṉ puṟavāyil aṭarnta kuravam, vakuḷam, kōṅkam, vēṅki, maravam, nākam, tilakam, marutam, cēṭal, cerunti,ceṇpakam, pātiriyākiya maraṅkaḷ malar virintu  viḷaṅkuvaṉa pūntukilākavum, ataṉ akavāyiluḷḷa kuruku, taḷavam, mucuṇṭi, atiral,kūtāḷam, kuṭacam, vetiram, pakaṉṟi, piṭavam, mayili eṉṉum tūṟukaḷum, koṭikaḷum malarntu piṉṉikkiṭappaṉa mēkaliyākavum aḻaku cey kiṉṟaṉavām! viyiyāṟu muḻutum nīr puṟantōṉṟāmal malarkaḷāl mūṭappaṭṭiruppatu avaḷ kaṇṇakikku mēl varuntuṉpatti aṟintu ‘puṇṇiya naṟumalar āṭi pōrttuk, kaṇṇiṟi neṭunīr karantaṭakkiyatu’ pōlkiṉṟa tām! ivaṟṟiliruntu iyaṟkip poruḷkaḷiṉ kāṭciyil iḷaṅkōviṉ uḷḷam evvāṟu īṭupaṭṭirunta teṉpatum tām kaṇṭu nukarnta apporuḷkaḷik katiyil eṅṅaṉam ēṟṟa peṟṟiyamittuc cuvi yūṭṭukiṉṟaṉareṉpatum pulaṉākiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, cilappatikārattil kāṭcik kāti eṉpa toṉṟuṇṭu. cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ kaṇṇakikkup paṭimañ ceytaṟ poruṭṭu imaya maliyiṟ kaṟkoḷḷak karutiṉaṉ eṉpatu akkātiyiṉ muṭinta poruḷāyiṉum, kuṟiñci nilattiṉ iyaṟki vaḷaṅkaḷi avaṉ kaṇṭu kaḷittami ataṉ muṟpakutiyākavuḷḷatu. ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ mañcucūḻ cōli malivaḷaṅ kāṇṭaṟ poruṭṭāka taṉ tēviyākiya vēṇ māḷuṭaṉum, viḷiyāṭṭiṟkuriya palavakip poruḷuṅ koṇṭu tāṉikaḷ cūḻa vañci nakariṉiṉṟum puṟappaṭṭuc ceṉṟu pērāṟṟiṉ kariyi aṭikiṉṟāṉ. avviyāṟāṉatu ‘kōṅkam vēṅki tūṅkiṇark koṉṟi, nākam tilakam naṟuṅkāḻāram’ ākiya palavaṟṟiṉiṉṟum utirnta pūkkaḷiṉ parappiṉāl puṉal tōṉṟātu maṟiya, neṭiyōṉ mārpil ārampōṉṟu periya maliyik kuṟukkiṭṭuc cellā niṉṟatu. ataṉ aṭikariyiluḷḷa maṇal ekkaril aracaṉ mutalāṉōr ceṉṟu oruṅkirukkiṉṟaṉar. aṅkē kuṉṟak kuṟavarkaḷiṉ kuraviyiṟkoṭicciyar pāṭum pāṭṭiṉō ciyum, cevvēḷip pūcikkum vēlaṉatu pāṭṭiṉ ociyum, tiṉi kuṟṟuvōratu vaḷḷip pāṭṭōciyum puṉattilē kiḷiyōṭṭutal mutaliya vaṟṟāṉeḻum ōciyum, iṟāli ṉiṉṟum tēṉeṭukkum kuṟavarōciyum muḻaveṉa olikkum aruviyiṉ ōciyum, puliyoṭu pōr ceyyum yāṉiyiṉ ociyum,  paraṇiṉ mēluḷḷavaṉ vilaṅkiṉik kaṭintōṭṭum ōciyum, kuḻiyil viḻukiṉṟa yāṉiyiṉ pākar ceyyum ōciyum, iyaṅkukiṉṟa paṭikaḷiṉ ōciyōṭu oruṅkeḻuvaṉa vāyiṉa. avvōci yiṉpatti nukarntirukkum poḻutu vēṅki marattiṉ niḻalil kaṇṇaki kōvalaṉōṭu tēva vimāṉattil ēṟic ceṉṟa aticayattik kaṇṭa kuṉṟak kuṟavar kaḷ ataṉit taṅkaḷ malināṭṭukku vēntaṉākiya ceṅkuṭṭu vaṟku aṟivikkak karutip pala kāṇikkip poruḷkaḷōṭum vantu aracaṉik kaṇṭu terivikkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ koṇarnta poruḷkaḷ iviyeṉak kūṟumvāyilāka iḷaṅ kōvaṭikaḷ malivaḷamaṉittiyum oruṅku tokuttuk kāṭṭuvatu iyaṟkiyōṭu avar evvaḷavu payiṉṟuḷḷār eṉpataṉip pulappaṭuttum. “yāṉi veṇkōṭum akiliṉ kuppiyum, māṉ mayirkkavariyum, matuviṉ kuṭaṅkaḷum, cantaṉak kuṟiyum cinturakkaṭṭiyum, añcaṉat tiraḷum, aṇiyari tāramum, ēlavalliyum iruṅkaṟivalliyum, kūvi nūṟum, koḻuṅkoṭik kavaliyum, teṅkiṉ paḻaṉum tēmāṅkaṉiyum, piṅkoṭip paṭaliyum, palaviṉ paḻaṅkaḷum, kāyamum karumpum pū malikoṭiyum, koḻuntāṭkamukiṉ ceḻuṅkulit tāṟum, peruṅkuli vāḻiyiṉ iruṅkaṉittāṟum, āḷiyiṉ aṇiṅkum, ariyiṉ kuruḷiyum, vāḷvarip paṟaḻum, matakarik kaḷapamum, kuraṅkiṉ kuṭṭiyum kuṭāvaṭi yuḷiyamum, variyāṭu varuṭiyum maṭamāṉ maṟiyum, kācaṟik karuvum mācaṟu nakulamum, pīli maññiyum nāviyiṉ piḷḷiyum, kāṉakkōḻiyum tēmoḻikkiḷḷiyum” ākiya ivaṟṟi malivāṇar taliyiṟ cumantu vantu vittu “ēḻpiṟappaṭiyēm, vāḻka niṉ koṟṟam” eṉa vāḻtti, piṉpu, kaṇṇaki vāṉuṟṟa ceytiyik kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
aimpulaṉkaḷukkum iṉpam viḷippaṉavākiya mali paṭuporuḷ aṉittiyum, ōr kāṭci maṉṟattil tokuttu vippatu pōl iṅkeṭuttiyampiyuḷḷa itu malināṭṭu maṉṉaṉ mintarākiya avar maliyiṉ iyaṟki vaḷaṅkaḷi evvaḷavu naṉkaṟintuḷḷār eṉpataṉip pulappaṭuttukiṉṟatu.
10.10.1941 aṉṟu tirucci vāṉoliyil pēciya pēccu.

21. kalittoki māṇpu
ulakiṉ kaṇ ellā uyirkaḷum iṉpatti viḻi kiṉṟaṉa. eṉiṉum, makkaḷoḻinta ēṉiya uyirkaḷ yāvum uṇavum iṇiviḻiccum mutaliyavaṟṟāl eṉṟum oru peṟṟiyē ciṟucciṟu iṉpantuyttuvarutalaṉṟi, avviṉ pattip perukkavum. ēṉit tuṉpattit tavirkkavum vaki yaṟiyātaṉavā yuḷḷaṉa. makkaḷ maṭṭumē tam pakuttaṟivu koṇṭu muyaṉṟu poruḷīṭṭiyum aṟam purintum, avi cārpāka ivvulakil nukarum iṉpatti mēṉmēṟ perukac ceytaṟkum, maṟumiyiṉpiṉiyum eṉṟum niliyāya vīṭṭiṉpiṉiyum eytutaṟkum uriyarākiṉṟaṉar. eṉavē makkaḷāleytalākum pēṟukaḷ iṉpam,poruḷ, aṟam, vīṭu eṉṉum nāṉkumātal peṟṟām. īṇṭu iṉpam eṉṟatu immiyiṉpamākum. in nāṉkiṉiyum akam,puṟam eṉa iruvakippaṭutturippatu tamiḻ nūṉ muṟi. akapporuḷāvatu iṉpam. ēṉiya puṟapporuḷ eṉappaṭum. 
im malartali yulakil makkaḷ tuykkum iṉpam pala tiṟatta vāyiṉum, avi palavaṟṟuḷḷum, ulaka vaḷarccikkum, uyirkaḷiṉ malarccikkum iṉṟiyamiyātatum, oru kālattoru poruḷāl aimpulaṉum nukartaṟ ciṟappuṭiyatumākiya kāmaviṉpamē nūlōrāl iṉpameṉa vittup pōṟṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. otta aṉpuṭiya oruvaṉum oruttiyum kūṭukiṉṟa kālattup piṟanta pēriṉpam, akkūṭṭattiṉ piṉṉar oruvark koruvar pulaṉāka ivvāṟiruntateṉak kūṟappaṭātu yāṇṭum uḷḷattuṇarvē nukarntu iṉpamuṟuva toṉṟākaliṉ, aḵtu akapporuḷ eṉappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. ivviṉpattiṉi ettuṇiyum viḻumi tākkutaṟkut tolli nallāciriyaṉmār kaṇṭa vittakat tiṟaṅkaḷ pala. avi aṉittum ‘pulaṉeṟi vaḻakku’ eṉpatil aṭaṅkum. 
pulaṉeṟivaḻakkāvatu, ulakattu āṭavarum makaḷirum ulakiyalāṟ kūṭiyoḻukum ōḻukkattuṭaṉ nāṭaka vaḻakkāya puṉinturiyum cērttup pulavarāyiṉār  ceppañ ceytamittatōr tippiya neṟi. pulaneṟi vaḻakkilē otta aṉpuṭiya talivaṉun taliviyuṅ kūṭiyoḻukum ōḻukkam ‘kaḷavu’ eṉavum ‘kaṟpu’ eṉavum iruvakippaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ kaḷavāvatu uruvum, tiruvum, paruvamum, kulaṉum, kuṇaṉum, aṉpum mutaliyavaṟṟāl otta talimakaṉum, talimakaḷum aṭuppāruṅ koṭuppārumiṉṟip pālvakiyāl tāmē etirppaṭṭuk kūṭiyoḻukuvatu. kaṟpāvatu avviruvarum muṟippaṭi varintu koṇṭu illiliruntu aṟampurin toḻukuvatu. ivvirutiṟattum talimakkaḷ ōḻukkam puṇartal, pirital, iruttal, iraṅkal, ūṭal eṉa aivakippaṭṭu nikaḻumeṉpatum., avi muṟiyē kuṟiñci, pāli, mulli, neytal, marutam eṉṉum aintiṇikkum uriyavāmeṉpatum,. akattiṇikaḷ mutal, karu, uri eṉap pākupāṭeytumeṉpatum, kaḷavoḻukkamāṉatu iyaṟkippuṇarcci, iṭantalippāṭu,. pāṅkaṟkūṭṭam, tōḻiyiṟ kūṭṭameṉa nāl vakiyāṉ nikaḻumeṉpatum, kaḷaviṉkaṇ talivi tōḻikkum, tōḻi cevilikkum eṉṉum immuṟiyāṉē aṟattoṭu nili nikaḻumeṉpatum, iru kikōḷiṉum kūṟṟu nikaḻttu taṟkuriyār iṉṉiṉṉār eṉpatum, avar iṉṉiṉṉa neṟip pāṭuṭiyar eṉpatum, talivaṟku .ivvivvētuvāṟ pirivu nikaḻum eṉpatum, avaṉ mīṇṭu varum poḻutum muṟimiyum ivvivi eṉpatum, piṟavum pulaṉeṟivaḻakkiṭik kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. 
‘iṟiyaṉā rakapporuḷ’ uriyāciriyar itaṉi ‘illatu iṉiyatu nallatu eṉṟu pulavarāl nāṭṭappaṭṭatō roḻukkam’ eṉṟē palaviṭattum vaṟpuṟuttic collā niṟpar. eṉiṉum ulakiyalum puṉinturiyum viraviyatē pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkāmeṉal āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉāriṉ karutteṉpatu,
“	nāṭaka vaḻakkiṉum ulakiyal vaḻakkiṉum pāṭal cāṉṟa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam”
eṉpataṉāṟ peṟappaṭum. itaṉ uriyil āciriyar nacciṉārk kiṉiyar, “puṉinturi vakiyiṟ kūṟupaveṉṟaliṉ pulavar illaṉavum kūṟupavālōveṉiṉ, ulakattōrkku naṉmi payattaṟku nallōrkkuḷḷaṉavaṟṟi oḻintōr aṟin toḻukal aṟameṉak karuti, annallōrkkuḷḷaṉavaṟṟiṟ ciṟitillaṉavum kūṟutaliṉṟi, yāṇṭum eññāṉṟum illaṉa kūṟār” eṉṟurippatu cintikkaṟpālatu. 
ivaṟṟiliruntu, kaḷavuk kūṭṭameṉpatu tamiḻnāṭṭu aintiṇi makkaḷiṭam iyalpāka nikaḻntu vanta toṉṟeṉ patum, avarkaḷ mutaṟkaṇ kaḷavil aḷavaḷāviyiruntu piṉ varintu koḷḷum oḻukalāṟuṭiyar eṉpatum, ivaṟṟi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu nallicippulavarkaḷ cuvi payappaṉavākiya karuttukkaḷ palavaṟṟit tokuttup pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṉi amittuḷḷāreṉpatum karutaṟpālavākiṉṟaṉa. malināṭṭu mutuvar mutalāyiṉārpāl iññāṉṟum kāṇappaṭum oḻukalāṟukaḷ ikkaruttiṉi valiyuṟuttuvaṉavākum. kaḷavuk kūṭṭam pulaṉeṟiyaṉṟi ulakiyalāṉum aintiṇi makkaḷiṭi nikaḻtaluṉṭeṉṉuṅ karuttiṉar nacciṉārkiṉiyareṉpatu,
“	mutaloṭu puṇarnta yāḻōr mēṉa tavalaruñ ciṟappi ṉinnilam peṟumē”
eṉṉum kaḷaviyaṟ cūttirattiṟku avaruritta uriyāl aṟiyappaṭum. malivāṇariṭam ivvoḻukkam peru vaḻakka māyiṉami koṇṭē tollāciriyaṉmār kuṟiñcikkaṇ puṇarcciyi amittaṉar pōlum. eṉiṉum kūṭutal mutaliya uripporuḷkaṭku nilamum kālamum variyaṟi cey tiruppatil nāṭaka vaḻakku iyintuḷḷami tēṟṟam.
ivvāṟu pulaṉeṟiyiṟ puṉinturikaḷ pukuttap paṭṭamiyiṉ nōkkam talimakkaḷiṉ akavuṇarcciyākiya aṉpiṉip palavāṟum veḷippaṭuttutalē yātal vēṇṭum.
aintiṇi yoḻukkamāṉatu aṉpiṉ pālateṉpatu,
“aṉpoṭu puṇarnta aintiṇi maruṅkiṟ kāmak kūṭṭaṅ kāṇuṅ kāli”
eṉat tolkāppiyamum,
“aṉpi ṉi ntiṇi k kaḷaveṉap paṭuvatu”
eṉavum,
“	kaḷavu kaṟpeṉak kaṇṇiya īṇṭiyōr uḷanika ḻaṉpiṉ uyarcci mēṉa”
eṉavum iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyalum kūṟutalāṟ pōtarum.
pulaṉeṟivaḻakkiṭik kāṇappaṭum talimakkaḷiṉ viyakkattakka aṉpiṉ tiṟaṉum, taliviyatu kaṟpiṉ tiṇmiyum mutalāya aka vuṇarcci mēmpāṭellām tamiḻ naṉmakkaḷiṉ ulakiyalāṉa uṇarvoḻukkavuyarcciyiṉiṉṟum utittaṉavē yākum. pulaṉeṟivaḻakkiṉi yātta tamiḻppulavarkaḷiṉ uḷḷattē malarnta avvuyar paṇpukaḷ ēṉit tamiḻ makkaḷ akattil mukiḻttila eṉal yāṅṅaṉam amiyum? kaḷa voḻukka māṉatu āriya makkaḷiṉ eṇvaki maṉṟaluḷ oṉṟāya yāḻōr kūṭṭatti oppatu eṉak kūṟappaṭuvatu, iruvar tamiyarāy etirppaṭṭuk kūṭutalākiya oru puṭi oppumi karutiyē yākum. yāḻōr kūṭṭattil piṉ kaṟpu nikaḻtal orutali yaṉṟu. kaḷavuk kūṭṭamō kaṟpiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāmiyi yuṭiyatu. maṟṟum, pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṟ palavāṟum pukaḻnturittaṟkēṟṟa uḻuvalaṉpiṉ viḻumiya tiṟaṉellām kaḷavukkē ciṟappurimi yuṭiyaṉa. ‘coṟpāl amutivaḷ yāṉ cuviyeṉṉa’ eṉṉum tirucciṟṟampalak kōvic ceyyuḷum, ataṟkup pērāciriyar eḻutiya uriyum avvaṉpiṉ ciṟappiṉi naṉku viḷakkutal kāṇka. makaḷirkku uyiriṉum nāṇ ciṟantateṉpatum ataṉiṉuṅ kaṟpuc ciṟanta teṉpatum kaḷaviyaliṉ teḷiporuḷāka uḷḷaṉa. ittakiya nāṇuṅ kaṟpum pēṇu maṭavār iññāṉṟum uḷarātal kaṇkūṭākaliṉ ivi ulakiyalātal illeṉal cālātu.
“	kuṭimutaṟ cuṟṟamum kuṟṟiḷi yōrum aṭiyōr pāṅkum āyamum nīṅki nāṇamum maṭaṉum nallō rēttum pēṇiya kaṟpum peruntuṇi yāka eṉṉoṭu pōntīṅ keṉṟuyar kaḷinta poṉṉē koṭiyē puṉipūṅ kōtāy nāṇiṉ pāvāy nī ṇila viḷakkē kaṟpiṉ koḻuntē poṟpiṉ celvi”
eṉṟu kōvalaṉ kūṟuvaṉa kaṇṇakiyiṉ uṇmip peṇmi nalaṅkaḷē yaṉṟō? iṅṅaṉam kāntaruvattiṉum ciṟanta nalaṅkaḷōṭu tamiḻariṉ kaḷavu muṟi tikaḻntamiyāṟṟāṉ itaṉit tamiḻakkē uriyateṉac cāṉṟōr palarum kūṟuvārāyiṉareṉka.
paripāṭalil kuṉṟampūtaṉār pāṭiya oṉpatāñ ceyyuḷil 
“	nāṉmaṟi virittu nallici viḷakkum vāymoḻip pulavīr kēṇmiṉ”
eṉa nāṉmaṟip pulavari viḷittuk kaḷavu, kaṟpu eṉpavaṟṟiṉ iyalpiṉiyum, avaṟṟuḷ meyyuṟṟu aṟiyātār iruvar aṉpottup pāṉmi vakiyāl tāmē meyyuṟṟuk kūṭum kātaṟkūṭṭamākiya kaḷavē ciṟanta teṉpataṉiyuṅ kūṟi vantu, itaṉi vēṇṭum poruḷilakkaṇattiyuṭiya tamiḻi āyātārkku itaṉ ciṟappu viḷaṅkā teṉpār,
“taḷḷāp poruḷiyalpiṟ ṟaṇṭamiḻ āyvantilār koḷḷārik kuṉṟu payaṉ”
eṉṟu kūṟi, itu tamiḻukkē uriyatāmāṟum, ataṉāl tamiḻ ciṟantatāmāṟum valiyuṟutti urittiṭṭami kāṇṭum.
maṟṟum, patumiyōṭuṅ kaḷaviṟ kūṭṭam nikaḻtta veṇṇiya cīvakaṉ,
“	tēvar paṇṇiya tī toṭi yiṉcuvi mēvar teṉṟamiḻ meypporu ḷātaliṉ”
eṉa ataṉit teṉṟamiḻiṉ meypporuḷāmeṉak koṇṭāṉ eṉṟum, curamañcariyum cīvakaṉum kaḷaviṟ kūṭṭam nikaḻttiṉamiyi, “iṉṟamiḻiyaṟkiyiṉpam nilipeṟa neṟiyiṟṟuyttār” eṉṟum tiruttakkatēvar kūṟiyuḷḷamiyum itu tamiḻiyalā meṉpataṉi valiyuṟuttāniṟkum. nacciṉārk kiṉiyar ivaṟṟic cuṭṭi oruvar peyar koḷḷap paṭutaliṉ ‘ulakiyal vaḻakkāṉa kāntaruvam’eṉpar.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl aintiṇip pāṟpaṭum aṉpiṉ viḷivākiya akap poruḷiṉ ciṟappu iṉitu pulaṉākā niṟkum. āyiṉ ulakattil iruvariruvarāy iṉpan tuykkum ellā makkaḷum,
“	piṟappē kuṭimi yāṇmi yāṇṭō ṭuruvu niṟutta kāmavāyil niṟiyē yaruḷē uṇarvoṭu tiruveṉa muṟiyuṟak kiḷanta oppiṉatu vakiyē.”
eṉat tolkāppiyar kūṟiya oppumi palavumuṭiyarāta lilli. aṭiyōrum viṉiceyvārum mutalāyiṉār tamakkuriyaraṉṟip piṟar ēviya vaḻi oḻukuvōrātaliṉ avaratu aṉpiṉ niliyum. iṉpamum kuṟipāṭuḷḷaṉavēyākum. ākaliṉ avaruṭiya iṉpa oḻukkaṅkaḷ talimippāṭuṭiya naṉ makkaḷiṉ aṉpoḻukkaṅkaḷōṭu oru taṉmiyavākā eṉpatu paṟṟip paṇṭit tamiḻc cāṉṟōrkaḷ avaṟṟiṟkuk kikkiḷi, peruntiṇiyeṉap peyar koṭuttu akap puṟameṉa orucār otukkivippārāyiṉar. ēṉi naṉmakkaḷ pāl ulakiyalāl nikaḻum orucār kikkiḷi peruntiṇiyum uḷa. aviyum akappuṟamē yākumeṉka.
iṉi, ittakiya pulaṉeṟivaḻakkup paṟṟiya ceyyuṭkaḷē puṟapporuṭ ceyyuḷiṉum mikutiyākac caṅkattuc cāṉ ṟōrāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. puṟapporuḷ muyaṟciyil uḻantu vāṭṭamuṟum makkaḷ uḷḷattil iṉpunīr pāyccik kiḷarcciyi viḷippataṟku iḵtu ēṟṟa karuviyātal karutip pōlum itaṉi mikuttup pāṭuvārāyiṉar! maṟṟum kāṭcip poruḷum karuttup poruḷumākiya iru vakip poru ḷiyum iṉitamittuk kaṟpār neñcam kāmuṟac ceyyuḷ puṉitaṟku iḵtu ēṟṟa peṟṟiyatātalum karutaṟpāṟṟu.
aintiṇiyiṉ pākupāṭākiya mutal karu urip poruḷ kaḷil mutaṟporuḷākiya nilam poḻutukaḷum avaṟṟic cārntu tōṉṟum karupporuḷākiya vilaṅku, puḷ, maram, pū mutalāyiṉavum, āṇṭu vāḻum makkaḷum avarkaḷ cey toḻil mutalāyiṉavum kāṭcip poruḷām. urip poruḷākiya puṇartal, pirital mutaliyaṉa paṟṟi akattē tōṉṟum uṇarcci vakikaḷ karuttup poruḷām.
“	oppum uruvum veṟuppum eṉṟā kaṟpum ērum eḻilum eṉṟā cāyalum nāṇum maṭaṉum eṉṟā nōyum vēṭkiyum nukarvum eṉṟāṅ kāvayiṉ varuuṅ kiḷavi yellām nāṭṭiya marapiṉ neñkoḷi ṉallatu kāṭṭa lākāp poruḷa veṉpa”
eṉṉum poruḷiyaṟ cūttirattuk kiḷantōtiya oppu mutaliyaṉavum, ‘ellām’ eṉpataṉāl uri yakattē nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya oḷi, aḷi, kāytal, aṉpu, aḻukkāṟu, poṟi, niṟi ,aṟivu mutalāyiṉavum karuttup poruḷāmāṟu orka.
iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷ uriyāciriyarum, “ulakattup poruḷtāṉ iraṇṭu vakiyāṉ uṇarttappaṭum. uṇmi māttiri uṇarttip piḻampu uṇarttappaṭātaṉavum, uṇmiyum piḻampum uṇarttappaṭuvaṉavumeṉa” eṉṟu kūṟi avaṟṟuḷ uṇmi uṇarttip piḻampu uṇarttappaṭātaṉa vaṟṟukkuk kāmam mutaliyavaṟṟiyum, ‘oppum uruvum’ eṉṉum cūttirattuk kūṟappaṭṭavaṟṟiyumē eṭuttuk kāṭṭiṉār. iviyellām piḻampuṇarttappaṭāta vāyiṉum nallicip pulavar tām colluntiṟaṅkaḷāl ivaṟṟi nam neñcilē niṟuttukiṉṟaṉar. poṟikaḷāṟ kāṇṭaṟkuriyavākiya iyaṟkip poruḷkaḷ palavaṟṟiṉ tiṟaṅkaḷiyum nuṉittaṟintu kūṟu vatilum, karuttup poruḷāya aṉpu mutaliya uṇarccikaḷi veḷippaṭuttuvatilum, caṅkap pulavarkaḷiṉ vittakak kavittiṟam ettuṇiyum pārāṭṭaṟkuriyatām.
palvēṟu kālattirunta pulavarkaḷ palarāl pāṭap paṭṭaṉavum kaṭiccaṅkat tiṟuti nāḷiṟ cāṉṟōr cilarāl ovvoru muṟi paṟṟit tokukkappaṭṭaṉavum ākiya eṭṭut tokiyuḷ oṉṟāyatu kalittoki. iḵtu akapporuḷāya pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiyē poruḷāka uṭiyatu; muḻutuṅ kalippāvāl amintatu. āciriyar nallantuvaṉārāl pāli, kuṟiñci, marutam, mulli, neytal eṉṉum immuṟiyē tokuttu niṟuttappeṟṟatu. itil uḷḷa pāli mutaliya aintu pakutikaḷum muṟiyē peruṅkaṭuṅkōṉ, kapilar, marutaṉiḷa nākaṉār, nalluttaraṉ, nallantuvaṉār eṉpōrāṟ pāṭappeṟṟaṉa eṉpatu or veṇpāvāl aṟiyalāvatu. ivvintu pakutikaḷilum pāṇṭiyar kuṭiyum, pāṇṭiyark kuriya pati, yāṟu, malikaḷākiya kūṭal, viyi, potiyil eṉpaṉavum, piṟavum pārāṭṭappaṭṭiruttaliṉ ivaṟṟi iyaṟṟiya āciriyar varalāṟu ārāyntu aṟutiyiṭap peṟal vēṇṭum. iṉi, kalittokiyeṉṉum ivvilakkiyattiṉ ciṟappu aḷaviṭaṟkariya toṉṟākum. pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṉip pāṭutaṟkuc ciṟappurimiyuṭiya ceyyuḷ kaliyē yeṉpatu.
“	nāṭaka vaḻakkiṉum ulakiyal vaḻakkiṉum pāṭal cāṉṟa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam kaliyē paripāṭ ṭāyīru pāṅkiṉum uriya tāku meṉmaṉār pulavar”
eṉṉum tolkāppiyac cūttirattāl aṟiyap paṭum. ceyyuḷiyaliṟ kūṟiya muṟiyaṉṟik kaliyi muṟkūṟiyatu paripāṭṭiṉum atu ciṟantatu eṉpataṉip pulappaṭuttum. kaliyuḷḷum ciṟappuṭiya ottāḻicik kaliyē palavākavum ēṉiya cilavākavum ittokiyil amintuḷḷaṉa. iṉpam paṟṟiya ekkaḷippu allatu maṉaveḻuccikkuk kaliyiṉ tuḷḷalōci porutta muṭittākaliṉ āciriyar pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṟku itaṉiyē ciṟantateṉak koṇṭārātal vēṇṭum. maṟṟum taravu tāḻici mutaliya kaliyuṟuppukkaḷ pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṟ kūṟṟu nikaḻttutaṟ kuriyār oruvar maṟṟoruvarkku oṉṟu kaṭṭurikkumiṭattu akkaruttiṉit tokac colliyum tūvāta nīkki vakutturittu valiyuṟuttiyum, muṭiviṉkaṇ payaṉāl nakac colliyum naṉṟi payattaṟ kēṟṟa vevvēṟu ōciyum amippum uṭiyavākat tikaḻtalum itaṉatu ciṟappurimikku ētuvātal vēṇṭum. 
iṉi, ikkalic ceyyuḷellām caṅku tiraṇṭu muraṉṟeḻum ōcipōlat taḻintu iṉpam payakkum ōci nala muṭiyaṉavāka, avaṟṟil amintuḷḷa poruḷvaḷamum, karuttu māṇpum aḷaviṭaṟ kariya ciṟappiṉavāyt tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa.. ittokiyiṉ kaṇ aintiṇiyiṉ karupporuḷkaḷiṉ iyalkaḷum, āṇṭu vāḻum makkaḷiṉ peṟṟimikaḷum kiḻiyil eḻutikkāṭṭiṉā loppa aḻakuṟatteḷintu tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. itiluḷḷa uvamaṅ kaḷiṉ māṭci yoṉṟē itaṉi iṇiyaṟṟa ciṟappiṉatākkum takutippāṭuṭiyatu. āyiṉ, iṉpam nutali eḻunta itaṉkaṇ pāliyiṉ koṭumi palapaṭiyākak kūṟappaṭutal tuṉpa niṉivi yeḻuppi iṉpa veḻuccikku ūṟu ceyvatākumālō eṉiṉ, ākātu. eṉṉi? talivaṉatu mīṭciyiṉkaṇ avviṉpam muṉṉiyilum ciṟantu tōṉṟu mākaliṉ eṉka. allatūum talimakaṉ taṉ kaṭappāṭukaḷi āṟṟutaṟkaṇ ettakiya tuṉpaṅ kaḷiyum poṟukku miyalpiṉaṉ eṉpataṉi nāṭṭutaṟkum, talivaṉip pirinta taliviyiṉ taliyaṉpu paṟṟiya aka uṇarccikaḷik kāṭṭutaṟkum, talivaṉ pirintu cellum pāliyiṉ koṭumi kūṟal peritum payappāṭuṭiyatātaluṅ kāṇka. taliviyip pirintu vēṟṟunāṭṭakalum taliva ṉoruvaṉ yāṇṭum vemmi mikka āratarattam naṭantu cēṟal ulakiyalil orutaliyaṉṟāyiṉum, muṟkuṟitta payaṉ karutiyē tollāciriyar nāṭaka vaḻakkup paṟṟi itaṉi amittārākal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam iṟappa iṉṉātavaṟṟi urikka vēṇṭiya iṭattum nallicip pulavarkaḷ tam colluntiṟattāl avaṟṟiṟ kiṉimiyūṭṭik kaṟpavar kāmuṟumāṟu ceytuviṭukiṉṟaṉar. 
iṉi, tūyatoru kātaliṉpam tuykkum ivaruḷ talivaṉāṉavaṉ parattiyiṟ pirintoḻukum oḻukkamum iḻivu tarum nīratākavē tōṉṟukiṟatu. iḵtulakiyal pōlap pulaṉāyiṉum nāṭaka vaḻakkup paṟṟiyē pulami mikkār itaṉip pukutturittārātal vēṇṭum. 
iṉi, tūyatoru kātaliṉpam tuykkum iruvaruḷ talivaṉāṉavaṉ parattiyiṟ pirintoḻukum ōḻukkam matikkaṇ maṟuppōlva toṉṟāyt tōṉṟukiṟatu. iv voḻukkamāṉatu pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkiṟ kāṇappaṭuvatu pēruri yāciriyarkaḷiṉ uḷḷattit tikikkac ceytuḷateṉpatu aṉṉōr paṉṉeṟippaṭa itaṟku amiti kūṟuntiṟattāṉ aṟiyappaṭum.
“kātaṟ paratti ellārkku murittē”
eṉṉum iṟiyaṉārakap poruṭ cūttira vuriyil āciriyar nakkīraṉār parattiyiṟ pirivu nikaḻtaṟku ētuvākak kūṟappaṭum pala karuttukkaḷi ārāyntu kaḻittup, puṉinturi vakiyāl or karuttiṉi niṟuvi, iṟutiyil, “parattiyiṟ pirintāṉ talimakaṉ eṉṟāl ūṭalē pulaviyē tuṉiyē eṉṟivi nikaḻum, nikaḻntāl avi nīkkik kūṭiṉaviṭattum periya tōriṉpamāy avviṉpat taṉmiyi veḷippaṭuppaṉa aviyeṉak koḷka” eṉṟu muṭikkiṉṟaṉar. 
tirucciṟṟampalak kōviyāriṉ uriyil pērāciriyar “parattiyiṟ piriveṉpatu talimakaḷi varinteytiya piṉṉar vikalum pālē nukarvāṉoruvaṉ iṭiyē puḷiṅkāṭiyum nukarntu ataṉ iṉimiyi nukarntāṟpōla, avaḷ nukarcciyiṉ iṉimi yaṟitaṟkup puṟappeṇṭir māṭṭup piriyāniṟṟal allatūum paṇṇum pāṭalum mutalāyiṉa kāṭṭip puṟappeṇṭir taṉṉik kātalittāl tāṉ ellārkkum talivaṉātaliṉ avarkkum iṉpañ ceyyap piriyā niṟṟal eṉṟumām. allatūum talimakaḷi ūṭalaṟi taṟkup pirital eṉṟumām” eṉṟu kūṟāniṟpar. iviyellām parattiyiṟ piriveṉpatu nāṭaka vaḻakkup paṟṟi amikkappaṭṭa teṉṉum karuttiṉi, uṭkoṇṭu, aṅṅaṉam amikkappaṭu taṟku ētukkaḷi ārāynta vāṟēyām. variviṉ makaḷiroṭu maruviyoḻukum pūriya voḻukkam celvar cilariṭi ulakiyalāṟ kāṇappaṭiṉum, cīriyarākum talimakkaḷ ataṉi uṭiyarātal orutaliyaṉṟē; ātalāṉ, cila payaṉ karutip pulavarāyiṉār itaṉip puṉinturittaṉareṉṟē kōṭal vēṇṭum. ap payaṉkaḷuḷ, talivaṉ ettuṇik koṭumi ceyyiṉum talivi taṉ kaṟpiṉiṉṟum ciṟitum kalaṅkutal ilaḷāvaḷ eṉpataṉi veḷippaṭuppatē taliyāyatākum. māṇikkavācakap perumāṉ kūṭaliṉāynta tīntamiḻk kaṭaliṟ paṭintu koḻitteṭutta arumpeṟaṉ maṇikaḷuḷ oṉṟāya,
“	vēyātu ceppi ṉaṭittut tamivikum vīyiṉaṉṉa tīyāṭi ciṟṟam palamaṉiyāḷ tilli yūraṉukkiṉ ṟēyāp paḻiyeṉa nāṇiyeṉ kaṇṇiṅṅa ṉēmaṟittāḷ yāyā miyalpivaḷ kaṟpunaṟ pāla viyalpukaḷē”
eṉṉum kōvic ceyyuḷil, talivaṉatu parattimi yoḻukkattāl, vēyātu ceppiṉaṭitta pūppōlat taliviyiṉ mey vāṭiyum talivaṉukku ovvāp paḻi varumeṉṟu añci, avaḷ, avaṉatu koṭumiyit taṉ āruyirt tōḻikkum maṟitta lākiya kaṟpiṉ poṟpu iṉitu viḷakkappaṭutal kāṇka,
iṉi, miṭāc coṉṟikku ōr aviḻpatamām eṉṉum muṟiyāl kalittokip poṟpēḻi yakattu oḷiviṭum katirmaṇik kuviyuḷ oru cila kāṭṭi ivvuri muṭikkappeṟum.
pālik kaliyuḷḷē, curattiṉ vemmiyik kuṟittaṟku,
“	uṇṇīr vaṟappap pularvāṭu nāvikut taṇṇīr peṟāat taṭumāṟ ṟaruntuyaram kaṇṇīr naṉikkum kaṭumiya kāṭu”
eṉṟum,
“	iṉṉiḻaliṉmiyāl varuntiya maṭappiṇikkut taṉṉiḻalik koṭuttaḷikkuṅ kali”
eṉṟum, kūṟuvaṉa neñciṉi urukkun tiṟattaṉa. ivaṟṟiṉkaṇ avalamēyaṉṟi, aruḷum tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa, piṉṉataṉ kaṇ, kāṭṭakattu vāḻum vilaṅkum ittakiya pēraṉpiṉa tākaliṉ ataṉi yaṟintu taliviyit taliyaḷi ceytaṟkut talivaṉ vārātirāṉ eṉṉum karuttu valiyuṟuttappaṭukiṉṟatu. 
“vīṟucāl ñālattu’ eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷil, iḷavēṉiṟ kālattu yāṟu taṉṉālē vaḷampeṟṟa nāṭṭiṉ poṟpiṉik kaṇviḻittuk kaṇṭāṟ pōl ataṉ maruṅkuḷḷa kayaṅkaḷ viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, paḷikkuk kaṇṇāṭiyiṉuḷḷē pavaḻam patittāṟpōlak kāmpiṉiṉṟu kaḻaṉṟu utirnta murukkiṉ cēyitaḻkaḷ kuḷattu nīriṭik kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum kūṟiya uvamaṅkaḷ maruṭkiyum makiḻcciyum viḷippaṉa.
kuṟiñcik kaliyilē ‘kāṉavaṉ yāṉi mītu vīciya kavaṇkal vēṅkiyiṉ pūvic citaṟi, āciṉi meṉpaḻatti yutirttu, tēṉiṉ iṟālit tuḷippaṭuttu, māviṉ kuliyi uḻakki, vāḻiyiṉ maṭalik kiḻittu, palaviṉ paḻattuṭ ceṉṟu taṅkum’ eṉak kuṟiñciyiṉ karupporuḷ palavaṟṟicciṟappu ciṟappuṟa eṭuttaṭukki,avaṟṟiṉ kaṇ akapporuḷ oḻukka nikaḻccikaḷi uḷḷuṟut tamittirukkum tiṟappāṭu pārāṭṭaṟkuriyatu.
marutak kaliyil,
“	kātalkoḷ vatuvināṭ kaliṅkattuḷ oṭuṅkiya mātarkoḷ māṉōkkiṉ maṭantitaṉ tuṇiyāka ōtuṭi yantaṇaṉ erivalañ ceyvāṉpōl”
aṉṉam taṉ aṇinaṭip peṭiyōṭu tāmarit taṉi malarip puṟañcūḻntu tiritarum eṉṟurippatu iṉimi payappatoṉṟām.
mullik kaliyilē, veḷḷi, kāri, kurāl, cēy, kari, neṟṟik kāri, cemmaṟu veḷḷi, ēntimiṟ kurāl, palpoṟip pukar eṉṟiṅṅaṉam niṟampaṟṟi ēṟukaḷukkup peyar kūṟi, avaṟṟukkup palarāmaṉ, kaṇṇaṉ, mukkaṇṇāṉ, murukaṉ eṉṉum kaṭavuḷarkaḷiyum, avarkaḷ tarittuḷḷa tār, vaḷi, maṟu, uṭi mutaliyavaṟṟiyum poruntavittu uvamaṅ kūṟiyiruppatu pārāṭṭun tiṟattatu.
“	kollēṟṟuk kōṭañcuvāṇi maṟumiyum pullāḷē āya makaḷ”
eṉpataṉ karuttu aṟintu cuvittu iṉpuṟaṟpālatu.
neytaṟkaliyuḷ, talivaṉip pirintu āṟṟāmi mikkuk kaṭal, mukil, ñāyiṟu mutaliyavaṟṟi nōkkit talivi kūṟum kaḻipaṭark kiḷaviyellām iruppu neñciyum nekiḻkkun tiṟattaṉa.  āṟṟutal, pōṟṟutal, paṇpu, aṉpu, aṟivu, ceṟivu, niṟi, muṟi, poṟi eṉṉum paṇpukaḷi oru ceyyuḷil eṭuttu niṟutti, “āṟṟutal eṉpatoṉ ṟalantavark kutavutal” eṉṟiṅṅaṉam avaṟṟiṉ karuttiṉi variyaṟut tuṇartti yiruppatu āciriyaratu māṇ pulamikkuc cāṉṟākum.
“	tampukaḻ kēṭṭārpōl talicāyttu marantuñca” 
eṉṟu āciriyar uvamamākat tērntu kūṟiyatōr naṟpaṇpu naṉmakkaḷ uḷḷattē eṉṟum niṉṟu nilavutaṟ kuriyatu.
maṟṟum ikkalittokiyiṟ kūṟappaṭṭirukkum tali makkaḷiṉ paṇpukaḷum, aṟaneṟikaḷum, ulakiyalkaḷum mutalāyiṉa vellām pēruṟuti payappaṉavākum. mutal karuurip porukaḷiṉ iyalpukaḷit teḷivuṟukku miṭaṅkaḷil vēttiyal mutalāya ēttarum aṟaneṟikaḷ ettaṉiyō pala uvamaṅkaḷil vittu aṟivuṟuttap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. makkaḷiṉ maṉanili, oḻukkam mutaliya paṇpukaḷukku maram, pū mutaliya parupporuḷ kaḷiyum, parupporuḷkaḷukku appaṇpukaḷiyum uvamiyāka amittirukkum aḻaku itaṉiṟpōla ittuṇi mikutiyāka vēṟeṅkum kāṇṭalaritu. itaṉik kaṟpatil tam neñciṉit tiṟi koṭuttōrē tamiḻiṉ cuviyi iṉitu nukarntu iṉpuṟuvōrāvar.
kalittokikku āciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar eḻutiya uri viḻumiyatoṉṟu. avariyaṟṟiya uri palavaṟṟiṉum itu ciṟantatu eṉṉalām. nuṇmāṇ karuttukkaḷi āṇṭāṇṭu āyntu veḷippaṭuttiyum, uḷḷuṟiyuvamaṅkaḷit teḷḷitiṉ viḷakkiyum, ceyyuḷtōṟum meyppāṭum, payaṉum terivittum ivvuri iyaṉṟuḷḷatu. cila toṭarkaḷukku veḷippaṭi yākat teriyum poruḷi viṭuttu, viḻumiya poruḷ pukutturippatu ivvuriyāciriyar iyalpeṉpatu palarum aṟintatoṉṟu. kaliyiṉ kaṭavuḷ vāḻttiluḷḷa,
“ kūṟāmaṟ kuṟittaṉmēṟ cellum kaṭuṅkūḷi  māṟāppōr maṇimiṭaṟ ṟeṇkiyāy kēḷiṉi”
eṉpatil ‘kūṟāmaṟ kuṟittataṉmēṟ cellum’ eṉpatu kūḷikku aṭimoḻiyāy niṉṟu, ‘kūṟavēṇṭāmalē karutiya viṉiyiṟ cellum’ eṉṉum poruḷ payappatu kaṇkūṭu. takkayākap paraṇi uriyāciriyar, “iṟivartam, mēli neṟṟi viḻikkavantu paṇintu niṉṟaṉaṉ vīraṉē” eṉpataṟku uri kūṟumiṭattu, “kūṟāmaṟ ... kēḷiṉi” eṉṉum ippakutiyi eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyiruppatu avar karuttum aṉṉatātalip pulappaṭuttum. āyiṉ, nacciṉārkkiṉiyarō, ataṉi iṟivaṉukku aṭiyākki, “māṟṟam maṉam kaḻiya niṉṟavaṉ” eṉṟa karuttiṉip pulappaṭuttukiṉṟār. 

1940 ām āṇṭu mārccut tiṅkaḷ 9, 10 nāṭkaḷil ceṉṉiyil naṭipeṟṟa kalittoki  mānāṭṭil tamiḻaṟiñar na.mu.vē. avarkaḷ  nikaḻttiya talimi uri.

nāvalar na.mu.vē nāṭṭār  eḻutiya nūlkaḷ / urikaḷ

urikaḷ 
1925	-	iṉṉānāṟpatu, kārnāṟpatu, kaḷavaḻināṟpatu, ātticcūṭi, koṉṟivēntaṉ, veṟṟi vēṟki, mūturi, ulakanīti, nalvaḻi, naṉṉeṟi
			tirikaṭukam - kiyeḻuttup paṭiyākak kiṭittu  mutaṉ mutalāka  veḷivarukiṟatu. nāvalar nāṭṭār tamiḻ urikaḷ tokuti eṇ : 20 il pārkka.

urittiruttam :
1940 	-	taṇṭiyalaṅkārap paḻiya uri
			yāpparuṅkalakkāriki 
			akattiyar tēvārattiraṭṭu 
1930 	-	pipravari 11,12,13,14 ākiya nāṭkaḷil ceṉṉip palkalikkaḻaka aṟakkaṭṭaḷi cārpāka tolkāppiya ārāycci coṟpoḻivu. (inta coṟpoḻivu ituvari veḷivarātavi kiyeḻuttup paṭiyākak  kiṭittatu. mutaṉ mutalāka  veḷivarukiṟatu.   nāvalar nāṭṭār tamiḻurikaḷ tokuti eṇ - 17 il 15-vatu kaṭṭuriyil pārkka .)

piṟappu: 		-	naṭukkāviri,  12. 3. 1884			tiruviyāṟu vaṭṭam 			tañcāvūr māvaṭṭam
	tanti	-	vī.muttucāmi nāṭṭār
	tāy	-	tirumati tilammi
iḷamik kalvi:
	tiṇṇippaḷḷi	-	naṭukkāviri
	toṭakkappaḷḷi	-	3,4 ām vakuppukaḷ 			naṭukkāviri
tāmē payiṉṟa tamiḻp pērācāṉ maturit tamiḻc caṅkam
	1905	-	piravēca paṇṭitam	
	1906	-	pālapaṇṭitam
	1907	-	paṇṭitam	
āṟu āṇṭukaḷ paṭikka vēṇṭiyati mūṉṟē āṇṭukaḷil paṭittu mutal vakuppil mutalniliyil tērcci peṟṟa pārāṭṭukkuriyavar. poṟ patakkam, taṅkattōṭā, aḷikkappaṭṭu ciṟappikkappaṭṭavar.

	1910-1933	-	tamiḻp pērāciriyar paṇi 	(22 āṇṭukaḷ)				es.pi.ji. kallūri , 		tiruccirāppaḷḷi
	1933-1940	-	tamiḻp pērāciriyar, 								aṇṇāmalip 	palkalikkaḻakam,  						citamparam
	1940	-	ceṉṉi mākāṇa tamiḻar 							mānāṭṭil ‘nāvalar’  paṭṭam vaḻaṅkic 						ciṟappikkap paṭṭavar.
	1941-1944	-	matippiyal mutalvar 			karantip pulavar 			kallūri 			tañcāvūr
	28.3.1944	-	ivvulaka vāḻvil iruntu 				maṟintār.

kuṟippu :  nāṭṭār toṭarpāṉa varalāṟṟuc cuvaṭukaḷiṉ virivāṉa ceytikaḷi patippāciriyar uriyilum,  nāvalar nāṭṭār tamiḻ urikaḷ tokuti eṇ. 22 lum  pārkka)
